However, raw language-modeling gradients are optimized for token-level document reconstruction rather than for query-driven knowledge use. We therefore introduce a bi-level meta-learning objective that reshapes document-derived gradients into generalizable adaptation signals for downstream tasks. Experiments across general and domain-specific settings show that \textsc{ReGrad} outperforms CPT and RAG baselines, enabling scalable and reversible parametric knowledge injection without accumulating weight drift.

</details>


### [114] [EHRNote-ChatQA: A Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Multi-Turn Clinical Question Answering over Longitudinal Discharge Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15735)
*Jiyoun Kim,Muhan Yeo,Eunhye Jang,Jeewon Yang,Hangyul Yoon,Su Ji Lee,Hee Jo Han,Hee-Jae Jung,Doyun Kwon,Jun young Lee,Jaehun Lee,Jung-Oh Lee,Sunjun Kweon,Jong Hak Moon,Daseul Kim,Minjae Cho,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EHRNote-ChatQA，首个面向基于多份出院记录的多轮临床问答及证据溯源的基准评测数据集，基于MIMIC-IV构建，包含967个患者级别的多轮样本和16,072个经医学专家验证的问答对，用于评估大语言模型在真实临床信息整合场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床问答基准多关注单轮、考试式问题，缺乏对医生在实际工作中需跨多份出院记录进行多轮、证据支持问答的评估场景建模，因此需要构建更贴近真实临床决策过程的评测基准。

Method: 基于去标识化的MIMIC-IV出院记录，采用专家指导的流程：结合出院记录结构化模式、专家设计的多轮问答模板和大语言模型生成，最终由11位医学专家逐一样本审核修订，构建出EHRNote-ChatQA基准。

Result: EHRNote-ChatQA包含967个患者样本（涵盖1至5份笔记）和16,072个专家验证的问答对（8,036个内容问题及其对应的证据溯源问题），覆盖八个临床类别；对22个开源与闭源大模型的评测显示，模型在证据溯源上表现较差，多轮错误会累积，且单轮问答性能无法可靠迁移到该多轮设置中。

Conclusion: EHRNote-ChatQA是一个严格且贴近实际的临床问答评测基准，揭示了当前大语言模型在多轮、证据驱动的临床信息整合任务中的局限性，为未来研究提供了重要工具和方向。

Abstract: Discharge summaries are crucial clinical documents containing the context of a patient's overall hospital stay, and are routinely reviewed by medical experts for patient readmission, ongoing care, and diagnostic decision-making. When reviewing them, medical experts often must iteratively synthesize information across multiple summaries while verifying the evidence supporting each answer. Although large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored for clinical question answering, existing benchmarks do not sufficiently reflect this setting: they often evaluate exam-style medical knowledge or focus on single-turn question answering with limited evidence-grounding evaluation. We introduce EHRNote-ChatQA, the first benchmark for evidence-grounded multi-turn clinical question answering over patients' multiple discharge summaries. Built from de-identified MIMIC-IV discharge summaries, EHRNote-ChatQA contains 967 patient-level multi-turn samples spanning one to five notes and 16,072 medical-expert-verified QA pairs (8,036 content questions, each paired with an evidence-grounding question) across eight clinical categories. The benchmark is constructed through an expert-informed pipeline combining discharge-summary structuring schema, expert-curated multi-turn QA templates, and LLM-based generation, followed by review and revision of every single QA sample by 11 medical experts. Benchmarking 22 open- and closed-source LLMs reveals several challenges, including that LLMs struggle more with evidence grounding than content answering, multi-turn errors compound across turns, and single-turn clinical QA performance does not reliably transfer to this setting. These findings establish EHRNote-ChatQA as a rigorous and practical benchmark for evaluating clinical QA systems. The dataset will be made publicly available through PhysioNet credentialed access.

</details>


### [115] [A Self Consistency Based Reranking for Narrative Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15741)
*Molham Mohamed,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自一致性重排序的自集成框架，通过生成多个候选答案并基于语义一致性选择最终答案，提升叙事问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有叙事问答模型在推理时依赖单一解码输出，易受生成变异性影响，导致回答不完整或不一致。

Method: 结合预训练语言模型生成多个候选答案，利用多答案推理和基于相似性的重排序，通过语义一致性进行最终答案选择，无需修改模型结构。

Result: 在NarrativeQA数据集上验证，所有模型性能均提升：FLAN-T5-Base从82.32%升至86.66%，Pegasus-Large从72.50%大幅提升至87.07%。

Conclusion: 该自集成框架有效增强了模型鲁棒性和回答质量，尤其对生成能力较弱的模型提升显著。

Abstract: Narrative question answering (NQA) is a challenging task in natural language processing that requires models to understand long textual contexts, capture relationships across events, and generate coherent responses. Despite recent advances in pretrained language models, most existing approaches rely on a single decoding output during inference, making them sensitive to generation variability and often resulting in incomplete or inconsistent answers .To address this limitation, we propose a self-ensemble Self-Consistency-Based reranking framework for narrative question answering. The proposed method generates multiple candidate answers for each story-question pair and selects the final answer based on semantic agreement among the generated responses. This allows the model to explore diverse answer formulations while improving robustness through consensus-based selection without requiring modifications to the underlying architecture .The framework combines pretrained and fine-tuned language generation with multi-answer inference and similarity-based reranking. We evaluate the proposed approach on the NarrativeQA dataset using multiple models, including FLAN-T5 (Base and Small) and Pegasus-Large, under both baseline and fine-tuned settings .Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves performance across all models. In particular, FLAN-T5-Base achieves the best overall performance, improving from 82.32% to 86.66% (+4.34%) when combined with self-ensemble inference. Additionally, the largest improvement is observed with Pegasus-Large, which increases from 72.50% to 87.07% (+14.57%), highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed strategy.

</details>


### [116] [ttda704 at SemEval-2026 Task 6: Structured Chain-of-Thought Prompting for Political Evasion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15770)
*Tai Tran Tan,An Dinh Thien*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种用于识别美国总统访谈中政治逃避策略的系统，比较了参数高效微调与结构化思维链提示两种方法，发现后者在处理类别不平衡和复杂推理任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决英语问答对中政治逃避行为分类的挑战，特别是面对严重类别不平衡和需要多步语用分析的任务。

Method: 采用QLoRA对Qwen3模型进行参数高效微调，并结合分层过采样和加权交叉熵损失；同时使用DeepSeek-V3.2和Grok-4-Fast等具备推理能力的API模型，设计包含层次化标签结构和少样本示例的结构化思维链提示。

Result: 结构化思维链提示显著优于微调方法，在子任务2（9类逃避）上最高达到0.5147 Macro F1，子任务1（3类清晰度）上达到0.7979 Macro F1；消融实验表明扩展推理模式和层次化提示设计对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 推理型大模型配合精心设计的结构化提示在政治逃避检测任务中表现更佳，尤其适合需要复杂语用推理的场景，提示工程比模型微调更具潜力。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for SemEval-2026 Task 6, which addresses the classification of political evasion strategies in English question-answer pairs extracted from U.S. presidential interviews. We systematically compare two distinct paradigms: (1) Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Qwen3 models (4B-32B) using QLoRA, enhanced with tiered upsampling and weighted cross-entropy loss to address severe class imbalance, and (2) structured Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting of reasoning-capable API models, namely DeepSeek-V3.2 and Grok-4-Fast. Our evaluation demonstrates that structured CoT prompting of reasoning-enabled models substantially outperforms our baseline parameter-efficient fine-tuning implementation in absolute Macro F1. Our best system, Grok-4-Fast with extended reasoning and few-shot hierarchical CoT prompting, achieves a Macro F1 of 0.5147 on Subtask 2 (9-class evasion) and 0.7979 on Subtask 1 (3-class clarity), ranking 8th out of 33 teams on Subtask 2 and 13th out of 41 teams on Subtask 1 on the official leaderboard. Furthermore, our ablation studies reveal key insights into effective prompt design for evasion detection: presenting labels within a hierarchical taxonomy helps structure model reasoning, while few-shot exemplars provide task calibration. However, the strongest prompt variants are not statistically distinguishable in Macro F1, and explicitly enabling extended reasoning modes yields substantial performance gains by facilitating the multi-step pragmatic analysis required to detect evasive intent.

</details>


### [117] [DYNA : Dynamic Episodic Memory Networks for Augmenting Large Language Models with Temporal Knowledge Graphs in Continuous Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15778)
*Ali Sarabadani,Mahtab Tajvidiyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DYNA是一种轻量级框架，通过将事件组织为时间知识图谱作为外部可更新记忆，增强冻结的大型语言模型，避免了重新训练，在三个时间回忆任务中减少了约7%的灾难性遗忘，并提高了约5%的时间排序准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在不遗忘或高成本重训练的情况下难以融入新知识，因此需要一种无需重新训练即可持续更新知识的方法。

Method: 提出DYNA框架，使用时间知识图（节点为事件，边为带时间戳的时序关系）作为外部记忆；在查询时通过随机游走和中心性度量检索相关信息，并增强LLM输出。

Result: 在三个时间回忆任务上，DYNA相比微调减少了约7%的灾难性遗忘，相比标准RAG在时间排序上提升了约5%，且图的聚类系数越高，检索效果越好。

Conclusion: DYNA实现了无需重训练的LLM知识增强，验证了时间知识图作为情景记忆的有效性，并表明图结构特性可预测检索性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to incorporate new knowledge without forgetting or costly retraining. We propose DYNA, a lightweight framework that augments a frozen LLM with a temporal knowledge graph where events are nodes and temporal relations are directed, timestamped edges. The graph serves as an external, updatable memory. At query time, DYNA retrieves relevant nodes via random walks and centrality measures, then augments the LLM's response. Evaluated on three temporal recall tasks, DYNA reduces catastrophic forgetting by ~7% compared to fine-tuning and improves temporal ordering by ~5% over standard RAG. Higher graph clustering coefficients correlate with better retrieval, showing that graph structure matters. Contributions: (1) episodic memory as temporal KG, (2) retraining-free LLM augmentation, (3) graph properties as predictors of retrieval performance.

</details>


### [118] [ttda704 at SemEval-2026 Task 4: Modeling Narrative Structures via Pseudonymization and Multi-View Sentence Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15783)
*Tai Tran Tan,An Dinh Thien*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比学习和微调句子变换器的方法，用于解决SemEval 2026 Task 4中的叙事故事相似性与表示学习问题，包含单视图和多视图两种管道，并在合成数据上进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在捕捉抽象主题、行为过程和结果之间的叙事相似性，提升叙事表示学习的效果。

Method: 采用对比学习结合微调的sentence-transformers模型；Track A使用全叙事编码与智能层冻结，Track B采用多视图建模（主题、情节、结果）并引入视图特定投影头和自监督对齐机制。

Result: 开发了两个有效的处理管道，在合成数据上成功训练了叙事相似性模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效建模多层次的叙事相似性，为叙事理解提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: We present our approach to SemEval 2026 Task 4: Narrative Story Similarity and Narrative Representation Learning. Our solution uses contrastive learning with fine-tuned sentence transformers to capture narrative similarity across abstract themes, course of action, and outcomes. We develop two pipelines: (Track A) a single-view method that encodes full narratives with smart layer freezing to reduce overfitting, and (Track B) a multi-view method that models theme, plot, and outcome with view-specific projection heads and self-supervised alignment. Both pipelines build on sentence-transformers models and are trained with contrastive loss on synthetic data. The code is available at the following GitHub repository: https://github.com/dinhthienan33/SemEval2026-Task4-ttda704.

</details>


### [119] [On Defining Erasure Harms for NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15815)
*Yu Lu Liu,Arnav Goel,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung,Alexandra Olteanu,Ziang Xiao,Su Lin Blodgett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化的“消除”（erasure）定义，以解决当前NLP系统中缺乏清晰、一致的概念基础来识别和衡量消除现象的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的消除概念要么过于宽泛，难以明确判断和测量消除是否发生；要么局限于特定场景，难以推广到其他情境。因此需要一个更清晰、通用的框架。

Method: 通过分析现有文献中的概念化方法，构建一个结构化的消除定义框架，明确识别消除所需的必要组成部分，并指导实践者如何具体阐述和操作化这些部分。

Result: 提出了一个结构化的消除定义，能够帮助研究者和实践者更准确地识别和测量不同情境下的消除现象。

Conclusion: 该结构化定义为在NLP系统中识别和衡量代表性的消除危害提供了清晰的概念基础，有助于提升系统的公平性和包容性。

Abstract: The deployment of NLP systems has raised concerns about harms they might produce, including representational harms. Recent literature has begun to conceptualize and measure one such harm, the harm of erasure. Nevertheless, the field lacks a clear and cohesive conceptual foundation for identifying and measuring erasure. Existing conceptualizations of erasure are often broad -- making it difficult to identify what is needed to establish and measure erasure -- or else specific to particular settings -- facilitating measurement for those settings but potentially challenging to adapt to other settings. To address this gap, we develop and propose a structured definition of erasure that clarifies what components are necessary for establishing whether erasure has occurred, which practitioners need to explicitly articulate and operationalize in order to measure erasure.

</details>


### [120] [The Truth Stays in the Family: Enhancing Contextual Grounding via Inherited Truthful Heads in Model Lineages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15821)
*Miso Choi,Seonga Choi,Mincheol Kwon,Woosung Joung,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基础大语言模型（LLM）与其下游多模态变体之间的行为一致性，提出了一种名为TruthProbe的软门控策略，通过放大上下文真实性的注意力头来减少幻觉，提升了多个评测基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基础大语言模型在经过指令微调或多模态适配后，其下游变体是否继承了基础模型的行为特性，尤其是上下文真实性。

Method: 通过量化注意力头级别的上下文真实性得分，并分析注意力权重的保留情况，提出TruthProbe软门控机制，在保持其他头贡献的同时增强真实头的影响。

Result: 在Vicuna、Qwen2.5、LLaMA2和Mistral等系列模型中，发现上下文真实性得分在家族内部高度保留；TruthProbe有效减少了HaluEval、POPE和CHAIR上的幻觉现象。

Conclusion: 基础LLM的上下文真实性行为可传递至其微调后的LLM和MLLM后代，且可通过TruthProbe进一步增强，为抑制模型幻觉提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have produced many specialized multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) that share common foundational LLMs, forming distinct model lineages. It remains unclear whether a fundamental behavioral link exists between the foundational LLMs and downstream variants. We investigate this question by quantifying head-level context-truthfulness scores. Across diverse LLM and MLLM lineages, including Vicuna-, Qwen2.5-, LLaMA2-, and Mistral-based models, we find that Truth Scores are strongly preserved within model families, even after instruction tuning or multimodal adaptation. We further show that this inheritance is consistent with attention-head weight preservation, and that context-truthful heads attend to query-relevant evidence. Building on this finding, we propose TruthProbe, a soft-gating strategy that amplifies context-truthful heads while preserving other head contributions. TruthProbe improves contextual truthfulness on HaluEval and reduces multimodal hallucination on POPE and CHAIR, with base-LLM Truth Scores transferring effectively to their fine-tuned LLM and MLLM descendants. Code is available at https://github.com/miso-choi/TruthProbe.

</details>


### [121] [When Correct Edges Cannot Be Verified: A Provenance Gap in Incomplete KGQA and a Provenance-Favoring Completion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15833)
*Yongqi Kang,Yu Fu,Yong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完整知识图谱问答（IKGQA）中基于文本验证的边补全方法的有效性，发现大多数正确的补全边在文本中并无支持，表明文本可验证性反映的是来源可靠性而非正确性。因此，作者提出TGComplete方法，优先考虑有文本支持的边，在保证高精确率和可信度的同时权衡召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作假设文本可验证性能够反映知识图谱补全边的正确性，但该假设未被系统检验。本文旨在探究这一假设是否成立，并重新思考边补全的目标。

Method: 利用标准随机删除协议提供的黄金缺失三元组，评估补全边的正确性与文本支持之间的关系；提出TGComplete方法，通过推理断点处检索证据、轻量级验证循环和无支持时放弃的策略，优先保留有文本支持的边。

Result: 76-96%的正确补全边在文本中无支持，说明文本可验证性不能代表正确性；TGComplete相比GoG基线显著提升边精确率（15-21% vs 3-14%）和严格可信度（提高3.1-7.4倍），EM无显著下降，但召回率较低。

Conclusion: 文本可验证性衡量的是来源（provenance）而非正确性，多数正确边在文本中不可见；应将边补全问题从‘是否正确’转向‘在来源不确定下是否采纳’；TGComplete为注重可审计性的场景提供了一个高精度、高可信、低召回的合理权衡方案。

Abstract: Incomplete Knowledge Graph Question Answering (IKGQA) requires completing missing edges to continue reasoning. A growing line of work verifies completed edges against retrieved text, treating textual support as a proxy for edge quality. We ask a question that, to our knowledge, has not been systematically tested: does textual verifiability actually track correctness? Exploiting the gold deleted triples provided by the standard random-deletion protocol, we measure both. The finding is counterintuitive: among gold-correct completed edges, 76-96% have no supporting passage even under exhaustive retrieval, robustly across deletion rates (20%/40%), datasets (CWQ/WebQSP), and relation types (structural, commonsense, long-tail). Most Freebase-style facts simply do not occur as head-tail co-mentions in text. Textual faithfulness therefore measures provenance, not correctness -- separated by a paradigm-level gap no in-corpus retrieval closes. This reframes edge completion. Since most completed edges -- correct or not -- are causally redundant for the answer (95-97% of correct answers do not depend on any unsupported edge), the central question shifts from "is the edge correct?" to "admit or abstain under provenance uncertainty?" Within this framing we present TGComplete, a provenance-favoring admission policy that retrieves evidence at a reasoning breakpoint, verifies a candidate through a lightweight loop, and abstains when support is absent. Against the generate-to-complete baseline GoG, it attains higher edge precision against gold (15-21% vs 3-14%), with no statistically detectable EM loss and 3.1-7.4 times higher strict faithfulness of admitted edges -- at the cost of lower recall. We position TGComplete not as uniformly better, but as a principled point on a precision/provenance-recall trade-off, appropriate when auditability matters.

</details>


### [122] [SciOrch: Learning to Orchestrate Expert LLMs for Solving Frontier Multimodal Scientific Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15872)
*Jingru Guo,Xiangyuan Xue,Lian Zhang,Wanghan Xu,Siki Chen,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciOrch是一个通过训练轻量级模型来协调多个前沿大语言模型进行科学推理的框架，利用MCTS和GRPO方法实现高效、低成本的多模型协同，在科学问题解答上超越了单一模型和多智能体基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在不同科学问题上表现各异，单个模型难以全面胜任前沿科学推理任务，需要一种能整合多个模型优势的机制。

Method: 提出SciOrch框架：使用8B参数的轻量模型作为协调器，将问题分解并选择合适的商业模型通过API处理子问题，结合MCTS生成多样化轨迹，提取单步样本，并采用GRPO风格训练优化协调器。

Result: 在包含240个问题的测试集上，SciOrch达到56.66%的平均准确率，超过最强单模型3.74%，优于最强多智能体基线3.33%，且在SGI和SFE两个子集上表现最佳，API成本不足典型多智能体方法的一半。

Conclusion: SciOrch通过有效协调多个大语言模型，在降低调用成本的同时提升了科学推理的整体性能，展示了多模型协作在前沿科学任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Frontier scientific reasoning remains a major challenge for large language models (LLMs), where even the strongest commercial systems fall short of expert-level performance. A closer look at model behavior reveals substantial complementarity that single-model evaluation hides: different frontier models excel on different question types, and no single model captures the full picture. We present SciOrch, a framework that trains a lightweight 8B model to orchestrate frontier LLMs for scientific reasoning. The orchestrator decomposes each question, delegates sub-problems to selected commercial models through API calls, and synthesizes a final answer. Training such an orchestrator is fundamentally harder than conventional agentic RL: each action triggers an API call that is expensive in both dollar cost and latency, making standard online rollouts infeasible. We address this with MCTS-based approach, producing diverse orchestration trajectories, extracting per-node single-turn samples, and optimizing the orchestrator with GRPO-style training. On a 240-question test set spanning SGI-Reasoning and Scientists' First Exam, SciOrch reaches 56.66% average accuracy, outperforming the strongest single commercial model by 3.74% and the strongest multi-agent baseline by 3.33%. It also attains the best accuracy on both SGI and SFE with less than half the API cost of typical multi-agent methods.

</details>


### [123] [Free Energy Heuristics: Fast-And-Frugal Cognition as Active Inference Under Uncertain Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15877)
*Alex Bogdan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出元不确定性（meta-uncertainty）是决定链式思维（CoT）推理是否提升模型性能的关键因素：在元不确定性高的任务中，更多推理会制造虚假置信，导致准确率下降；作者通过理论证明和实验验证了该观点，并统一了贝叶斯推理与快速节俭启发式框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管链式思维（CoT）在数学和符号推理中有效，但在规划、伦理争议等无法自我验证的任务上可能适得其反，缺乏一个原则性标准来判断何时推理有益。本文旨在找出决定CoT效果的关键属性。

Method: 提出元不确定性作为关键变量，通过最小化预期自由能的策略分析，在重尾精度先验和递减主导条件下进行理论建模，并设计FEH-79基准测试，涵盖七种模型、五种CoT长度和7,875个响应的预注册研究。

Result: 实验证明高元不确定性下更长CoT显著降低准确率（平均下降17.3点，95% CI [7.7, 25.5]），而答案明确的对照项无此损耗；效应在中大型模型中显著，在弱模型中缺失或反转。

Conclusion: 元不确定性决定了CoT是否有效；高元不确定性下应停止过度推理，快速节俭启发式与主动推断本质上是同一计算的两种描述，支持‘少即是多’现象作为贝叶斯认知的证据而非反例。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) improves large language models' performance in math and symbolic reasoning. But on planning, contested ethics, and tasks where the model cannot check itself, more reasoning makes things worse. Both effects are documented; what has been missing is a principled account of which property decides the outcome. We argue it is meta-uncertainty: how unsure the model is about the reliability of its own evidence. When that uncertainty is high, extra reasoning stops adding signal and starts manufacturing false confidence. We prove that the policy minimizing expected free energy under uncertain precision stops integrating cues after a finite number of high-validity ones when the precision prior is heavy-tailed (Theorem 2.6.1), and under a Descending Dominance condition, is sample-wise identical to take-the-best (Theorem 2.7.4). Fast-and-frugal heuristics and active inference are, then, two descriptions of the same computation. The prediction is that on high-meta-uncertainty items, longer CoT should degrade accuracy. We score the regime per item (simulate-and-recover rho > 0.96), build FEH-79, a benchmark of Knightian frames with matched controls, and run a pre-registered study across seven models (five open-weight 3B-32B, two frontier), five CoT lengths, and 7,875 responses. The gate, fixed before any data, required a negative interaction with posterior probability above 0.95 and an accuracy drop of more than 6 points. It held. The high-regime drop is 17.3 points (95% CI [7.7, 25.5]); matched items with definite answers show no cost. The effect is regime-dependent: decisive in capable mid-to-large models, directional in the two frontier systems, absent-to-reversed in the weakest. The framework answers when CoT helps and unifies the Bayesian and fast-and-frugal traditions: less-is-more effects are evidence about the meta-uncertainty regime, not against Bayesian cognition.

</details>


### [124] [Koshur Diacritizer: A Byte-Level Sequence-to-Sequence Model for Kashmiri Diacritic Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15883)
*Haq Nawaz Malik,Nahfid Nissar,Faizan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ByT5-small的序列到序列模型Koshur Diacritizer，用于恢复克什米尔语文本中的变音符号，并发布了包含23.7k对句子的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于克什米尔语在数字文本中常省略变音符号，导致自然语言处理任务面临歧义和挑战。

Method: 采用ByT5-small字节级序列到序列模型，结合脚本感知归一化、对齐验证和骨架保持推理方法。

Result: 在保留测试集上取得DERm为0.2012，WER为0.2159；母语语言专家评估显示平均准确率为77.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效恢复了克什米尔语的变音符号，同时保持原始字母序列不变，且数据集、模型和代码均已公开，为低资源语言研究提供了可复现的基线。

Abstract: Kashmiri, an Indo-Aryan language written in a modified Perso-Arabic script, frequently omits diacritic marks in digital text, creating ambiguity and challenging downstream NLP applications. We present Koshur Diacritizer, a ByT5-small byte-level sequence-to-sequence model for restoring diacritics in Kashmiri text. To support this task, we release a publicly available dataset of 23.7k aligned undiacritized diacritized Kashmiri sentence pairs. The proposed framework combines script-aware normalization, alignment validation, and skeleton-preserving inference to ensure reliable restoration while maintaining the original base-letter sequence. Experimental results on a held-out test set achieve a DERm of 0.2012 and a WER of 0.2159. Additionally, evaluation by a native Kashmiri linguistic expert yields a mean accuracy of 77.5%. The dataset, model, and source code are publicly released to provide a reproducible baseline for Kashmiri diacritic restoration and future low-resource language research.

</details>


### [125] [Neuron Level Analysis of Large Language Model in Legal Domain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15884)
*Eri Onami,Youmi Ma,Shuhei Kurita,Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对法律领域推理中的神经元进行了分析，比较了七个开源模型在多个应用领域的表现，发现存在跨任务的重要神经元以及特定于任务的神经元，并指出重要神经元集中在中间MLP层的假设可能依赖于输入格式和内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在法律领域推理中关键神经元的作用及其分布规律，验证是否存在任务通用或任务特定的神经元。

Method: 使用神经元归因分数来识别重要神经元，并通过抑制这些神经元观察模型在目标任务上的准确率变化，同时与随机抑制神经元进行对比。

Result: 抑制关键神经元会导致目标任准确率显著下降，而随机抑制则不会；发现了少量在所有七项任务中均起作用的共同神经元，去除后其余神经元的影响具有任务特异性；法律领域三个基准测试间存在较高神经元重叠，表明可能存在跨司法管辖区的法律相关神经元；重要神经元的分布受输入格式和内容影响，不总集中在中间MLP层。

Conclusion: 法律领域存在部分共享的功能神经元和真正任务特定的神经元，且重要神经元的位置并非固定于中间MLP层，而是受输入内容和格式的影响。

Abstract: We presented a neuron-level analysis of legal-domain reasoning in LLMs, comparing it with other applied domain tasks across seven open-weight models. Using neuron attribution scores to rank and suppress influential neurons, we confirmed that suppressing the identified neurons collapses accuracy on the target task, whereas suppressing the same number of random neurons does not. We further found a small subset of neurons influential across all seven tasks; once these are removed, suppressing the remaining neurons degrades only the task they were identified from, revealing genuinely task-specific neurons in every model studied. Within the legal domain, the three benchmarks exhibit relatively high neuron overlap and tend to be affected jointly, suggesting of legal components neurons that span jurisdictions. The distribution of identified neurons in our experiments suggests that the hypothesis that influential neurons are concentrated in middle MLP layers may depend on the input format and content, rather than being a universal phenomenon.

</details>


### [126] [BALTO: Balanced Token-Level Policy Optimization for Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15893)
*Ning Li,Zixuan Guo,Yan Xu,Wenbo Fei,Yifan Niu,Chang Luo,Yasheng Wang,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BALTO是一种用于缓解大语言模型幻觉的平衡令牌级策略优化框架，通过将声明级判断映射到令牌级标签并重新分配概率质量来提升响应的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的幻觉缓解方法在响应级别上存在粒度不匹配问题，导致局部幻觉影响整体内容评估，且现有细粒度反馈机制可能引入偏差。

Method: 提出BALTO框架，提取可验证的事实声明，基于参考上下文进行验证，并将声明级判断投影为令牌级标签，引入平衡的令牌级信用分配机制，仅抑制不支持的内容而非整个响应。

Result: 理论分析表明BALTO在训练稳定性和优化效率方面具有优势；在ConFiQA、RAGTruth和FinLLM-Eval上的实验显示，BALTO在六个模型-基准设置中均实现了最高的忠实性，并在Q-Score上持续优于现有后训练基线。

Conclusion: BALTO通过细粒度且平衡的信用分配有效缓解了幻觉问题，在保持信息量的同时显著提升了生成内容的忠实性，适用于知识密集型应用场景。

Abstract: Hallucinations remain a major obstacle to deploying large language models (LLMs) in knowledge-intensive settings, where generated responses must be faithfully grounded in provided evidence. Reinforcement learning (RL) is a promising direction for hallucination mitigation, but response-level faithfulness rewards suffer from a granularity mismatch: localized hallucinations can cause supported content to receive spurious penalties. Although recent work introduces fine-grained feedback such as claim-level verification and token-level rewards, unbalanced credit assignment can still induce length, verbosity, or optimization-noise biases. We propose BALTO, a Balanced Token-level Policy Optimization framework for hallucination mitigation. BALTO extracts checkable factual claims, verifies them against the reference context, and projects claim-level judgments to token-level labels. A balanced token-level credit assignment mechanism is introduced into the framework. This design redistributes probability mass from unsupported content toward faithful content, rather than suppressing the entire response. We systematically analyze the limitations of response-level rewards from a theoretical standpoint, and prove BALTO's advantages in training stability and optimization efficiency for hallucination mitigation. Experiments on ConFiQA, RAGTruth, and FinLLM-Eval show that BALTO achieves the highest faithfulness across all six model--benchmark settings and consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in Q-Score, demonstrating a stronger faithfulness--informativeness trade-off.

</details>


### [127] [Control-Plane Placement Shapes Forgetting: An Architectural Study of Agent Memory Across Thirteen System Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15903)
*Dongxu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ForgetEval，一个用于评估代理记忆管道中遗忘失败模式的基准测试，并比较了13种系统配置，发现不同LLM放置策略在处理词汇/时间、规范化和意图感知删除等任务时表现出互补的优势与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注记忆的召回能力，而忽视了遗忘机制中的控制平面问题；作者旨在揭示LLM在记忆更新过程中的不同部署位置对各类遗忘失败模式的影响，并填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建ForgetEval基准，包含1000个模板化案例和385个对抗性案例（含人工设计与LLM生成），采用确定性子串匹配评分；提出六种适配器协议，使异构记忆存储可通过130行代码接入；通过Fleiss' kappa=0.958的多人标注验证有效性，并引入外部贡献者数据集进行复现。

Result: 实验显示：确定性原语可处理词汇/时间类遗忘但无法解决规范化问题（标识符混淆仅5%，跨语言0%）；插入时LLM能完全恢复规范化（100%）但无法处理意图感知删除（前缀冲突和复合事实为0%）；变异时hook表现最佳，整体准确率达91.7-93.2%，意图感知删除达78-85%，单次运行成本0.17美元，每例变异延迟2.3秒。外部验证集复制了规范化不对称现象并显示出联合部署提升+27.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 遗忘失败是实际应用中的主要问题，但当前缺乏相应基准；ForgetEval填补了该空白，揭示了LLM在记忆控制平面中不同部署位置的关键权衡，证明变异时介入最优，且联合部署可显著提升性能。

Abstract: Where an LLM sits in an agent memory pipeline -- between the recall plane that retrieves stored facts (extensively benchmarked) and the control plane that mutates them via supersede, release, purge (largely untested) -- shapes which forgetting failure modes the system recovers. Comparing thirteen system configurations on a 385-case adversarial surface, we observe three placement regimes with partly complementary coverage: deterministic primitives suffice for lexical/temporal categories but fail canonicalization (5% on identifier-obfuscation, 0% on cross-lingual); inscribe-time LLM recovers canonicalization (100%) but cannot help intent-aware deletion (0% on prefix-collision and compound-fact); a mutation-time hook recovers intent-aware deletion (78-85%) and brightens nearly all categories simultaneously (91.7-93.2% overall, $0.17 per 385-case run, 2.3s/case mutation latency vs. 64-191ms/case deterministic, recall path unchanged).
  We expose the trade-off via ForgetEval, a 1000-case templated suite plus a 385-case adversarial layer (132 hand-crafted + 253 LLM-drafted oracle-validated) scored by deterministic substring match, paired with a six-method Adapter Protocol with honest N/A scoring that lets heterogeneous memory stores enter in 130 lines. Admission is corroborated by 10-annotator IAA (Fleiss' kappa = 0.958) and a 77-case external-authored subset (four blind contributors) that replicates the canonicalization asymmetry and amplifies the joint-placement lift (+27.8 pt). Production failures are predominantly forgetting failures rather than recall failures, yet existing benchmarks measure only recall. ForgetEval and all adapters are released under MIT.

</details>


### [128] [Calibrated Triage, Not Autonomy: Confidence Estimation for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15910)
*Reza Khanmohammadi,Kundan Thind,Mohammad M. Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在医疗视觉问答任务中，视觉-语言模型依赖语言先验而非图像内容的问题，评估了七种置信度估计方法在多个模型和数据集上的表现，发现现有置信度信号在部署安全性方面存在局限，强调应将当前角色定位为校准后的分诊而非完全自主决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学领域，视觉-语言模型可能仅凭语言先验生成看似可信但错误的答案，缺乏可靠的置信度评分会导致误用。因此，需要评估何种置信度信号能支持安全的自动化部署。

Method: 评估了七种置信度估计器，在五个开源的视觉-语言大模型、三个涵盖临床影像、放射学和病理学的数据集上进行测试，所有探测器仅在自然图像上训练且未经领域适配，采用有界选择性预测框架（设定置信度阈值以决定是否自动化处理）。

Result: 标准指标对方法区分能力差，温度缩放可改善校准但不提升实际可用性；最佳探测器在高置信度区域的错误率仅为1-4%，而最弱基线高达41-45%；不同领域和模型下无单一最优估计器；基础模型能力决定了性能上限，校准后仅能回收约三分之一的放射学案例，病理学几乎无法自动化。

Conclusion: 当前安全的应用模式应是基于校准置信度的分诊机制：仅自动化高置信度案例，其余交由医生处理，实现人机协同而非完全自主。

Abstract: A vision-language model can answer a question about a medical image fluently and confidently while barely using the image, leaning instead on language priors. In medicine this is the failure that matters most, because the answer looks trustworthy and is not, and the only protection is a confidence score reliable enough to tell the system when to abstain. We ask a deployment question rather than an accuracy one: how much imaging work a model can safely handle alone, and which confidence signal makes that possible. We evaluate seven confidence estimators across five open-weight LVLMs and three medical visual-question-answering datasets spanning broad clinical imaging, radiology, and pathology, with every probe trained only on natural images and applied without adaptation. Recast as bounded selective prediction (automate a case only when confidence clears a threshold, defer the rest), the comparison is cautionary. The standard metrics are poor guides: discrimination barely separates the methods, and the weak calibration of a cheap self-report is cheaply removed by off-domain temperature scaling without changing deployable yield. What distinguishes a usable estimator is the high-confidence region a clinician acts on: the weakest baselines are confidently wrong on 41 to 45 percent of their errors against 1 to 4 percent for the best probe, and no estimator is reliably best across domains or models. Safe handoff is governed at two levels: base-model competence sets a ceiling, so a well-calibrated score recovers roughly a third of radiology cases at a 20 percent error tolerance but almost none of pathology; the confidence layer then decides how much of that ceiling is reachable. The usable role today is calibrated triage, not autonomy: automate the cases a calibrated score marks safe, route the rest to a clinician. We release all outputs, correctness judgments, and confidence scores, with code.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Bayesian Optimization for Learning Nonlinear MPC in Autonomous Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14763)
*Lorenzo Ortolani,Gabriel Voss,Gabriele Beltrami,Francesco Dorati,Tommaso Felice Banfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需地图的自主导航框架，结合滚动规划与非线性MPC，在动态未知环境中实现高效避障，通过贝叶斯优化调参并在四足机器人上验证了仿真到实物的有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态且未知的环境中实现实时自主导航是移动机器人面临的核心挑战，传统方法依赖地图或对参数敏感，难以兼顾实时性与鲁棒性。

Method: 采用LiDAR构建高斯占据表示，结合A*搜索生成无碰撞轨迹，并利用CasADi/IPOPT实现带有Sigmoid障碍屏障的非线性模型预测控制；通过基于TPE的贝叶斯优化进行离线参数调优，并使用高斯过程代理分析参数敏感性。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和Unitree Go2实体机器人上验证了该框架的有效性，导航成功率最高达90.0%，在模拟环境中评估指标平均提升38.9%，且仿真调优参数可直接用于实物，无需额外调整。

Conclusion: 所提方法具有良好的鲁棒性和跨平台适用性，实现了从仿真到真实机器人的高效部署，为动态未知环境中的自主导航提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous navigation in dynamic, unknown environments remains a fundamental challenge for mobile robotics. We propose a map-free framework that tightly integrates reactive rolling-horizon planning with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC). At each control cycle, a LiDAR-based Gaussian occupancy representation is constructed and used to generate collision-free trajectories via A* search, which are then tracked by a CasADi/IPOPT MPC formulation incorporating a smooth sigmoid obstacle barrier. To improve robustness to parameter sensitivity, we adopt an offline Bayesian optimization scheme based on Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE), which identifies near-optimal controller parameters with respect to a composite navigation objective. In addition, a Gaussian Process surrogate is used to analyze parameter sensitivity and provide insight into the optimization landscape.
  The proposed framework is robot-agnostic and is evaluated on the Unitree Go2 quadruped in simulation using Gazebo, followed by deployment on the physical robot. Experimental results show that parameters tuned in simulation transfer effectively to hardware, maintaining comparable performance without additional tuning. The full system achieves up to a 90.0\% navigation success rate when deployed, along with a 38.9\% average improvement in the evaluation metrics across simulated environments.

</details>


### [130] [Synthetic-to-Real Pipeline for Safe Landing Zone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14767)
*Shrikant Banerjee,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高保真程序化合成数据的感知与数据生成流程，用于实现无人机在非协作、非结构化环境中的自主着陆，无需手动标注数据即可实现鲁棒的语义分割与安全着陆区识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏标注的空中数据集，无人机在复杂环境中实现高精度语义分割和自主安全着陆面临挑战，本文旨在通过合成数据解决数据稀缺问题并弥合仿真到现实的差距。

Method: 开发了一个程序化合成数据引擎，生成具有自动语义标注的逼真城市环境，并采用域随机化技术；使用纯合成数据微调基于Transformer的OneFormer模型进行语义分割；结合欧氏距离变换（EDT）和动态推理逻辑设计确定性着陆模块，以识别最大安全着陆区域并保持障碍物缓冲区。

Result: 在UAVid数据集上的定量评估显示了强大的语义分割性能，在真实无人机视频上的定性验证证明了系统能在未见过的环境中准确识别无碰撞着陆点。

Conclusion: 高保真程序化仿真能够有效消除对手动标注数据的依赖，为自主无人机回收提供可靠且可部署于边缘设备的情境感知能力。

Abstract: As Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) transition toward higher levels of autonomy, the ability to perform unassisted recovery in non-cooperative, unstructured environments becomes critical. Achieving safe autonomous landing requires high-fidelity semantic resolution to distinguish navigable terrain from hazardous obstacles, yet development is often hindered by the scarcity of annotated aerial datasets. This work proposes a comprehensive perception and data generation pipeline designed to bridge the sim-to-real gap for autonomous landing tasks. We introduce a procedural synthetic data engine that generates photorealistic urban environments with automated semantic annotations through domain randomization. A Transformer-based OneFormer architecture is fine-tuned exclusively on this synthetic data, leveraging multi-head self-attention mechanisms for global context resolution. To ensure operational safety, a deterministic landing module utilizes a Euclidean Distance Transform (EDT) and dynamic inference logic to identify the largest inscribed safe landing zones while maintaining strict clearance buffers around obstacles. Quantitative benchmarking against the UAVid dataset demonstrates robust semantic segmentation performance, while qualitative validation on real-world UAV footage confirms the system's ability to identify collision-free landing sites in unseen environments. Our results highlight the potential of high-fidelity procedural simulation to eliminate the need for manual annotation while providing robust, edge-deployable situational awareness for autonomous UAV recovery.

</details>


### [131] [Deep Learning-Based Lunar Crater Terrain Relative Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14776)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习陨石坑检测器与扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）的地形相对导航（TRN）算法，用于月球着陆器在特征稀疏环境下的精确定位。通过单目图像匹配全球数据库中的陨石坑，并采用匈牙利算法和一致性方法去除异常值，最终实现从5公里初始偏差中恢复至数百米级定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏明显地形特征的危险月球环境中，传统的定位方法难以满足高精度需求，因此需要开发一种鲁棒性强、适应性高的自主导航系统以支持未来月球着陆任务。

Method: 提出一种融合深度学习陨石坑检测器与扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）的地形相对导航（TRN）算法；利用单目轨道图像提取陨石坑特征，通过匈牙利算法进行特征匹配，并采用基于共识的异常值剔除方法优化匹配结果；将估计结果输入EKF，结合LCLF坐标系与高度辅助信息抑制径向漂移。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使航天器初始位置偏差达5公里，该方法仍可将其导航误差降低至数百米；同时发现，保持图像分辨率与场景尺度与训练集分布一致对维持陨石坑特征匹配至关重要。

Conclusion: 所提出的TRN算法能有效提升月面着陆器在复杂环境下的定位精度与鲁棒性，具备应用于未来自主月球探测任务的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate position estimation is crucial for the successful implementation of future lunar landings using autonomous vehicles, especially in dangerous environments with sparse terrain features. In this paper, we propose a terrain relative navigation (TRN) algorithm combining our deep-learning crater detector, which was designed specifically for the NASA Crater Detection Challenge problem, and an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). Our detector analyzes crater features from the monocular images acquired from orbit, and their matches with craters from a global database are identified via a Hungarian assignment approach followed by the consensus-based outliers removal method. The estimated measurements are then used to refine an EKF, where spacecraft pose estimation in the Lunar-Centered Lunar-Fixed (LCLF) frame of reference, augmented with altitude aiding information, constrains radial drift. The simulation results indicate that even if the spacecraft is off from its actual location up to 5 km, TRN could recover from this situation, achieving navigation error reduction to a few hundred meters. It should be noted that in order to maintain crater feature correspondences, it is important to match the image resolution and the scales within the scene to the detector training set distribution.

</details>


### [132] [Computing Smooth Geodesics under Two-Sided Curvature Bounds with Applications to Robotics and Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14794)
*Da Chen,Zhenjiang Li,Jean-Marie Mirebeau,Xuecheng Tai,Jinglin Zhang,Wei Zhang,Laurent D. Cohen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的曲率受限测地线模型，用于追踪具有上下界曲率约束的最小路径，基于HJB偏微分方程框架，并通过离散化方案实现高效求解，在机器人路径规划和图像中曲线结构提取中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于曲率与平滑性、刚性和弹性等几何特性密切相关，现有方法难以处理具有任意上下界曲率约束的最小路径追踪问题，因此需要更强几何控制的方法。

Method: 提出一种基于Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman（HJB）PDE框架的曲率受限测地线模型，并设计了结合曲率约束的哈密顿量和HJB方程的离散化求解方案。

Result: 数值实验表明该模型能有效生成满足曲率约束的平滑路径，在机器人路径规划和图像中细长结构追踪任务中表现良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的曲率受限测地线模型为寻找满足几何约束的最优路径提供了强大且鲁棒的工具。

Abstract: Curvature of planar curves serves as a key regularization term for computing second-order minimal paths, due to its tight relevance to desirable geometric properties such as smoothness, rigidity, and elasticity. In this paper, we tackle a more challenging problem in computational physics and geometry problem: tracking minimal paths whose curvature is constrained by arbitrary upper and lower bounds. For that purpose, we propose a new curvature-bounded geodesic model, developed under the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) partial differential equation (PDE) framework. It provides strong geometric control over minimal paths by enforcing curvature range constraints, whose paths are smooth and of bounded curvature limitation. We also present a discretization scheme for the Hamiltonian and the HJB PDE incorporating curvature bounds, allowing efficient solver for estimating numerical solutions to the model. Finally, we illustrate the capability of the proposed curvature-bounded geodesic model in applications of robot path planning and curvilinear structures tracking from images. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed curvature-bounded geodesic model serves as a powerful and robust tool for finding satisfactory paths.

</details>


### [133] [TacStyle: Personalizing Tactile Robot Policies using Structured Behavior Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14862)
*Kevin Robledo,Matías I. Torres Galaz,Kumar Dixhant Rai,Shelly Sara Ulman,Tasmia Tasrin,Heramb Nemlekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用语言推理用户偏好的新方法，通过结构化潜在空间实现机器人行为对用户偏好的精细适应，减少对大量标注数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件机器人策略难以实现对连续行为偏好（如力度控制）的精细调节，且依赖大量带偏好标签的数据，标注成本高且控制不够精确。

Method: 首先学习一个结构化的潜在表示空间来组织用户偏好对应的行为轨迹差异；然后利用基础模型解释该潜在空间，并根据语言偏好指令选择合适的潜在变量以生成期望行为。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中验证了该方法能更精确地适应用户偏好，同时比传统语言条件策略所需偏好标签少得多。

Conclusion: 通过语言在结构化潜在空间中进行推理，可有效实现机器人行为对用户偏好的细粒度、低标注成本的自适应控制。

Abstract: Robotic systems that assist humans should be capable of adapting their behaviors to individual user preferences. For instance, users may want a robot arm to adjust the amount of force it applies while folding their laundry or cleaning furniture. Natural language provides an intuitive way for humans to communicate such preferences. Recent progress in language-conditioned robot policies has shown that robots can successfully use language prompts to determine what task to perform. However, extending the same approach to realize how the task should be performed requires detailed labels describing the preferences or styles of trajectories in the task data. Not only is collecting such annotations challenging, but conditioning directly on these labels may also fail to provide fine-grained control over a continuous range of behaviors. For example, it can be difficult to convey the exact force that a robot must apply through abstract instructions like "apply a bit more pressure than before". Therefore, in this work, we propose using language to reason over preferred behaviors instead of directly generating them. We first learn a structured latent representation that organizes user preferences according to differences in the corresponding trajectories. Then, given a preference prompt, we use a foundation model to interpret this latent space and choose a value that produces the desired behavior. Through both simulation and real-world experiments, we show that selecting robot behaviors from an intuitively structured latent space enables more precise adaptation to user preferences while requiring significantly fewer preference labels than language-conditioned policies.

</details>


### [134] [VANDERER: Map-Free Exploration using Future-Aware and Visual-Curiosity-Guided Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14879)
*Venkata Naren Devarakonda,Raktim Gautam Goswami,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VANDERER的探索框架，利用视觉好奇心模块（VCM）引导预训练扩散策略，仅使用单目图像数据实现对未知环境的高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传感器受限的情况下，如仅使用单目相机，传统方法难以生成准确的占据地图，因此需要一种不依赖精确地图的高效探索策略。

Method: VANDERER通过导航世界模型预测动作结果，并利用好奇心成本评估这些预测，从而指导扩散策略生成更有利于探索的动作序列。

Result: 在多个模拟环境中进行评估，VANDERER平均比NoMaD多探索13.4%的区域，并揭示了户外环境中视觉与几何好奇心之间的直接关联。

Conclusion: VANDERER能够有效利用视觉与几何好奇心的关系，在传感器受限的条件下实现更高效的自主探索。

Abstract: Mobile agents require efficient exploration strategies to map unseen environments and autonomously plan tasks. Traditional methods rely on generating occupancy maps and optimizing the sequence in which unexplored regions are visited. However, in sensor-constrained settings, such as those limited to monocular cameras, generating accurate occupancy maps is challenging. To address this, we propose VANDERER, an exploration framework that leverages a Visual Curiosity Module (VCM) to guide pre-trained diffusion policies using only monocular image data. This curiosity module predicts the outcomes of proposed actions via a navigation world model and evaluates them through a curiosity cost. The cost then guides the diffusion process toward generating actions that maximize exploration. Evaluated across diverse simulated environments, VANDERER consistently outperforms established baselines, exploring an average of 13.4% more area than NoMaD. Our results reveal a direct correlation between visual and geometric curiosity in outdoor environments, demonstrating that VANDERER can effectively leverage this relationship for efficient exploration using sensor-constrained agents.

</details>


### [135] [DynaHMRC: Decentralized Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Collaboration for Dynamic Tasks with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14882)
*Wenhao Yu,Yu'ang Xie,Yifan Duan,Jie Peng,Guanting Ye,Ka-Veng Yuen,Yanyong Zhang,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化的多机器人协作框架DynaHMRC，利用角色感知的大语言模型（LLM）代理解决现有集中式调度在可扩展性、动态适应性和领域专业知识方面的不足，并通过四阶段闭环流程和专用数据集微调提升任务成功率与通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在多机器人系统中面临上下文处理瓶颈、动态环境适应能力弱以及领域数据稀缺导致专业任务表现不佳的问题，亟需一种可扩展且适应动态场景的去中心化解决方案。

Method: 提出DynaHMRC框架，每个机器人作为角色感知的LLM代理，采用自描述、带领导竞价的任务分配、领导选举和反思执行的四阶段闭环协作机制；构建涵盖多种任务、动态变化和团队配置的基准测试，并通过构建领域专家数据集对预训练LLM进行微调以增强专业推理能力。

Result: 实验表明，DynaHMRC在不同团队规模下均优于强基线方法，具有更高的任务成功率和更少的动作与通信步骤，展现出良好的可扩展性；同时验证了领域微调对提升专业任务性能的有效性。

Conclusion: DynaHMRC为异构多机器人系统在长周期、动态任务中的协调提供了一个高效、可扩展且适应性强的去中心化框架，结合角色感知LLM与闭环协作机制，显著提升了系统的整体性能与实用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide robots with richer task understanding and adaptability, making them promising for coordinating heterogeneous multi-robot systems in long-horizon tasks. Despite this potential, several challenges remain underexplored: (1) Centralized LLM schedulers scale poorly as team size and environmental complexity increase. A single model must process excessive contextual information, and long-context approximation may degrade reasoning quality; (2) Existing task formulations insufficiently consider dynamic settings, while robust adaptation to evolving task conditions is essential for real-world deployment; (3) Domain-specific data scarcity limits specialized robotic reasoning, making proprietary general-purpose models inefficient for expert tasks. To address these limitations, we propose DynaHMRC, a decentralized framework in which each robot acts as a role-aware LLM agent. This design mitigates the single-model context bottleneck and supports flexible collaboration across heterogeneous team configurations. DynaHMRC organizes collaboration as a four-stage closed-loop process: self-description, task allocation with leadership bidding, leader election, and reflective execution, supported by executable robot interfaces. We further develop a benchmark covering three task families, four dynamic variations, and six team configurations to systematically study dynamic task modeling. In addition, we conduct an empirical analysis to guide the construction of domain-specific expert datasets and fine-tune pretrained LLMs to improve specialized competence. Experiments show that DynaHMRC achieves higher success rates than strong baselines with fewer action and communication steps, while demonstrating promising scalability trends as team size grows within the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [136] [Multimodal Physiological Assessment of Contact-Rich Physical Human-Robot Interaction Under Varying Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14969)
*Yanyi Chen,Xi Wang,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过多模态实证方法，探究了在不同环境条件下（温度、噪音和光照）进行物理人机交互时操作者的生理负荷。尽管任务表现保持稳定，但随着温度升高，自主神经工作负荷显著增加，揭示了操作者通过提高生理努力来补偿热不适的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统以任务为中心的评估忽略了真实环境中接触丰富任务对操作者造成的生理负担，因此需要探索环境压力源如何影响操作者的生理状态。

Method: 在18种不同的温度、噪声和照度组合下进行接触丰富的追踪任务，并同步记录皮肤电活动（EDA）、表面肌电图（sEMG）、眼动数据及主观环境舒适度评分，结合执行数据进行多模态分析。

Result: 任务表现不受环境条件影响而保持稳定；静息皮肤电导水平（SCL）随温度上升而增加，表明自主神经工作负荷增加；物理和认知工作负荷未受影响；主观环境舒适度与追踪误差或完成时间无显著关联。

Conclusion: 操作者能通过增加生理努力来维持在不利环境中的任务表现，这揭示了隐藏的生理成本。该发现支持开发基于实时生理信号的生理感知控制架构，以减轻非结构化环境中操作者的工作负荷。

Abstract: Physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) in real-world settings exposes operators to fluctuating environmental conditions during contact-rich tasks. Traditional task-centric evaluations overlook the physiological burdens imposed by these stressors. Therefore, we conducted a multimodal empirical study involving contact-rich tracing tasks under 18 distinct combinations of temperature, acoustic noise, and illuminance. Synchronously, we recorded electrodermal activity (EDA), surface electromyography (sEMG), eye-tracking data, and subjective environmental comfort ratings. Evaluating these physiological signals alongside execution data revealed hidden physiological costs not captured by objective performance. The results revealed that task performance remained stable across all environmental conditions. Autonomic workload, indexed by tonic skin conductance level (SCL), increased with temperature, while physical and cognitive workload were unaffected. Perceived environmental comfort showed no significant association with tracing error or completion time. These findings reveal a compensatory mechanism where operators maintain consistent performance by increasing their physiological effort to suppress thermal discomfort. Such insight motivates the development of physiology-aware control architectures that leverage real-time physiological metrics to reduce operator workload in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [137] [Inference-time Policy Steering via Vision and Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14981)
*Yilin Wu,Zilin Si,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTaL是一种视觉-触觉联合的推理时 steering 框架，通过双层优化实现长视野行为选择与短视野动作细化，在接触丰富的操作任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的操作任务中，仅依赖视觉信息不足以捕捉关键的局部接触状态（如接触力），因此需要结合触觉信息以实现更精确的动作调整。

Method: ViTaL提出一种双层优化框架：高层利用视觉采样与验证进行长视野模式选择，决定机器人应执行的行为；低层通过触觉引导的扩散编辑在短视野内优化动作序列以满足局部接触需求。该方法基于学习到的视觉-触觉潜在世界模型，并采用语义对齐的视觉与触觉验证器，包括一种新颖的文本条件触觉奖励，直接在潜在空间中评分预测的触觉未来。

Result: 在三个真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，ViTaL相比基础策略整体成功率提高51%，优于单模态 steering 方法至少33%，优于朴素多模态融合方法至少20%。

Conclusion: ViTaL通过融合视觉与触觉信息进行推理时策略调整，有效提升了机器人在复杂接触操作中的性能，验证了多模态感知在长期任务与精细控制中的协同优势。

Abstract: Inference-time steering adapts pre-trained generative robot policies during deployment by verifying candidate actions before execution. While prior methods typically perform this verification only with visual observations, vision alone is often insufficient for contact-rich manipulation, where success depends on both global task progress and subtle local interactions such as contact force. We introduce ViTaL, a visuo-tactile inference-time steering framework that formulates multimodal guidance as a bi-level optimization problem. At the high level, visual sampling-and-verification performs long-horizon mode selection, deciding what behavior the robot should execute. At the low level, tactile-guided diffusion editing refines the selected action sequence over a shorter horizon to satisfy local contact requirements. To support outcome-based steering, ViTaL learns a visuo-tactile latent world model and employs semantically aligned visual and tactile verifiers, including a novel text-conditioned tactile reward that scores predicted tactile futures directly in latent space. Across three real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ViTaL improves overall success by 51% over the base policy, outperforms unimodal steering by at least 33%, and exceeds naive multimodal fusion by at least 20%. Website: https://yilin-wu98.github.io/vital_website.

</details>


### [138] [LV-Calib: LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration with Boundary-Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15010)
*Sheng Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LV-Calib的LiDAR-相机外参标定与LiDAR边界响应联合优化框架，利用可打印平面靶标实现高精度标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在处理LiDAR因光束足迹和混合回波导致的边界模糊问题时精度受限，缺乏对实际传感器响应的建模，影响标定精度与下游任务性能。

Method: 采用带有视觉 fiducials 和反射率边界的平面靶标作为共享观测载体；通过强度与几何约束迭代优化提取精确的LiDAR三维特征点，并提出加权重投影一致性外参优化方法，结合置信度加权的LiDAR特征残差；进一步利用边界过渡带样本估计俯仰-偏航-距离残差统计以校准LiDAR边界响应。

Result: 在实测数据上实现了亚像素级重投影精度、毫米级LiDAR特征一致性，并提升了里程计性能。

Conclusion: LV-Calib实现了高精度的LiDAR-相机外参标定与传感器响应联合校准，显著提升标定精度与感知系统鲁棒性，支持开源复现。

Abstract: We present LV-Calib, a calibration framework for LiDAR-camera extrinsic estimation and LiDAR boundary-response calibration using a printable planar target. The target serves as a shared observation carrier: visual fiducials provide indexed image measurements, while circular reflectivity boundaries provide LiDAR-observable structural feature points. Instead of directly fitting boundary points as ideal geometric contours, LV-Calib automatically crops background points, estimates the target plane, and iteratively refines accurate LiDAR-side 3-D feature points from intensity and geometric constraints. The refinement explicitly handles the broadened and distorted transition band induced by finite beam footprint and mixed-intensity returns around black-white reflectivity discontinuities. Given these refined LiDAR features, we formulate a weighted reprojection-consistent extrinsic optimization with LiDAR feature alignment, where image observations are kept in the reprojection domain and LiDAR feature residuals are weighted by refinement confidence. Finally, using the estimated extrinsic and the extracted transition band, LV-Calib calibrates the LiDAR boundary response by estimating pitch-yaw-range residual statistics of boundary-overlap samples. Experiments on printed-board calibration data demonstrate sub-pixel reprojection accuracy, millimeter-level LiDAR feature consistency, and improved odometry performance. Code and calibration data will be released for reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [Steering Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Policies via Action Token Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15021)
*Jason Chan,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Token Steering（TS）的方法，通过在动作令牌空间中直接干预自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型来动态引导其轨迹生成，无需修改模型结构或重新训练，显著提升了家庭操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不改变预训练VLA模型结构的前提下，实现对机器人行为的实时、轻量级且直观的引导，提升人机交互体验，尤其是在消费级环境和辅助技术中的应用。

Method: TS方法将低维用户输入注入到VLA模型的原生动作令牌表示中，在推理时直接调节输出轨迹，既保留了模型原有的任务先验知识和动作流畅性，又实现了外部控制。

Result: 在两个家庭操作任务（关抽屉和状态感知物体交换）中，TS将任务成功率分别从10.0%提升至72.5%，以及从16.7%提升至93.8%。

Conclusion: Token Steering为机器人基础模型提供了一种无需训练、高效直观的控制接口，有望增强消费场景下的人机交互，并提高行动不便人群对智能机器人的可及性。

Abstract: We present Token Steering (TS), a method for dynamically steering trajectories generated by an autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) model through direct intervention in the action-token space. TS injects low-dimensional user inputs into the model's native action-token representation, allowing users to influence trajectory generation without modifying the underlying vision-language model (VLM) architecture. Because TS operates entirely at inference time, it requires no additional training or finetuning. User inputs guide rather than override the pretrained policy, allowing users to influence robot actions while preserving the dexterity, smoothness, and task priors learned by the VLA. We evaluate TS on two household manipulation tasks -- drawer closing after object placement and state-aware object swapping -- and improve success rates from 10.0% to 72.5% and from 16.7% to 93.8%, respectively. By enabling lightweight, intuitive steering over robot foundation models, our interface has the potential to improve human-robot interaction in consumer environments and broaden accessibility for individuals with limited physical control. Project website: https://jasontchan.github.io/token-steering/ .

</details>


### [140] [An Autonomous Subgram SMA-Based Swimmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15028)
*Conor K. Trygstad,Francisco M. F. R. Gonçalves,Néstor O. Pérez-Arancibia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Swima的900毫克仿生微型游泳机器人，采用形状记忆合金驱动，具备板载电源和计算能力，可实现超过18分钟的自主游泳，最高速度达22.4毫米/秒，并具备良好的轨迹跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发轻量级、完全集成的微型游泳机器人，以拓展微尺度机器人在复杂环境中的应用能力。

Method: 利用高功重比的形状记忆合金（SMA）作为执行器，结合定制的PCB和锂电池，实现板载供电与控制，构建全集成的亚克级游泳机器人。

Result: Swima实现了最高22.4 mm/s的游泳速度、14°/s的转向速率，并可在多组实验中以约6.5°的RMS误差跟踪航向轨迹，连续自主运行超过18分钟。

Conclusion: Swima是首个集成了板载电源、驱动和计算的亚克级微型游泳机器人，为未来微型水下机器人的自主化和小型化提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We present the Swima, a bioinspired 900-mg swimmer propelled by two 10-mg high-work-density (HWD) actuators driven by shape-memory alloy (SMA) wires. We integrated onboard power and computation by using a custom-built printed circuit board (PCB) and an 11-mAh 3.7-V 507-mg single-cell lithium-ion (Li-Ion) battery, which in conjunction enable autonomous swimming in excess of 18 min. The Swima can swim at speeds of up to 22.4 mm/s (0.56 Bl/s), achieves turning rates of up to 14°/s, and can follow 0-degree heading reference trajectories with root mean square (RMS) values of tracking errors of about 6.5° across multiple tests. This robot is the first subgram microswimmer with onboard power, actuation, and computation developed to date.

</details>


### [141] [Exact, Efficient, and Safe Occlusion-Aware Planning Using AH-Polyhedrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15046)
*Long Kiu Chung,David Isele,Toktam Mohammadnejad,Faizan M. Tariq,Sangjae Bae,Shreyas Kousik,Jovin D'sa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为APRO的精确且高效的遮挡感知规划框架，基于博弈论主动感知和AH-多面体可达性分析，用于解决自动代客泊车（AVP）中的遮挡问题。该方法通过线性规划实现无保守性的精确安全验证，并在仿真和真实实验中验证了其100%的安全率和实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态环境中，自主移动机器人面临遮挡处理的安全挑战，尤其是在交通规则宽松、遮挡频繁且复杂的自动代客泊车场景中，现有方法缺乏形式化的安全保证或过于保守，导致车辆无法有效行驶。

Method: 提出APRO框架，将先前工作中的基于集合的安全条件重新表述为AH-多面体的并集，利用线性规划进行精确的安全验证，并将其集成到基于优化的规划器或二分搜索方案中以实现实时应用。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验（包括真实停车场数据回放）中验证了该方法的有效性，结果显示在所有测试场景中均实现了100%的安全率，同时保持实时性能，相比现有有安全保证的方法更安全且决策更优。

Conclusion: APRO提供了一种无额外保守性、无需道路拓扑假设的精确遮挡感知规划方案，显著提升了AVP场景下的安全性与实用性，为解决动态环境中的遮挡问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Safely handling occlusions is a fundamental challenge for autonomous mobile robots operating in dynamic environments. This issue is especially prominent in autonomous valet parking (AVP), where traffic rules are lax, occlusions are frequent and cluttered, and overly conservative behavior can leave vehicles stuck. However, existing methods either lack formal safety guarantees, assume agents follow road structures, or introduce conservatism, leaving occlusion-aware planning for AVP an open challenge. In this paper, we propose APRO (AH-Polyhedron Reachability for Occlusions), an exact and efficient occlusion-aware planning framework based on game-theoretic active perception and AH-polyhedron reachability analysis with AVP as our canonical use case. Our key insight is to reformulate set-based safety conditions in prior work as unions of AH-polyhedrons, enabling exact safety verification through linear programming (LP) without any additional conservatism in set computations or assumptions on road topology. We further show how the resulting safety conditions can be integrated into optimization-based planners or a bisection search scheme for real-time applications. We validate our method in simulation and hardware experiments, including data replay on a real-world parking lot dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that our method consistently achieved a 100% safety rate across all evaluated scenarios while maintaining real-time performance, resulting in safer and more optimal decisions than existing methods with formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [142] [Design and Fabrication of a Spin Coater with In-Situ Optical Measurement for Soft Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15068)
*Daniel Gliksberg,Jiajie Qiu,Jun Suzuki,Kamal Youcef-Toumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本、主要由3D打印部件构成的台式旋涂机，集成了非接触式光学测厚系统，用于软薄膜（如介电弹性体执行器用硅胶膜）的可重复、精确厚度控制制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触式测量会导致软材料变形，而常规光学测量设备成本高、操作复杂，难以实现对50-300微米软聚合物薄膜的可靠厚度验证与控制。

Method: 设计并构建了一台集成四象限光电探测器的旋涂设备，通过原位检测反射激光束的位移来非接触测量薄膜厚度；结合有限元分析验证结构设计，并使用标准金属垫片进行实验校准。

Result: 系统实现了3.6–3.7微米的厚度分辨率，最佳重复性为13微米（95%置信区间）；在实际硅胶膜制备中，膜厚可控制在目标值±9微米范围内。

Conclusion: 该平台证明了将低成本光学测厚技术集成到旋涂系统中的可行性，为无需专用工业设备的软薄膜精确制造提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Spin coating is widely used for fabrication of thin polymer and elastomer films, yet reliable thickness verification of highly compliant materials remains challenging due to deformation from contact-based measurements and the cost and complexity of conventional optical metrology. Accurate thickness control is especially critical in soft elastomer applications such as dielectric elastomer actuators (DEAs), where mechanical and functional performance scales strongly with film thickness. This work presents a low-cost, primarily 3D-printed benchtop spin coater with an integrated, minimally deforming optical thickness measurement system for soft-film fabrication workflows. The system is designed to manufacture films between 50 and 300 microns thick with repeatability within 10 microns. Thickness is measured in-situ by tracking displacement of a reflected laser beam via quadrant photodetector, avoiding significant deformation. Optical geometry, sensor linearity constraints, and structural validation via finite element analysis are discussed. Experimental validation using calibrated metal shims demonstrated a thickness resolution of 3.6-3.7 microns and best-case measurement repeatability of 13 microns (95 percent confidence interval). The platform repeatably produced silicone films within 9 microns of target thickness, demonstrating that accessible optical metrology can be integrated into a low-cost spin coating system for practical, thickness-controlled fabrication of compliant thin films without specialized industrial instrumentation.

</details>


### [143] [DragMesh-2: Physically Plausible Dexterous Hand-Object Interaction with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15133)
*Tianshan Zhang,Yijia Duan,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DragMesh-2的接触驱动框架，用于灵巧手与关节物体的交互，并引入PICA训练机制以增强在不同接触负载下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关节物体操作不同于静态物体操作，其运动需通过持续的手-物接触实现，传统方法难以建模接触动力学且易在接触负载变化时性能下降。

Method: 提出DragMesh-2框架和PICA物理感知训练机制，利用几何信息和物理信号进行策略学习，无需触觉或力反馈即可提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在七种GAPartNet物体上验证了DragMesh-2在不同阻尼条件和接触负载下的优越鲁棒性和任务成功率。

Conclusion: DragMesh-2结合PICA能有效实现灵巧手对关节物体的鲁棒操作，为未来类人手-物交互研究提供了纯几何资源支持。

Abstract: Dexterous interaction with articulated objects is important for household, assistive, and humanoid manipulation, where multi-finger hands can provide compliant contact patterns beyond parallel-jaw grasping. However, articulated-object manipulation differs from static-object manipulation: the target part cannot be directly actuated, and its motion must emerge through sustained physical hand--handle contact. This makes the transition from object-centric articulated generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction non-trivial, since geometric trajectory replay or open-loop execution does not model the contact dynamics required to move the articulated part. Moreover, policies trained only for task completion under fixed dynamics can overfit nominal contact loads, especially without tactile or force feedback, and may degrade when the contact load changes. To address these challenges, we present DragMesh-2, a contact-driven framework for dexterous interaction with articulated objects that extends articulated interaction from object-centric generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction, where articulated motion must arise through physical contact. We further propose PICA, a physically informed contact-aware training mechanism that injects physical signals into policy learning without tactile or force feedback, improving robustness and task success under changing contact loads. Finally, we conduct systematic evaluation across multiple damping conditions and articulated-object categories to study robustness under contact-load variation, and provide a pure-geometry dexterous interaction resource to support future loco-manipulation and humanoid hand--object interaction research. Across seven GAPartNet objects, DragMesh-2 achieves stronger robustness under contact-load variation than the compared methods while maintaining high task success across damping conditions.

</details>


### [144] [MimicIK: Real-Time Generative Inverse Kinematics from Teleoperation with FK Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15148)
*Jiahao Yang,Shenhao Yan,Fan Feng,Chengsi Yao,Ge Wang,Zhixin Mai,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicIK是一种实时生成式逆运动学框架，通过条件流匹配从遥操作演示中学习平滑且鲁棒的关节空间运动先验，结合FK一致性损失和最小迭代策略，在精度、平滑性和实时性方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器在奇异性附近存在不连续分支切换和不稳定行为，而基于学习的方法难以平衡空间精度、运动平滑性和实时效率，尤其是在噪声较大的遥操作数据下。因此需要一种更鲁棒、平滑且高效的实时逆运动学方法。

Method: 提出MimicIK，采用条件流匹配学习关节空间运动先验；使用最小迭代策略（MIP）进行两步迭代优化，预测连续的关节增量命令；引入可微分的前向运动学一致性损失（FK consistency loss），在训练中约束任务空间偏差，提升物理一致性。

Result: 在包含8,848段遥操作数据的真实6自由度机器人数据集上测试，MimicIK达到平均位置误差4.65毫米，10毫米成功率为92.01%，轨迹尖峰率仅7.99%；相比UNet扩散基线，推理延迟从21.66毫秒降至6.74毫秒，同时提升空间精度与运动平滑性；在分布外场景和奇异性附近仍保持稳定，支持20Hz实时控制。

Conclusion: MimicIK在真实遥操作场景中实现了高精度、高平滑性和强鲁棒性的实时逆运动学控制，克服了传统方法在奇异性与噪声数据下的不稳定性，具备良好的实际部署能力。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics (IK) remains a critical bottleneck for real-time robot manipulation. Classical numerical solvers achieve high geometric precision but often suffer from discontinuous branch switching and unstable behavior near kinematic singularities during closed-loop deployment. Meanwhile, learned IK approaches frequently struggle to balance spatial accuracy, motion smoothness, and real-time efficiency, particularly when trained on noisy human teleoperation data. We present \textbf{MimicIK}, a real-time generative inverse kinematics framework that learns smooth and robust joint-space motion priors from teleoperation demonstrations through conditional flow matching. Given the current joint configuration and a target end-effector pose, MimicIK predicts continuous delta-joint commands using an efficient two-step iterative refinement process based on a Minimal Iterative Policy (MIP) backbone. To enforce physical consistency, we further introduce an FK consistency loss, a differentiable forward-kinematics regularization that penalizes task-space deviations from the target pose during training. We evaluate MimicIK on a real-world 6-DOF robot dataset containing 8,848 teleoperation demonstrations. MimicIK achieves a mean position error of 4.65 mm, a 10 mm success rate of 92.01\%, and a trajectory spike rate of only 7.99\%. Compared with a UNet diffusion baseline, our method improves both spatial accuracy and motion smoothness while reducing inference latency from 21.66 ms to 6.74 ms. Furthermore, unlike deterministic MLP baselines that catastrophically diverge under out-of-distribution deployment, MimicIK remains stable near singular configurations and enables robust 20 Hz real-time control on deployment hardware.

</details>


### [145] [Task-Aware Environment Augmentation for Reliable Navigation via Shielded Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15154)
*Bharawee Phoompho,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Yan Miao,Ruben Hernandez,Tim Bretl,Sayan Mitra,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务感知的环境增强方法SCoDA，通过在环境中 strategically 布置少量视觉 fiducial 标记来提升机器人在部分可观测环境下的轨迹执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常固定环境并调整机器人自身（如信念空间规划、主动定位），但在观测贫乏区域容易出现不确定性累积和脆弱行为。本文旨在解决如何在有限预算下对环境进行增强以支持可靠的状态估计这一开放问题。

Method: 提出SCoDA（Shielded Conditional Diffusion for Environment Augmentation），利用条件扩散模型学习高性能标记布局的分布，并通过屏蔽采样器决定在轨迹的哪些位姿需要进行状态校正，从而生成任务相关且符合预算约束的环境增强方案。

Result: 在多个模拟基准和实际硬件实验中，SCoDA相比强基线方法提升了轨迹执行的可靠性和完成速度。

Conclusion: 通过将环境视为可优化的一部分，SCoDA展示了任务感知的环境增强在降低不确定性、提高控制鲁棒性方面的有效性，为资源受限下的可靠导航提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reliable trajectory planning under partial observability depends not only on computing a feasible geometric path, but also on whether the robot receives informative observations while executing that trajectory. Existing approaches usually keep the environment fixed and adapt the robot through belief-space planning, active localization, or added sensing, often incurring costly uncertainty propagation and brittle behavior in observation-poor regions. We flip this perspective and address the largely open problem of \emph{task-aware environment augmentation}: given a mapped environment, a planned task trajectory, and a small budget of visual fiducial markers, where should the environment be augmented so that the planned trajectory can be executed reliably under uncertainty? Our key observation is that useful marker layouts are defined by the localization support they provide along the task trajectory: a small number of well-timed observations can be sufficient to prevent uncertainty from accumulating in regions where state-estimation error would otherwise compromise control. Building on this observation, we present \tbp{SCoDA}, $\textbf{S}$hielded $\textbf{Co}$nditional $\textbf{D}$iffusion for Environment $\textbf{A}$ugmentation. \tbp{SCoDA} learns a conditional distribution over high-performing fiducial layouts from data, using the environment, planned trajectory, disturbance context, and desired execution profile as conditioning. Its shielded sampler reasons over where along the planned execution pose corrections should occur, and steers this distribution toward task-relevant, finite-budget augmentations. Across simulated benchmarks and hardware deployments, we show that \tbp{SCoDA} improves trajectory execution reliability and completion time over strong baselines.
  Code, models and dataset available at: \hyperlink{scoda-diffusion.github.io}{https://scoda-diffusion.github.io/}

</details>


### [146] [Seam-to-Graph Reconstruction for Garment Configuration Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15171)
*Xuzhao Huang,Kai Tang,Fuyuki Tokuda,Norman C. Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图神经网络和注意力机制的Seam-to-Graph网络，用于从不完整的缝线观测中生成编码拓扑结构的骨架图，并结合分层视觉伺服控制器实现衣物精确对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操作场景中，衣物的缝线信息常部分可见，难以充分利用其结构信息进行状态估计和操作控制，因此需要一种鲁棒的方法来利用不完整缝线信息。

Method: 提出Seam-to-Graph网络，将非结构化的缝线观测映射为拓扑编码的结构骨架图，并设计变形感知的分层视觉伺服控制器，用于衣物配置对齐。

Result: 在双臂机器人系统上实现了衣物装载与精确对齐，实验表明该方法达到人类水平的对准精度，误差方差更小，且对不同类型衣物具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 利用缝线信息进行衣物状态估计和操作控制是有效的，所提方法能鲁棒地处理部分可观测缝线并实现高精度衣物操纵。

Abstract: Seams encode rich structural information about garments but are frequently partially observable in robotic manipulation scenarios. To robustly leverage seam information, we propose a Seam-to-Graph network based on graph neural networks and attention mechanisms. This network maps unstructured seam observations to a topology-encoded structural skeleton graph for real-time garment state estimation. Using this skeleton-graph-based state estimation, we design a deformation-aware, hierarchical visual servoing controller for garment configuration alignment. We implement this controller on a bimanual robot system to load a garment onto a screen printing platen and to align it to the desired configuration precisely. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that the robot using the proposed method not only achieves human-level alignment accuracy with reduced variance in alignment error but is also robust to different garments. These results demonstrate that the use of seam information is effective for garment manipulation.

</details>


### [147] [Rethinking Implicit Spatial Representation in Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15232)
*Xiangyu Chen,Yuxuan Hu,Chuhao Zhou,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了空间softmax池化在机器人操作中的作用，发现其生成的紧凑且稳定的隐式空间表示优于特征值表示，但受限于现有视觉编码器中的表示瓶颈。为此，作者提出了PRISM编码器，通过自上而下的交叉注意力融合保留多尺度隐式空间信息，在多个任务中显著提升了性能，尤其在低分辨率高精度任务中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管空间softmax已被用于视觉运动策略，但其有效性与机制尚不明确，且现有编码器因重复下采样导致细粒度空间信息丢失，限制了策略性能，尤其是在低分辨率输入下。

Method: 系统研究不同池化方法对视觉编码器的影响，提出PRISM编码器，采用自上而下的跨层交叉注意力融合机制，保留多尺度隐式空间表示。

Result: 实验表明，PRISM在多个任务和策略主干网络中均带来一致提升；在ToolHang任务中，成功率从5.0%提升至13.4%，参数仅增加15.4%。

Conclusion: 多尺度隐式空间表示是一种有效且高效的机器人操作设计原则，PRISM通过缓解表示瓶颈显著提升了在低分辨率条件下的操作性能。

Abstract: Generative model-based imitation learning has become a widely adopted paradigm for robotic manipulation, where policy performance depends critically on the conditioned visual representations. Although spatial softmax-based representations have been adopted in prior visuomotor policies, their effectiveness and underlying mechanisms remain insufficiently understood. This work rethinks the use of spatial softmax pooling: do such implicit spatial representations provide effective and stable visual features for robotic manipulation? Through systematic studies of different pooling methods in visual encoders, we find that this pooling operation produces compact and stable spatial representations, which outperform feature-value representations, despite using substantially fewer dimensions. Complementary saliency analysis further suggests that these spatial representations guide the encoder to focus more consistently on task-relevant regions. However, this advantage is limited by a representation bottleneck in current visual encoders: repeated downsampling operations weaken fine-grained spatial information before the action-generation module can use it, especially under low-resolution observations. Motivated by these findings, we propose PRISM, a visual encoder that preserves multiscale implicit spatial information through top-down cross-attention fusion. Experiments across multiple tasks and policy backbones show consistent improvements. In particular, on the low-resolution, high-precision ToolHang task, PRISM shows clear gains, improving the average success rate from 5.0% to 13.4% while increasing parameters by only 15.4%. These results support the use of multiscale implicit spatial representations as an effective and efficient design principle for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [148] [Co-Creating Buildable and Open Social Robot Study Companions with University Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15239)
*Farnaz Baksh,Matevž B. Zorec,Feiazie Baksh,Karl Kruusamäe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过应用设计装配（DfA）和设计拆卸（DfD）原则，Robot Study Companion v4.1 降低了开源机器人构建的门槛，提升了 usability 并减少了感知工作量，从而增强学生参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源机器人平台在构建过程中存在障碍，如预组装限制动手学习或使用复杂组件降低参与感，需要探索如何通过机械 redesign 来降低构建难度同时保持结构完整性。

Method: 采用双钻石框架，与圭亚那和爱沙尼亚的大学生合作，识别构建痛点，并围绕扭锁紧固件、卡扣接头和无工具检修 latch 重新设计机器人底盘。

Result: 系统可用性评分从59.4提升至89.4（SUS），感知工作量下降（NASA-TLX 4.29到4.00），平均组装时间从21.4分钟减少到13.7分钟，首次构建者的方向提示和导航连续性成为下一步改进重点。

Conclusion: 感知工作量而非完成时间是影响学生是否参与开源硬件的关键因素，DfA/DfD 设计干预能有效改善构建体验并促进教育应用。

Abstract: Open-source social robots offer accessibility, repairability, and student empowerment, yet the build itself often presents a barrier. Existing platforms either ship pre-assembled, foreclosing hands-on learning, or expose students to unfamiliar fasteners, opaque wiring, and inaccessible service points that erode engagement. Whether targeted mechanical redesign can lower this barrier whilst maintaining structural integrity remains untested. Here we show that Design for Assembly (DfA) and Design for Disassembly (DfD) interventions reshape how a build feels before they shorten how long it takes. Working with university students in Guyana and Estonia, we applied the Double Diamond framework to co-create the Robot Study Companion (RSC) v4.1: mapping pain points, then redesigning its chassis around twist-lock fasteners, snap-fit joints, and tool-free service latches. Across two studies with developers and first-time builders, system usability climbed from Poor to Excellent (SUS 59.4 to 89.4), perceived workload trended downward (NASA-TLX 4.29 to 4.00), and mean assembly time trended downward (21.4 to 13.7 minutes, with juniors' learning effect), whilst orientation cues and navigation continuity for first-time builders emerged as the next documentation frontier. Perceived workload, not completion time, appears to govern whether students take up open hardware.

</details>


### [149] [Driving, Fast or Slow? Neuro-Symbolic Guidance for Motion Prediction in Multi-Modal Ground Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15251)
*Simon Kohaut,Felix Divo,Julius Hahnewald,Benedict Flade,Julian Eggert,Kristian Kersting,Devendra Singh Dhami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Trajectory Compliance-Shaping (TraCS)的神经符号框架，通过结合自然语言交通规则与概率运动预测，提升异构交通场景中运动预测的准确性和可解释性，并在Argoverse 2基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动预测方法多为黑箱模型，缺乏对现实世界交通规则和行为约束的显式建模，难以保证预测结果的可解释性和合规性。

Method: 设计了一种神经符号系统TraCS，利用基于智能体的代码生成流程将自然语言交通规则转化为一阶逻辑形式，并结合反应式数据流推理引擎动态更新合规性分布；同时引入神经置信度评分机制以避免过度修正。

Result: 在Argoverse 2数据集上实验表明，TraCS能持续提升多种先进预测骨干网络的性能，具备良好的通用性与计算效率。

Conclusion: 将符号化的合规性推理与神经网络预测相结合，是一种有效且实用的方法，能够增强自动驾驶在复杂交通环境中的安全性和可解释性。

Abstract: Accurate and interpretable motion prediction for heterogeneous traffic spaces, including pedestrians, bicycles, cars, and trucks, is essential for safe autonomous navigation. Nevertheless, state-of-the-art approaches remain predominantly black-box, lacking explicit encoding of the regulatory and behavioral constraints of real-world mobility. We propose Trajectory Compliance-Shaping (TraCS), a neuro-symbolic framework that augments existing black-box motion prediction backbones with interpretable and probabilistic first-order logic. To do so, TraCS employs an agentic code-generation pipeline to bridge the gap between natural-language descriptions of traffic regulations and probabilistic motion prediction. Furthermore, TraCS employs a reactive data-streaming inference engine that maintains and efficiently updates compliance landscapes as scenes evolve. To prevent TraCS from overconfidently steering the backbone's predictions in the wrong direction, we propose a neural confidence rating learned as a context-aware attenuation of the compliance signal. We demonstrate on the Argoverse 2 benchmark how TraCS consistently improves state-of-the-art prediction backbones, showing that probabilistic and symbolic compliance reasoning is a broadly applicable and computationally efficient complement to purely neural motion predictors.

</details>


### [150] [OSDAG: Online Scheduling for Efficient Multi-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15255)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Thang Tran Viet,Phuc Van Dinh,Xiem HoangVan,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OSDAG的新框架，通过将大语言模型（LLM）的任务推理与有向无环图（DAG）表示和约束感知的在线调度相结合，实现异构多机器人系统的高效协调。该方法仅需一次LLM调用生成任务图，并利用轻量级在线调度器动态分配任务，显著减少了推理时间和任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多机器人系统协调方法存在重复推理导致延迟高和离线预分配调度造成资源闲置的问题，难以满足复杂长周期任务对高效并行执行的需求。

Method: OSDAG框架首先使用LLM将自然语言指令一次性分解为带有依赖关系标注的任务DAG图，然后采用轻量级在线调度器根据DAG中的优先级和资源约束，实时将就绪任务分配给空闲机器人。
