Result: 在五个基准场景中的实验表明，与对话式方法相比，OSDAG的推理时间快5-15倍，相较于顺序基线最多减少38%的完成时间，并保持较高的任务成功率。仿真和真实双臂操作实验验证了其有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: OSDAG通过分离任务推理与执行调度，结合DAG表示和在线调度机制，在保证正确性的同时充分释放了多机器人系统的并行潜力，实现了高效、实用的多机器人协同。

Abstract: Coordinating heterogeneous multi-robot systems (MRS) for complex, long-horizon tasks requires both flexible high-level reasoning and efficient low-level scheduling. Existing LLM-based approaches address the reasoning side but introduce two critical bottlenecks: (1) repeated LLM inference during execution, which inflates latency with agent count, and (2) offline, pre-committed scheduling, which forces robots to idle while waiting for sequentially ordered predecessors even when independent work is available. This paper presents OSDAG, a novel framework that integrates LLM-based task reasoning with Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) representation and constraint-aware online scheduling. The LLM is invoked once to decompose a natural-language instruction into a dependency-annotated task graph, and a lightweight online scheduler then allocates ready tasks to idle agents in real time. The DAG representation encodes both precedence and resource constraints, ensuring correctness while exposing all available parallelism. Experiments across five benchmark scenarios demonstrate that OSDAG achieves 5-15x faster reasoning time compared to dialogue-based methods, reduces makespan by up to 38% over sequential baselines, and maintains competitive success rates. Both simulation and real-world experiments on dual-arm manipulation tasks validate the effectiveness and practicality of the proposed approach for efficient multi-robot coordination. The website and resources are available at http://thanhnguyencanh.github.io/LLM_DAG4MultiRobot

</details>


### [151] [Acting While Understanding: Asynchronous Semantic-Action Decoupling for Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15285)
*Shenhao Yan,Ge Wang,Qi Liu,Weilin Meng,Jiahao Yang,Chengsi Yao,Fan Feng,Xiaoguang Ma,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种异步语义-动作解耦框架，通过分离语义理解与动作生成，在不改变现有视觉-语言模型结构的前提下实现高频闭环控制，显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）在机器人操作中虽具备良好的任务理解和泛化能力，但全模型推理的高计算成本限制了其在低延迟、高频率闭环控制中的应用。

Method: 将VLA内部的语义与动作通路解耦，设计低频语义理解模块和高频动作生成模块；通过历史动作条件建模和时间错位训练缓解语义滞后带来的影响。

Result: 在LIBERO数据集上基于π₀.₅和UniVLA的实验及真实机器人部署表明，该方法服务器端动作模块推理吞吐量可达35.6 Hz，无需以控制频率运行完整VLA即可实现高效闭环控制。

Conclusion: 所提框架为现有VLAs提供了一条低侵入性的高频率闭环控制路径，在保持良好性能的同时大幅降低计算负担。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have demonstrated strong task understanding and generalization in robotic manipulation, yet the high computational cost of full-model inference limits their deployment in low-latency, high-frequency closed-loop control. We propose an asynchronous semantic-action decoupling framework that separates semantic understanding from action generation along the internal semantic-action interface of existing VLAs, without redesigning the vision-language backbone or introducing an external planner. A low-frequency understanding module asynchronously updates reusable semantic conditions, while a high-frequency action module continuously outputs control actions without repeatedly invoking the full model. To mitigate the temporal mismatch between stale semantics and the current execution state, we further introduce historical action conditioning and time-misalignment training, which provide short-horizon execution context and improve feedback control robustness under stale semantic conditions. Experiments on LIBERO with $π_{0.5}$ and UniVLA, together with real-robot deployment using UniVLA, show that the proposed framework achieves up to 35.6 Hz server-side action-module inference throughput and offers a low-intrusion path to high-frequency closed-loop control without running full VLA inference at control rate.

</details>


### [152] [Covariance-Regulated Recursive Koopman Learning for Nonlinear Systems with Uncertain Time-Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15317)
*Weibin Gu,Chen Yang,Lu Shi,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协方差调节递归Koopman学习（CR-RKL）框架，用于在时间变化动力学下实现机器人系统的稳定在线建模与控制，通过误差死区门控和常数迹归一化策略解决了传统方法中的协方差膨胀与参数冻结问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线模型在训练分布之外的时间变化动力学下表现不佳，而Koopman算子理论在递归估计中存在数值不稳定问题，如协方差膨胀和增益消失，亟需一种鲁棒的在线学习框架。

Method: 提出CR-RKL框架，结合误差死区门控和常数迹归一化两种策略，以抑制协方差膨胀并保持不确定性几何结构，实现稳定的递归Koopman学习。

Result: 在具有轮滑和Stribeck摩擦的非完整机器人及微型扑翼飞行器上验证了CR-RKL的有效性，实现了准确且数值稳定的在线建模，并在模型预测控制中保持了良好的轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: CR-RKL有效解决了Koopman学习中的数值不稳定性问题，适用于复杂、时变环境下的自主机器人实时建模与控制。

Abstract: Offline models for autonomous robots often fail under time-varying dynamics outside their training distribution. Koopman operator theory offers a linear representation of nonlinear dynamics via lifting, but its transition to real-time recursive estimation may suffer numerical vulnerabilities: covariance windup under low excitation when using exponential forgetting, and vanishing gain without forgetting. This paper introduces a Covariance-Regulated Recursive Koopman Learning (CR-RKL) framework with two complementary strategies--error dead-zone gating and constant-trace normalization--each independently capable of preventing covariance explosion and parameter freezing, with the latter additionally preserving the geometric structure of uncertainty. Validated on a non-holonomic differential-drive robot with wheel slip and Stribeck friction and on a 26-gram butterfly-inspired flapping-wing micro aerial vehicle, CR-RKL achieves numerically stable and accurate online modeling, and when embedded in model predictive control, it maintains reliable tracking performance under uncertain, time-varying dynamics.

</details>


### [153] [SimWeaver: Zero-Shot RGB Sim-to-Real for Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15338)
*Wenkang Hu,Haoran Wang,Yitong Li,Liu Liu,Mengao Zhao,Lai Jiang,Xincheng Tang,Junhang Wei,Zhengjie Shu,Zhendong Wang,Zhizhong Su,Huamin Wang,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimWeaver实现无需真实数据微调的零样本RGB视觉语言动作策略，通过高保真模拟器与资产框架，在5种可变形物体操作任务中实现超过80%的真实世界成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决RGB仿真到现实迁移在可变形物体操作中依赖真实世界微调的问题，实现高效的零样本迁移。

Method: 提出SimWeaver框架，包含基于测量的模拟器(SimWeaver-Sim)、单图像生成的资产库(SimWeaver-Asset)、拓扑感知轨迹合成器(SimWeaver-Syn)和ISP感知的光度增强sim-to-real协议(SimWeaver-Real)。

Result: 在5个多样化可变形任务上，每个任务仅用200个模拟演示训练，达到平均91%的真实世界成功率；在丝绸抓取任务中，面对视觉分布偏移仍保持100%成功率，远超依赖真实数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: SimWeaver实现了高效、低成本的可变形物体操作策略训练，显著降低了对真实世界数据的依赖，并在多种复杂任务中展现出强鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: RGB sim-to-real for deformable manipulation has remained largely unsolved without real-world fine-tuning. We present SimWeaver, which trains zero-shot RGB VLA policies on 200 simulated demonstrations per task, reaching above 80% per-task and 91% average real-world success across 5 diverse deformable tasks including plastic-bag manipulation, without teleoperation or per-task calibration. SimWeaver combines a reliable measurement-backed simulator (SimWeaver-Sim) with an extensible asset framework supporting single-image generation(SimWeaver-Asset), a deterministic topology-aware trajectory synthesizer (SimWeaver-Syn), and a sim-to-real protocol with ISP-aware photometric augmentation (SimWeaver-Real). On silk grasping, the sim-trained policy reaches 100% under visual distribution shifts where real-data baselines drop to 9-70%, at two orders of magnitude lower per-trajectory cost. We will release SimWeaver and a representative asset subset. Project page: https://simweaver.github.io/

</details>


### [154] [A Hybrid Model-Based and Model-Free Framework for Active Multi-View Viewpoint Optimization in Sonar Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15373)
*Yongkyoon Park,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型与无模型相结合的主动多视角目标识别框架，利用CNN和Radon变换实现无需角度标注的感知策略，并通过PPO学习信息增益驱动的视点选择，在减少传感步骤和运动成本的同时保持较高的识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在无角度标注条件下实现高效、实时的前视声呐多视角目标识别，克服传统模型方法计算开销大的问题。

Method: 结合CNN提供数据驱动的观测似然，Radon变换进行朝向估计，使用基于信息增益的奖励函数通过PPO算法离线训练视点选择策略，部署时仅依赖CNN信念更新进行实时决策。

Result: 在海洋垃圾前视声呐数据集上验证，相比纯模型方法，在识别准确率相当的情况下减少了感知步数和运动成本。

Conclusion: 所提混合框架有效平衡了识别性能与计算效率，适用于资源受限的水下主动感知任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid model-based and model-free framework for active multi-view target recognition using forward-looking sonar. A convolutional neural network (CNN) provides data-driven observation likelihoods, while Radon-based orientation estimation enables viewpoint-aware sensing without requiring angle annotations. During training, an information-gain-based reward guides a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent to learn a belief-aware viewpoint selection policy offline. At deployment, the learned policy performs real-time viewpoint selection using only CNN-based belief updates, eliminating the need for computationally expensive online POMDP tree search. Experiments on a marine-debris forward-looking sonar dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive recognition accuracy while reducing sensing steps and motion cost compared to model-based baselines.

</details>


### [155] [A Corridor-Scale CARLA-VISSIM Co-Simulation Framework for Multi-Intersection Urban Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15431)
*Sima Ashayer,Austin Haris,Mina Sartipi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CARLA-VISSIM协同仿真框架，用于模拟田纳西州查塔努加市中心约十五个互联交叉口的城区走廊，实现了高保真3D渲染与微观交通仿真的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在城市走廊级别实现高精度、感知就绪的交通仿真，解决多交叉口协同仿真中的一致性与同步问题。

Method: 通过双向、步进同步接口集成CARLA 0.10.0（基于Unreal Engine 5）与PTV VISSIM 2026，利用LiDAR地形数据和RoadRunner构建的高精地图确保几何一致性，并采用显式参与者所有权、生命周期镜像管理与最新状态更新策略。

Result: 案例研究表明，该框架在高峰负荷下（约100辆车和100名行人）能稳定实现交通信号同步、车辆-行人交互同步及混合控制操作，验证了跨模拟器一致性。

Conclusion: 该MLK街为中心的走廊可作为有效的测试平台，所提出的架构支持可靠的城市级协同仿真，适用于复杂交通场景的研究与验证。

Abstract: This paper presents an implemented CARLA-VISSIM co-simulation framework for an urban corridor comprising approximately fifteen connected intersections centered on Martin Luther King Jr. Boulevard in Chattanooga, Tennessee. The system integrates CARLA 0.10.0 Unreal Engine 5 with PTV VISSIM 2026 through a bidirectional, step-synchronized interface that couples VISSIM's microscopic vehicle, pedestrian, and signal-controller logic with CARLA's high-fidelity 3D rendering. A LiDAR-derived elevation model and RoadRunner-based High Definition (HD) map provide terrain-accurate road geometry deployed consistently across both simulators. The framework incorporates explicit actor ownership, mirrored lifecycle management, coordinate reconciliation, and a latest-state-per-actor update policy, enabling stable interaction between VISSIM-controlled traffic and a CARLA-controlled ego vehicle. A corridor-scale case study demonstrates consistent traffic-signal mirroring, synchronized vehicle-pedestrian interactions, and stable mixed-authority operation under peak loads of approximately 100 vehicles and 100 pedestrians. The deployment captures the interaction of the five signalized intersections along MLK Street and their connecting upstream and downstream intersections, revealing synchronization challenges unique to multi-intersection corridors. Results indicate that this MLK-centered corridor provides an effective testbed for verifying cross-simulator consistency and that the proposed architecture supports reliable, perception-ready co-simulation for corridor-level traffic studies.

</details>


### [156] [A Bilateral Teleoperation Framework for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15434)
*Stefano Dalla Gasperina,Dong Ho Kang,Haiyun Zhang,Aldo Galvan,Job D. Ramirez,Aaron Kim,Mark Helwig,Kazuto Yokoyama,Takahisa Ueno,Tetsuya Narita,Ann Majewicz-Fey,Ashish D. Deshpande,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化的双边遥操作框架，实现了灵巧的手臂-手协调控制，支持低延迟反馈和在接触丰富的环境中的稳定操作，并通过实际任务验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现精确的臂-手协调、低延迟反馈以及在真实接触丰富环境中稳健交互的灵巧遥操作需求。

Method: 设计了一个模块化双边遥操作框架，整合操作端输入接口与机器人端灵巧手及柔性机械臂，采用位置基手部重定向、差分臂控制、多尺度触觉反馈和共享控制策略。

Result: 通过真实世界的灵巧操作任务验证了框架的有效性，展示了协调的臂-手控制和感知接触的能力。

Conclusion: 该平台提供了一个实用的遥操作系统，并为未来基于演示的学习研究提供了高质量示范数据的基础。

Abstract: Dexterous teleoperation requires precise arm-hand coordination, low-latency feedback, and robust interaction in real-world contact-rich environments. This paper presents a modular bilateral teleoperation framework that integrates operator-side input interfaces with a robot-side dexterous hand and compliant robotic arm in a unified control architecture. The system supports position-based hand retargeting, differential arm control, multi-scale haptic feedback, and shared control for stable manipulation. We validate the framework through a real-world dexterous manipulation task, highlighting coordinated arm-hand control and contact-aware interaction. Beyond feasibility, we identify key design insights related to cross-embodiment mismatch, haptic feedback granularity, and shared control. The proposed platform provides a practical teleoperation system and a foundation for collecting high-quality demonstrations for future learning-from-demonstration research.

</details>


### [157] [Learning Context-Aware Neural ODE Dynamics for Adaptive Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15469)
*Shao-Yi Yu,Jen-Wei Wang,Maya Horii,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经常微分方程的上下文感知动力学模型，通过两阶段训练从状态-动作历史中推断环境因素，提升机器人在动态变化环境中的自适应控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对不确定且动态变化环境中接触条件、空气动力效应和外部干扰带来的控制挑战，需要能够适应这些变化的动力学模型，特别是在无法直接获取完整环境信息的情况下。

Method: 采用基于神经常微分方程的上下文感知动力学模型，利用两阶段训练过程从状态-动作历史中推断环境因素，并与模型预测控制相结合以实现自适应控制。

Result: 在模拟的四旋翼、真实世界的Sphero BOLT机器人和Fanuc机械臂等多种机器人平台上验证了该方法的有效性，结果表明其能有效适应不同任务中的时空变化环境。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在缺乏完整环境信息的情况下，有效提升模型在动态复杂环境中的适应性和控制性能，具有良好的跨平台泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic systems deployed in uncertain and dynamically changing environments often face variations in contact conditions, aerodynamic effects, and external disturbances that challenge reliable control. To remain effective under model-based control, these systems require dynamics models that can adapt to such changes, especially when direct access to complete environmental information is limited. To enable adaptability and facilitate integration with model predictive control, we propose a context-aware dynamics model based on neural ordinary differential equations, which infers environmental factors from state-action histories using a two-phase training procedure. We validate the approach across diverse robotic platforms, including a quadrotor in simulation, as well as a Sphero BOLT robot and a Fanuc manipulator in real-world experiments. The results demonstrate that our method effectively adapts to temporally and spatially varying environmental changes across different tasks. Videos are available at https://youtu.be/PY0sNyF2rqE , and the source code is available at https://github.com/syyu410-yu/context-aware-neural-ode-control.git .

</details>


### [158] [FARM: Find Anything using Relational Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15476)
*Siming He,Leo Huang,Adam Lilja,Fabio Hubel,Jonas Frey,Marco Pavone,S. Shankar Sastry,Jitendra Malik,Claire Tomlin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FARM（基于关系空间记忆的任意物体查找）提出了一种实时、紧凑、开放词汇的对象级记忆系统，结合几何、视觉-语言描述和视角证据，通过显式建模空间关系实现高精度语言驱动的物体实例检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体记忆系统难以满足用户通过复杂语义和空间关系（如“飞镖盘下方、海报左侧的高灯”）定位特定物体的需求，缺乏支持多模态谓词检索的关系型空间记忆机制。

Method: FARM 实时构建包含几何结构、视觉-语言特征和视角证据的物体级记忆；在查询时利用视觉语言模型（VLM）解析自然语言并评分视觉证据，同时通过对象符号和关系谓词显式地建模空间约束，最后通过 VLM 重排序提升准确性。

Result: 在44k语言查询、67个室内外场景的实验中，FARM 相比先前方法将 Recall@5 和 Recall@10 分别提升了164%和224%，Accuracy@1 提升35%，并实现了搭载传感器与计算单元的四足机器人上的闭环部署。

Conclusion: FARM 通过结构化利用 VLM 和显式关系空间建模，在大规模真实环境中实现了高效、准确的语言引导物体检索，支持实时运行和实际机器人应用。

Abstract: Robots operating in homes, warehouses, and other object-rich environments need memory systems that can find specific object instances on demand. Object-level memory alone is often insufficient: scenes contain many plausibly matching objects, and users refer to the target through relations to landmarks and surrounding objects (e.g. ``the tall lamp below the dartboard and to the left of the poster''), demanding a relational spatial memory that supports retrieval through semantic, appearance, and spatial predicates over objects. To achieve this, we present FARM (Find Anything using Relational Spatial Memory), which builds, in real time at 5-10 Hz, a compact, open-vocabulary, object-level memory with geometry, visual-language descriptors, and viewpoint evidence. At query time, FARM uses VLMs to parse the query and score visual evidence, while grounding spatial constraints explicitly through object symbols and relational predicates. This structured use of VLMs enables more accurate and robust retrieval than end-to-end reasoning over frame histories or scene-graph context. In experiments on 44k language queries spanning 67 indoor and outdoor scenes, ranging from 15 to 15,000 m^2, FARM improves Recall@5 and Recall@10 over prior methods by 164% and 224%, and a final VLM reranking stage improves Accuracy@1 by 35%, while running in real time. We further demonstrate closed-loop deployment on a quadrupedal robot using onboard sensors and compute.

</details>


### [159] [FD-SLAM: Fast Dense Radar-Inertial SLAM with Frequency-Domain Loop Closure and Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15491)
*Nader J. Abu-Alrub,Nathir A. Rawashdeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FD-SLAM是一种快速密集的雷达-惯性SLAM系统，通过频域回环检测和位姿图优化提升了在视觉退化环境下的自主地面车辆定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于扫描雷达噪声大、扫描速率低且测量匹配困难，现有雷达SLAM在长轨迹下可靠性不足，因此需要一种更鲁棒、高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出FD-SLAM，扩展了密集雷达-惯性里程计，采用紧凑的频域极坐标描述符进行回环候选检索，并结合多阶段验证流程（包括时间滤波、相位相关筛选、扫描对齐相似性和几何一致性检查），将验证后的回环作为非连续约束加入SE(2)位姿图中，与雷达-惯性里程计因子共同优化。

Result: 在公开数据集上使用KITTI标准指标评估，FD-SLAM优于FD-RIO基线方法，达到当前最先进雷达SLAM方法的竞争力，并在多个驾驶轨迹中表现出更优的旋转精度；运行时分析显示前端在纯CPU上高于雷达采样率运行，回环检测与图优化可并行后台执行。

Conclusion: FD-SLAM通过频域表示和多阶段验证有效提升了雷达-惯性SLAM的精度与效率，适用于视觉退化环境中的实际应用。

Abstract: Radar SLAM is attractive for autonomous ground vehicles operating in visually degraded environments, however, scanning radars are noisy, have low scanning rates, and their measurements are challenging to match reliably over long trajectories. This paper presents FD-SLAM, a fast dense radar-inertial SLAM system that extends dense radar-inertial odometry with frequency-domain loop closure and pose graph optimization. The proposed method preserves an image-like structure of scanning radar measurements by using a compact frequency-domain polar descriptor for loop-candidate retrieval and a multi-stage verification pipeline based on temporal filtering, phase-correlation screening, scan-alignment similarity, and geometric consistency checks. Verified loop closures are added as non-sequential constraints in an SE(2) pose graph together with radar-inertial odometry factors. FD-SLAM is evaluated on a publicly available dataset using standard KITTI evaluation metrics. The results show that FD-SLAM improves FD-RIO baseline, achieves competitive performance against current state-of-the-art radar SLAM methods, and provides favorable rotational accuracy across multiple evaluated driving trajectories. Runtime analysis further indicates that the radar-inertial front-end operates above the radar sampling rate on a CPU-only setup, while loop closure detection and graph optimization remain suitable for parallel background execution.

</details>


### [160] [Understanding and Modeling Perceived Cognitive and Physical Strain Dynamics for Planning-Oriented Human-Robot Collaboration in Prefabricated Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15494)
*Yifan Wang,Bo Xiao,Shane T. Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究基于实证数据构建了适用于预制建筑人机协作的感知负荷累积与恢复模型，发现认知负荷呈线性累积，休息恢复则为非线性衰减，可为未来任务分配与调度提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划模型对疲劳、工作负荷或恢复常采用简化假设，缺乏针对预制建筑人机协作中工人状态变化的实证依据，难以准确反映实际感知负荷动态演变过程。

Method: 通过受控的重复工作-休息实验，使用心理努力评分量表和Borg主观用力评分评估认知与身体负荷，比较线性和指数函数形式，并采用混合效应模型分析协作条件、会话效应及个体差异。

Result: 认知负荷累积适合用线性混合效应模型描述，而休息阶段的恢复呈现非线性衰减特征；所建立的模型具有良好的规划适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了基于实证的负荷建模方法，能够更精确地刻画人机协作中工人的状态变化，有助于实现以人为本的任务规划与调度优化。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) in prefabricated construction requires planning approaches that consider not only productivity but also time-dependent worker states during repeated work and rest. Existing planning models often rely on simplified assumptions about fatigue, workload, or recovery, with limited domain-specific empirical evidence on how perceived strain evolves. This study develops an empirically grounded, planning-oriented approach to characterize perceived strain accumulation and recovery in prefabricated construction HRC. A controlled repeated work-rest experiment assessed perceived cognitive and physical strain using the Rating Scale for Mental Effort and Borg's Rating of Perceived Exertion. Linear and exponential functional forms were evaluated, followed by mixed-effects modeling to examine collaborative conditions, session effects, and inter-individual variability. Results indicate that cognitive strain accumulation is best represented by a linear mixed-effects model, whereas rest-phase recovery follows nonlinear decay. The resulting planning-oriented models may inform future human-state-aware task allocation and scheduling research.

</details>


### [161] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided Retrieval with Soft Fusion for Robust Multimodal Imitation Learning under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15514)
*Hassan Ismkhan,Hamid Bouchahcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RL4IL的强化学习引导的模仿学习方法，能够在传感器模态缺失的情况下通过检索最相关的专家示范并进行软注意力融合来选择动作，并利用跨模态注意力机制实现缺失嵌入的重构，无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际机器人应用中，传感器可能失效或模态缺失，现有模仿学习方法难以鲁棒应对这一问题，因此需要一种无需重训练即可处理多模态输入缺失的框架。

Method: 提出RL4IL方法，使用强化学习策略（基于PPO训练）对候选专家示范进行排序，通过软交叉注意力融合生成动作；当某一模态缺失时，由专用的每模态RL检索策略找出捐赠示范，并用软插补头通过交叉注意力重建缺失嵌入。

Result: 在三个LIBERO基准套件上的实验表明，RL4IL在传感器丢弃条件下显著优于现有的最先进模仿学习方法，且无需策略网络再训练。

Conclusion: RL4IL能够有效应对现实场景中的模态缺失问题，具备良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为无需重训练的多模态机器人学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robotic systems perceive the world through multiple input modalities -- including visual camera streams and natural language instructions -- and must select appropriate actions based on these signals. However, assuming the permanent availability of all input devices is unrealistic, as sensors may fail, become occluded, or drop out entirely during deployment. Robust handling of such missing-modality scenarios is therefore essential for real-world robot operation. This paper introduces RL4IL, a reinforcement learning guided method for imitation learning that selects the most suitable action for a given observation by identifying the most relevant expert demonstrations from a training library. A reinforcement learning policy, trained via Proximal Policy Optimisation over Breadth-First Search candidate sets, ranks candidate demonstrations and a soft cross-attention fusion head aggregates their action signals to produce the final prediction. When a modality is missing at inference time, a dedicated per-modality RL retrieval policy identifies donor demonstrations from the training library, and a soft imputation head reconstructs the missing embedding via cross-attention over the top-ranked donors -- without requiring any retraining of the system. Experiments on three LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that RL4IL substantially outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning methods under sensor dropout conditions, while requiring no policy network training. The code can be found at https://github.com/h-ismkhan/Reinforcement-Learning-via-kNN-for-Robotic-Learning-with-Missing-Camera

</details>


### [162] [Transferring Contact, Not Just Motion: Compliant Grasping Across Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15516)
*Soofiyan Atar,Yao-Ting Huang,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨具身灵巧手的力-位置接口，通过校准接触反馈实现可迁移的柔顺抓取，提升了异构灵巧手之间的操作策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧操作策略主要依赖运动映射，但力反馈受限于特定手部的传感与执行系统，难以在不同结构的手之间迁移；为了实现稳定的操作，需要统一且可转移的接触感知机制。

Method: 引入一种跨具身的力-位置接口，将运动意图表示为共享的手部位姿隐空间，同时通过系统辨识将各手的驱动力信号校准为物理关节扭矩（N.m），并映射到指尖力和每指负载描述符；结合视觉、本体感觉和校准后的接触反馈，采用流匹配方法训练视觉运动策略，并设计结构化视觉遮罩以增强在抓取相关遮挡下对力信号的依赖；同一校准信号用于混合力-位置控制器进行演示采集与执行。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在结构不同的灵巧手上均能实现可迁移的柔顺抓取，学习到的操作原语可用于长周期操作流程，验证了校准接触反馈对策略泛化的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过校准的力反馈与共享运动表示相结合，所提出的跨具身力-位置接口能够有效支持异构灵巧手之间的稳定、可迁移操作，增强了在接触变化、形变和视觉遮挡情况下的操作鲁棒性。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping depends on contact regulation, not motion alone. Stable manipulation requires fingers to maintain appropriate object loading as contacts slip, deform, or become visually occluded. Existing cross-embodiment dexterous policies unify motion through retargeted hand poses or latent actions, but force feedback remains tied to each hand's sensing and actuation, limiting transfer. This work introduces a cross-embodiment force-position interface for contact-aware manipulation across heterogeneous dexterous hands. Motion intent is represented in a shared hand-pose latent, while each hand's effort signal is calibrated through system identification into physical joint torque in N.m. These torques are mapped to fingertip forces and compact per-finger load descriptors, giving the policy comparable observations of where the hand should move and how the object is loaded. Using this interface, a flow-matching visuomotor policy is trained on vision, proprioception, and calibrated contact, with structured visual masking that encourages reliance on force under grasp-relevant occlusion. The same calibrated signal drives a hybrid force-position controller for demonstration collection and execution, keeping force targets consistent across training and deployment. Experiments across structurally different hands show that calibrated contact feedback enables transferable compliant grasping, with learned primitives reusable in long-horizon manipulation pipelines.

</details>


### [163] [Robots as Tokens: Unified Diffusion Transformer for Coordinated Multi-Robot Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15550)
*Ruofei Bai,Jie Chen,Yuxin Cai,Jun Li,Wei-Yun Yau,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Roken的统一扩散Transformer模型，能够以前馈方式直接生成满足安全性和连通性约束的多机器人协同轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多集中于单机器人规划或顺序生成多机器人轨迹并依赖后处理解决冲突，缺乏高效、端到端的协同轨迹生成方法。

Method: 将每个机器人表示为离散token，引入自注意力和交叉注意力机制使其与环境和其他机器人交互，并基于贝叶斯定理设计多尺度时空监督的辅助任务。

Result: 实验表明Roken在杂乱环境中能高成功率地完成连通性约束的导航任务，优于训练数据生成的基线方法，具有良好的可扩展性和对未见或部分观测环境的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Roken实现了端到端、可扩展且多功能的多机器人协同规划，展示了生成模型在复杂多智能体任务中的潜力。

Abstract: The success of generative models in language and visual generation has inspired extensive applications to generative robot planning. However, most existing works either focus on single-robot planning, or generate multi-robot trajectories in a sequential manner with iterative post-processing to resolve inter-robot conflicts. In this work, we investigate whether coordinated multi-robot trajectories, as a special spatiotemporal distribution, can be learned and generated with a generative model in a feed-forward manner. We propose Robots as Tokens (Roken), a unified diffusion transformer that directly generates multi-robot trajectories that satisfy both (individual) safety and (global) connectivity constraints. The core design of Roken is to represent each robot as a discrete token, allowing them to naturally interact with each other through self-attention, and cross-attend to map tokens for environment layouts. We further introduce several auxiliary tasks based on Bayes' theorem to provide multi-scale spatial-temporal supervision for efficient learning of the conditional distribution. In training, Roken absorbs diverse expert trajectories from different team sizes. During inference, Roken behaves as a versatile multi-robot planner that can handle single-robot planning, coordinated multi-robot trajectory generation, and conditional trajectory generation by fixing some robot tokens as conditions. Experiments in diverse cluttered environments show that Roken can generate coordinated multi-robot trajectories to perform connectivity-constrained goal navigation tasks with high success rates, outperforming the baseline method used to generate the training dataset. Roken also demonstrates good scalability after training with mixed team sizes, and shows generalization to unseen or partially observed environments, verifying its potential to learn from diverse data and perform versatile tasks.

</details>


### [164] [SAPS: Shared Autonomy for Policy Steering by Blending Teleoperation with a Pretrained VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15568)
*Crystal Zhou,Jehan Yang,Douglas J. Weber,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAPS的共享自主框架，通过在动作层面融合人类遥操作指令与预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的输出，实现对机器人策略的实时引导。该方法无需重新训练策略模型或修改架构，并设计了三种仲裁策略（包括基于余弦相似度的动态策略）来平衡人与模型的控制权。实验表明，SAPS在仿真和真实机器人任务中显著提升了任务成功率（最高达82%），减少了人为干预频率，并加快了任务完成速度，验证了其在实际应用中的有效性与通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在面对分布外的空间和语义扰动时表现脆弱，而完全依赖人类遥操作则认知负担高、操控要求精确。现有策略引导方法常需额外模型或修改采样器，缺乏灵活性和实用性。因此，需要一种无需模型修改、低干预、高效的人机协作机制来提升通用机器人策略的鲁棒性和可用性。

Method: 提出了Shared Autonomy for Policy Steering (SAPS) 框架，在动作层面融合人类遥操作命令与预训练VLA策略的动作输出。设计了三种仲裁策略：固定权重混合、优先级切换和基于余弦相似度的动态仲裁，其中动态策略通过计算人与策略动作之间的几何一致性来自适应调整控制权。整个框架无需重新训练策略、无需辅助动力学模型或架构改动，具备模型无关性。

Result: 在多个仿真环境（LIBERO, LIBERO-PRO, CALVIN）和真实机器人硬件上进行了评估。结果显示，相比纯自主执行，SAPS将任务成功率最高提升了82%，同时大幅减少所需的人类干预次数，并且比纯自主和纯遥操作更快完成任务。

Conclusion: 动作层面的共享自主是一种实用且模型无关的方法，可有效增强通用机器人策略在现实场景中的可靠性与部署能力。SAPS降低了对人类操作员的依赖，同时保持高性能，为辅助遥操作和可扩展的数据收集提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated impressive generalist capabilities in robot manipulation, yet these policies can be brittle under out-of-distribution spatial and semantic perturbations. While human teleoperation offers reliable recovery, it can demand high cognitive load and precise manual control, and existing policy steering methods often require auxiliary models or sampler modifications. In this work, we introduce Shared Autonomy for Policy Steering (SAPS), a framework that blends real-time human teleoperation commands with pretrained policy actions at the action level. SAPS requires no policy retraining, auxiliary dynamics models, or architectural modifications. We propose and evaluate three arbitration strategies to balance human and VLA policy control, including a dynamic Cosine-similarity arbitration strategy that computes the geometric agreement between human and policy actions. Across evaluations in simulation (LIBERO, LIBERO-PRO, CALVIN) and on real-world robot hardware, SAPS improves task success rates over autonomous execution by up to 82% in both simulation and the real world. Furthermore, our approach drastically reduces human intervention compared to pure teleoperation, while simultaneously achieving faster task completion times than both autonomous execution and pure teleoperation. These results demonstrate that action-level shared autonomy is a practical, model-agnostic approach for reliably deploying generalist robot policies in real-world contexts involving a human operator,with promising applications in assistive teleoperation and scalable data collection.

</details>


### [165] [Perfect Demo Makes Poor Teacher: Learning Robust Alignment from Critical Motion Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15587)
*Mingyu Liu,Zeju Li,Jiuhe Shu,Hanqing Wang,Yuhao Chao,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的机器人学习方法STAIR，解决了在精细操作任务中流畅示范数据对策略训练不足的问题，通过时空特征提供密集的运动感知监督，显著提升了模仿学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流畅的专家示范在精细操作任务中可能导致策略学习到冗余的自由空间运动，而在关键对齐和恢复时刻缺乏足够的监督信号，从而影响学习效果。

Method: 从数据层面和表示层面两方面解决问题：数据层面通过放慢对齐附近的动作并重采样关键片段来增加恢复状态的覆盖；表示层面提出STAIR（Spatio-Temporal feature as an Interface for Robot learning），利用紧凑的动态特征将视觉-语言模型与动作专家连接，提取轨迹中的短期运动信息作为密集监督信号。

Result: 仅使用流畅示范数据训练，STAIR将任务成功率从50.0%提升至62.2%，接近刻意放慢示范的64.4%。

Conclusion: 机器人数据应以机器可学习性为导向进行优化，而不仅仅追求人类操作效率，提倡更具教学性的数据设计。

Abstract: Expert demonstrations are widely assumed to be the gold standard for robot imitation learning. Yet for fine-grained manipulation such as insertion, stacking, and alignment, we uncover a counterintuitive failure mode: fluent demonstrations can be poor teachers. A skilled teleoperator compresses the decisive moments of alignment and recovery into a brief temporal window, leaving the policy flooded with redundant free-space motion and starved of supervision exactly where precision determines success. We address this bottleneck at two levels. At the data level, slowing down near alignment and resampling critical segments both help, yet the gain comes mainly from broadening the coverage of recovery states the policy must learn, not from reweighting frames it already has. Such data-side fixes, however, leave the policy's per-frame view untouched: a single image still maps directly to an action, and the local motion that governs correction stays implicit. We therefore turn to the representation level and introduce STAIR (\textbf{S}patio-\textbf{T}emporal feature \textbf{A}s an \textbf{I}nterface for \textbf{R}obot learning), a compact dynamic feature that bridges the vision-language model and the action expert, distilling the short-horizon motion already recorded in each trajectory into dense, motion-aware supervision. Trained on fluent data alone, STAIR recovers most of the deliberate-demonstration gain ($50.0$ to $62.2\%$ overall, approaching the $64.4\%$ of deliberate demonstrations). These results call for a more pedagogical view of robot data, optimized for machine learnability rather than human efficiency alone.

</details>


### [166] [Pixels to Proofs: Probabilistically-Safe Latent World Model Control via Parallel Conformal Robust MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15594)
*Devesh Nath,Anutam Srinivasan,Haoran Yin,Ruitong Jiang,Jeffrey Fang,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLS^2是一种基于像素的安全反馈运动规划框架，利用在学习的潜在世界模型中进行鲁棒模型预测控制，结合共形预测和系统级合成方法，实现高效且安全的视觉闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于视觉的运动规划中，如何在不完美的潜在空间预测下仍保证真实系统的安全性是一个关键挑战。现有方法难以兼顾规划效率与闭环安全性。

Method: 提出SLS^2框架：1）训练一个动作条件下的联合嵌入世界模型，获得紧凑的马尔可夫潜在状态；2）结合共形预测估计潜在空间误差边界；3）使用GPU加速的系统级合成（SLS）鲁棒MPC，并构建鲁棒的潜在约束集；4）学习并共形化潜在约束检查器，以在闭环中施加概率安全约束。

Result: 在基于视觉的控制任务中，SLS^2相比基线方法在目标达成率和安全性方面均有提升，验证了其在不确定环境下进行高效安全规划的有效性。

Conclusion: SLS^2通过融合学习型潜在世界模型与基于共形预测的鲁棒MPC，在无需精确环境建模的前提下实现了安全、高效的视觉反馈运动规划，为现实场景中的自主控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present SLS^2, a framework for safe feedback motion planning from pixels using robust model predictive control (MPC) in learned latent world models. Our approach trains an action-conditioned joint-embedding world model with compact Markovian latent states, enabling efficient gradient-based trajectory optimization through learned latent dynamics. To enforce safety for the true system despite imperfect latent predictions, we inform a GPU-accelerated system level synthesis (SLS) robust MPC scheme with conformal prediction to obtain calibrated latent error bounds and robust latent-space constraint sets. We further learn and conformalize a latent constraint checker, allowing the SLS planner to impose probabilistic safety constraints during closed-loop execution. We evaluate our method on vision-based control tasks, where it improves both goal-reaching performance and safety over latent world-model and safe-planning baselines.

</details>


### [167] [Retrieve, Don't Retrain: Extending Vision Language Action Models to New Tasks at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15631)
*Jeongeun Park,Juhan Park,Taekyung Kim,Sungjoon Choi,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，通过在部署时添加示范数据而非微调模型参数来适应新任务，从而降低跨任务迁移的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA策略在扩展到新任务时需要任务特定的遥操作演示和逐任务微调，导致数据收集和计算成本高昂。本文旨在消除这种每任务调整的开销。

Method: 提出一种检索增强策略：模型一次性在目标形态（查询）与低成本形态（如人手视频）的配对演示上训练后即冻结；新任务通过将低成本形态的演示加入检索池来引入，推理时每一步依赖检索到的轨迹进行决策。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在PushT和RoboTwin 2.0数据集上优于跨形态基线，在未见任务和目标角度上表现良好，并在真实机器人上得到验证；尤其在基于视频生成的世界-动作模型Cosmos Policy中效果显著，检索提供任务进程先验，未来图像预测增强视觉一致性。

Conclusion: 通过检索机制替代逐任务微调，实现了高效、可扩展的任务适应，仅需一次训练即可通过索引新数据支持新任务，大幅降低了部署新任务的资源成本。

Abstract: Extending a vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a new task typically requires task-specific teleoperated demonstrations and per-task fine-tuning, making adaptation costly in both data collection and compute. In this paper, we show that this target-side per-task adaptation cost can be replaced by retrieval. Our retrieval-augmented policy is trained once on paired demonstrations from the target embodiment (query) and a cheaper embodiment (pool, e.g., human-hand video), then frozen. New tasks are added at deployment by appending pool-side demonstrations to a retrieval pool. The frozen policy conditions on retrieved trajectories at every control step, so new tasks are absorbed by indexing data rather than updating parameters. Fine-tuning is needed only to take on a new, unseen embodiment, not for each new task. We show that retrieval improves policies beyond a specific backbone, including standard VLA policies, but its effect is especially pronounced in Cosmos Policy, a video-generation-based world-action model (WAM). In this setting, retrieval supplies coarse task progression, while the WAM's future-image objective provides an additional visual consistency signal that strengthens the retrieval-conditioned actions. On PushT, we study how retrieval provides a reusable high-level motion prior for cross-embodiment generalization to unseen goal angles, while on RoboTwin 2.0 our method outperforms cross-embodiment baselines on unseen tasks, and we additionally demonstrate the method on a real robot.

</details>


### [168] [TO-SoFiT: Topology Optimization of Hydraulic Soft Fish Tail Design for programmable undulating locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15645)
*A Padmaprabhan,Amal Shaji,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑优化的方法，用于自动化设计液压驱动的软体鱼尾，实现了流体-结构耦合的高效协同设计，并通过仿真验证了其在三维游动中的可调波状运动和多轴弯曲能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的软体机器人缺乏系统化的设计框架，难以实现复杂的三维运动（如鱼类游动），尤其是在水下探索和仿生海洋系统中对敏捷性和适应性要求较高的场景。

Method: 采用基于达西定律并引入排水项的模型模拟空间变化的液压载荷，并通过有限元分析将其转化为节点力；结合鲁棒的多目标优化方法，综合考虑变形效率、流固耦合、可制造性和刚度需求，进行拓扑优化设计。

Result: 优化后的鱼尾在不同液压载荷下表现出可调节的波动幅度和多轴弯曲能力，能够实现深度调节；级联多个优化段可编程生成多种游动模式；二维优化尾比矩形尾性能更优。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了液压执行器与软体结构的协同设计系统化发展，为在受限水下环境中实现具有脊椎动物般灵活性的机器人提供了自动化设计路径。

Abstract: Soft robots leverage compliant materials to generate motion through controlled elastic deformation, making them ideal for delicate tasks such as underwater exploration and biomimetic marine systems. Although hydraulic/pneumatic actuation remains pivotal for such systems, the lack of systematic design frameworks has hindered the development of robots capable of complex 3D motion, such as fish-like swimming. This work introduces a topology optimization method to automate the design of a hydraulic soft fish tail, explicitly addressing the design-dependent coupling between fluidic actuation and structural deformation. We use a Darcy law-based model augmented with a drainage term to simulate spatially varying hydraulic pressure loads, translating these into consistent nodal forces via finite element analysis. The employed robust multi-criteria optimization formulation balances deformation efficiency, fluid-structure interaction, geometric manufacturability, and required stiffness for optimizing a bioinspired soft fish tail for 3D swimming kinematics. The optimized tail topology is incorporated into a pneumatic network actuator and computationally validated under various hydraulic loads, achieving tunable undulatory amplitudes and multiaxis bending for depth adjustment. The optimized 2D tail outperforms its rectangular counterpart. By cascading optimized tail segments, we demonstrate programmable swimming patterns in soft robotic fish tails at different hydraulic loads. This work advances the systematic codesign of hydraulic actuators and soft structures, offering a pathway to automate underwater robots with optimized design and vertebrate-like agility in confined aquatic environments. Our implementations and simulations are publicly available at 'https://github.com/PrabhatIn/TO-SoFiT'.

</details>


### [169] [PO-PDDL: Learning Symbolic POMDPs from Visual Demonstrations for Robot Planning Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15654)
*Wenjing Tang,Xuanjin Jin,Yuan Liu,Renming Huang,Cewu Lu,Panpan Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PO-PDDL的符号化POMDP建模方法，结合演示驱动的学习流程，从真实机器人执行视频中自动学习包含部分可观测性、随机性和信念状态的可重用规划模型，实现了在感知与执行不确定下的高效在线任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实机器人任务规划中，动作执行具有随机性且环境部分可观测，传统POMDP建模困难且耗时，缺乏可扩展性和可重用性，因此需要一种更高效、自动化的方法来构建适用于真实机器人的POMDP模型。

Method: 提出PO-PDDL语言，保留PDDL的结构和语法优势，显式建模部分可观测性与随机性；通过从真实机器人操作视频中重构潜在符号状态轨迹，检测状态与观测之间的不一致性以识别部分可观测性，并据此学习转移与观测的随机模型。

Result: 在真实世界长周期操作任务上的实验表明，该方法优于现有的PDDL与POMDP建模范式，能够在更低规划成本下实现鲁棒的任务规划。

Conclusion: PO-PDDL及其演示驱动的学习框架为现实机器人系统提供了一种实用、可重用且低开销的不确定环境下任务规划解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world robot task planning must operate under both stochastic action execution and partial observability, yet constructing Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) models for real robotics domains remains difficult and labor-intensive. We introduce PO-PDDL, a symbolic formulation of POMDPs that preserves the relational structure and LLM-friendly syntax of the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL), while explicitly modeling partial observability, stochasticity, and beliefs. Building on this formulation, we propose a demonstration-driven pipeline for learning PO-PDDL models. The proposed method reconstructs latent symbolic state trajectories from real-robot execution videos, identifies partial observability via inconsistencies between inferred states and visual observations, and learns stochastic transition and observation models accordingly. The resulting PO-PDDL domains are reusable across tasks and enable online belief-space planning under both perception and execution uncertainty. Experiments on real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks show that our method consistently outperforms existing PDDL and POMDP model-learning approaches, achieving robust task planning under uncertainty with significantly lower planning cost.

</details>


### [170] [Learning New Tasks via Reusable Skills: Skill-Compositional Experts for Embodied Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15685)
*Shuaike Zhang,Shaokun Wang,Haoyu Tang,Jianlong Wu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于具身持续学习（ECL）的技能组合专家框架SCE，通过组合技能 grounding 和双执行-转换专家机制，有效缓解了闭环控制下的灾难性遗忘和特征漂移问题，在仿真和真实操作任务中均表现出优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身持续学习中，机器人需在闭环控制下不断学习新任务并保留旧知识，但现有方法易受严重灾难性遗忘和特征漂移影响，且缺乏对技能结构化复用的支持。

Method: 提出SCE框架，包含组合技能 grounding（CSG）构建可复用技能库，以及双执行-转换专家（DETE）机制，分别负责技能执行与技能间转换，实现连贯行为与持续学习。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和真实世界操作任务上，SCE显著优于现有方法，有效抑制特征漂移，提升任务性能与知识保留能力。

Conclusion: SCE通过结构化技能组织与双路径专家设计，成功解决了ECL中的灾难性遗忘与技能协调难题，为机器人持续学习提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied Continual Learning (ECL) aims to enable robots to continually acquire new manipulation tasks while retaining previously learned behaviors under closed-loop control. Compared with conventional continual learning, ECL suffers from more severe catastrophic forgetting. Feature drift accumulated under closed-loop control progressively propagates through sequential decision-making, leading to degradation of previously learned behaviors. A key challenge in ECL lies in structured skill reuse across continually evolving tasks, since existing methods primarily focus on skill learning without explicitly organizing them for coherent task execution. To address this issue, we propose SCE, a Skill-Compositional Experts framework for ECL. SCE builds a skill base via Compositional Skill Grounding (CSG), which decomposes task demonstrations into reusable skills. Based on this, Dual Execution-and-Transition Experts (DETE) enable new task learning through skill composition, where one branch ensures skill execution and the other supports transitions between skills for coherent behavior. Experiments on LIBERO benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SCE consistently improves retention and overall task performance. Further feature drift analyses and ablation studies verify the effectiveness of our method. Project website: https://eqcy.github.io/sce/.

</details>


### [171] [Can Causal Models Enhance Robot Navigation? Online Causal Adaptation for Real-Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15691)
*Zhitao Liang,Alex Mitrevski,Emmanuel Dean,Karinne Ramirez-Amaro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文研究了在真实机器人导航任务中迁移因果模型的有效性，提出将因果模型用于离线评估和在线适应，实验表明其在复杂场景中显著提升导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统缺乏对行为后果的可解释预测能力，且因果模型在真实机器人系统中的部署研究不足。

Method: 将因果模型应用于真实机器人巡逻任务，分别作为离线评估模块（预测导航轨迹的胜任力）和在线适应模块（在预测胜任力低时进行干预）。

Result: 预测胜任力与路径效率正相关，与路径不规则性负相关；模型预测与人类标注高度一致（Cohen's kappa = 0.88）；在线实验显示在复杂场景中导航性能提升，简单场景中改进有限。

Conclusion: 因果模型可有效集成到真实机器人导航系统中，尤其在任务复杂度较高时能显著增强导航表现。

Abstract: Causality in robotics aims to produce more interpretable and flexible robot behaviours by enabling robots to predict the consequences of their actions; however, deploying causal models with existing systems (e.g., navigation) operating in real environments remains understudied. This paper addresses the challenging problem of transferring causal models in real-robot experiments for a navigation scenario. We study this problem in two ways: (i) using the causal model as an offline evaluation module that predicts the competence of recorded real-robot navigation trajectories and relates it to quantitative navigation performance, and (ii) using the causal model as an online adaptation module that intervenes when the predicted competence of the default navigation is low. We validate our approach in a physical service robot that patrols around corridors. We show that the predicted competence correlates positively with path efficiency, and negatively with path irregularities (suboptimal behaviour). The model predictions also show strong agreement with human annotations (Cohen's kappa value of 0.88). In online experiments, the proposed method improves navigation performance in complex scenarios such as cornering and obstacle avoidance, yielding higher predicted competence and better navigation metrics than the default navigation baseline. In simpler scenarios, where the baseline already performs near-optimally, the causal adaptation provides limited benefit. These results indicate that causal models are particularly effective in enhancing navigation under increased task complexity. Overall, our results demonstrate that causal models developed for behavioural interpretation can be successfully integrated into real-robot navigation systems.

</details>


### [172] [LaWAM: Latent World Action Models for Efficient Dynamics-Aware Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15768)
*Jialei Chen,Kai Wang,Kang Chen,Shuaihang Chen,Feng Gao,Wenhao Tang,Zhiyuan Li,Weilin Liu,Zhuyu Yao,Boxun Li,Yuanbo Xu,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaWAM是一种基于潜在视觉子目标的轻量级世界-动作模型，通过在预训练视觉基础模型的潜在空间中预测场景演化，实现低延迟、高性能的机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型缺乏对动作如何改变场景的显式预见能力，而现有的世界-动作模型依赖计算成本高的视频生成，存在大量像素级冗余。

Method: 提出LaWAM，核心是一个潜在动作条件下的潜在世界模型（LaWM）。通过在预训练视觉基础模型的潜在空间中训练潜在动作模型，并重用其前向解码器来预测未来的观测特征，从而预测场景演化，并以这些潜在视觉子目标为条件生成动作。

Result: LaWAM在LIBERO（98.6%成功率）、RoboTwin（91.22%成功率）及真实世界操作任务上达到最先进或具有竞争力的表现，每动作块预测仅需187毫秒，相比像素空间WAM最多降低24倍的壁钟延迟。

Conclusion: LaWAM通过紧凑的潜在视觉子目标实现动态感知的机器人控制，在保持低推理延迟的同时显著提升了效率和性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) leverage large-scale vision-language pretraining for semantic robot control, but often lack explicit foresight into how robot actions change the scene. World-Action Models (WAMs) address this limitation by conditioning policies on predicted futures, yet existing approaches typically rely on computationally expensive video generation with substantial pixel-level redundancy. We present LaWAM, a Latent World Action Model that exposes predictive dynamics to robot policies through compact latent visual subgoals instead of reconstructed future video. At the core of LaWAM is a latent-action-conditioned Latent World Model (LaWM). We obtain LaWM by training a latent action model in the latent space of a pretrained vision foundation model and repurposing its forward decoder to predict future observation features for scene evolution. LaWAM then conditions action generation on these predicted latent visual subgoals to enable dynamics-aware robot control. LaWAM achieves state-of-the-art or competitive success rates (SRs) across LIBERO (98.6% SR), RoboTwin (91.22% SR), and real-world manipulation tasks while retaining low-latency inference. LaWAM runs in 187 ms per action-chunk prediction and achieves up to 24x lower wall-clock latency than pixel-space WAMs.

</details>


### [173] [FlashNav: Ultra-Fast Policy Training for Robot Navigation within 20 Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15846)
*Shanze Wang,Yiwei Qian,Xinming Zhang,Jun Xue,Siwei Cheng,Xianghui Wang,Qingyuan Hu,Xiaoyu Shen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashNav是首个基于深度强化学习的秒级机器人导航训练框架，通过GPU-first设计在保留必要导航组件的同时去除冗余计算，实现20秒内完成可部署策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在机器人导航中潜力巨大，但训练耗时过长限制了实际部署，亟需加速训练过程。

Method: 提出FlashNav框架，采用GPU-first架构，构建批量位图模拟器和全GPU驻留训练流水线，保留占据几何、范围感知、目标条件控制等关键导航MDP组件，去除渲染和高保真物理细节，并结合FastDSAC学习器实现完全在GPU上生成大规模并行导航转换。

Result: 在RTX 5090上实现了100%成功率且训练时间低于20秒，在多种桌面GPU上均保持在数十秒内；所学策略可成功迁移到TurtleBot2和Unitree Go2等真实轮式与足式机器人，在静态和动态室内场景中展现良好避障能力。

Conclusion: FlashNav首次实现了秒级可部署的DRL导航训练，证明在简化仿真复杂度的同时仍能保持实际可用的导航性能，推动了深度强化学习在机器人导航中的实用化进程。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has shown strong potential for robot navigation, but its practical deployment is still limited by the long wall-clock cost of policy training. This paper presents FlashNav, a GPU-first framework for ultra-fast range-based robot navigation training. To the best of our knowledge, FlashNav is the first DRL-based robot navigation framework that reaches seconds-level policy training, with the fastest deployable policy trained in less than 20 seconds. The key idea is to align simulation with the navigation MDP: FlashNav preserves the essential components for velocity-level navigation, including occupancy geometry, range sensing, goal-conditioned control, robot motion dynamics, collision handling, termination, and reset, while removing unnecessary rendering and high-fidelity physical details from the training loop. Built on a batched bitmap simulator and a fully GPU-resident training pipeline with our FastDSAC learner, FlashNav generates massive parallel navigation transitions entirely on GPU. Experiments on TurtleBot2 and Unitree Go2 show that FlashNav achieves a 100\% success-rate below 20 seconds on an RTX 5090 and remains within tens of seconds across desktop GPUs. The learned policies further transfer to physical wheeled and legged robots in static and dynamic indoor scenes, demonstrating that DRL-based navigation can be trained at seconds-level speed while preserving deployable obstacle-avoidance behavior.

</details>


### [174] [LoComposition: Terrain-Adaptive Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion without Gait Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15896)
*Loukas Kordos,Leonard T. Franz,Simon Rappenecker,Oliver Hausdoerfer,Angela P. Schoellig,Pavel Kolev,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于学习的四足机器人运动框架，通过分离任务规范、操作限制、步态偏好和地形适应等功能机制，实现了高效且适应复杂地形的运动。相比传统方法，该方法在保持穿越能力的同时显著降低了运输成本和操作违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于学习的四足运动依赖复杂的奖励函数，将多种需求耦合在一起，导致效率低下和行为僵化。本文旨在解耦这些功能以提升性能和适应性。

Method: 采用独立机制处理不同功能：用奖励表示任务目标，约束处理操作极限，能量最小化决定步态偏好，并利用外部感知（如LiDAR）根据地形难度调整能耗。不使用显式的步态先验（如腾空时间、触地次数等），让步态行为自然涌现。

Result: 与传统的复杂奖励基线相比，新方法在地形穿越能力相当的情况下，运输成本降低了56%，操作限制违规减少了96%。策略无需微调即可零样本迁移到真实的Unitree Go2机器人上，结合LiDAR高程地图实现实际运行。

Conclusion: 通过功能解耦的设计，可以实现更高效、更鲁棒且更具适应性的四足运动控制，且无需手工设计的步态先验，具备良好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Learning-based quadrupedal locomotion typically relies on complex reward formulations that entangle task specification, operational limits, gait preference, and terrain adaptation within a single optimization objective. We instead treat these functions through distinct mechanisms: rewards for task specification, constraints for operational limits, energy minimization for gait preference, and exteroceptive perception for adapting energy use to terrain difficulty. We show that these components jointly enable efficient, terrain-adaptive locomotion, and that removing each component exposes a distinct failure mode. Our formulation removes explicit gait priors (including air-time, contact-count, and foot-clearance targets) in favor of emergent behavior. Compared to a conventional complex-reward baseline, our formulation achieves comparable terrain traversal while reducing cost of transport by 56% and operational-limit violations by 96%. The resulting policies transfer zero-shot to a physical Unitree Go2 using LiDAR-based elevation mapping. Project website with videos: https://tinyurl.com/locomposition.

</details>


### [175] [VL2Spike: Spike-driven Distillation from VLMs for Low-Power Visual Perception in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15898)
*Zinan Liu,Eric Zheng,Soumyaratna Debnath,Hao Shi,Ling Xiao,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为VL2Spike的新型基于脉冲的知识蒸馏框架，通过结合视觉语言模型（VLMs）的多模态知识提升Spikformer模型的学习能力，同时保持其能效优势，在多个静态数据集和机器人视觉位置识别任务中表现出显著性能增益和低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管脉冲神经网络（SNNs）具有高能效，但其性能受限于基于脉冲的架构和优化难题；而视觉语言模型（VLMs）具备强大的多模态表征能力，因此利用VLMs来增强Spikformer训练是一个有前景的方向。

Method: 提出了空间-时间视觉脉冲（SVS）蒸馏方法，实现VLM图像特征与脉冲令牌之间的流形对齐以及时序一致性；并设计了脉冲原型引导的语言（SPL）蒸馏策略，将Spikformer的类别原型和logits与可提示的VLM文本嵌入对齐。

Result: 在三个静态数据集上实现了6.81%的性能提升，仅消耗15.7%的能量；在机器人视觉位置识别任务中取得6.63%的增益，展现出强泛化能力和低功耗潜力。

Conclusion: VL2Spike为连接多模态知识与紧凑的Spikformer模型提供了有效途径，在保持能量效率的同时显著提升了性能，推动了低功耗具身AI感知的发展。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are brain-inspired, event-driven models that compute with sparse spikes, which enables highly efficient visual perception in resource-constrained embodied AI models. The emergence of Spiking-Transformer models with spike self-attention has substantially improved the learning capacity of pure SNNs. Although SNNs are energy efficient, their performance is still limited by the spike-based architecture and optimization challenges, as standard gradient descent rules cannot be directly applied. Recently, vision-language models (VLMs) have shown rich multi-modal knowledge representation capabilities for visual perception. Thus, it is promising to leverage VLMs for better Spikformer training. To this end, we present VL2Spike, a novel spike-based knowledge distillation (KD) framework that bridges multi-modal knowledge from VLMs with compact Spikformer models. This design enhances the learning capacity of Spikformer models while preserving their energy-efficiency merits, thereby offering a practical pathway toward low-power robotic perception. Our VL2Spike brings two key technical contributions. To align with spiking dynamics, we first propose spatial-temporal visual spike (SVS) distillation, which achieves (1) shared manifold alignment between VLM image features and spike tokens, and (2) warm-started temporal consistency on membrane potentials and spike rates. We then design a novel spike prototype-guided linguistic (SPL) distillation strategy that aligns Spikformer's class prototypes and logits with promptable VLM text embeddings. Extensive experiments show that VL2Spike achieves 6.81% gain across three static datasets with only 15.7% energy consumption. It also exhibits strong generalization capacity on robotic visual place recognition (VPR) with a gain of 6.63%, highlighting its potential for low-power perception in embodied AI.

</details>


### [176] [GeoTLM: Geometry-aware Tactile-Language Models for Contact Motion Orientation Reasoning of Dynamic Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15909)
*Qiutian Li,Zinan Liu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的几何表示引导的触觉-语言模型GeoTLM，通过引入物理基础的几何先验（DGR）来提升动态接触事件中的方向推理能力，在旋转和滑动方向识别任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的触觉-语言模型（TLMs）在理解动态物体的物理属性（如旋转、滑动方向）方面表现不佳，缺乏对接触过程中几何变形的建模能力，因此需要引入物理上合理的几何先验以增强推理性能。

Method: 提出GeoTLM框架，核心是设计了一个轻量级的可微分几何表示模块（DGR），该模块利用接触掩码引导剪切场表征，并采用基于物理直觉的七区域反对称池化结构来捕捉旋转引起的对称性变形模式。

Result: 在两个典型任务（旋转方向与滑动方向推理）上的实验表明，相比未加入几何编码器的基线模型，GeoTLM在新物体旋转方向识别准确率上提升+14.6%，真实传感器滑动方向识别准确率提升+16.2%。

Conclusion: 本工作为触觉-语言模型引入物理一致的几何先验提供了有效路径，推动了动态物体感知与高接触复杂度机器人操作的发展。

Abstract: Modern tactile-language models (TLMs) have shown potential for robot learning tasks, such as material and texture recognition. However, for contact-rich scenarios, these TLMs struggle to understand the physical properties of dynamic objects, such as rotation and sliding directions. For instance, our preliminary experiments reveal that popular TLMs, such as Sparsh and AnyTouch2, exhibit weak performance on basic rotation direction reasoning from GelSight Mini tactile data. This surprising gap inspires us to explore a novel research question: Can we inject physically grounded geometric priors into TLMs to enable reliable contact orientation reasoning of dynamic object properties? To this end, we propose GeoTLM, a novel geometric representation-guided TLM for the perception of dynamic contact events. Our key idea is to preserve and structure tactile shear-field geometry before language-level reasoning, rather than forcing low-resolution tactile tokens into fragile closed-form physics operators. To achieve this, we propose a lightweight (only 14k parameters) yet novel Differentiable Geometric Representation (DGR). Specifically, DGR learns a contact-mask-guided representation in the shear field and aggregates it through an antisymmetric seven-region pooling design, motivated by the physical intuition that rotational contact produces antisymmetric deformation patterns. We conduct experiments on two representative tasks: rotation direction and sliding direction reasoning. Extensive experiments show that GeoTLM improves novel-object rotation accuracy by +14.6% and real-sensor sliding accuracy by +16.2% over the same backbone without the geometric encoder. Overall, our work paves a new way for physically grounded tactile-language reasoning, with strong potential for dynamic object understanding and contact-rich robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [177] [Identification of a Physics-Based Electrical Power Consumption Model for the Unitree G1 Humanoid Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15915)
*Nestor N. Deniz,Sebastian Vega,Simon Parsons,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理的线性参数模型，用于预测Unitree G1人形机器人左臂的电功耗，结合执行器损耗和基线转矩校正，并通过实验数据验证了其高精度和强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测电力消耗对于电池驱动的人形机器人在能量感知运动规划、电池管理和热监控方面至关重要。

Method: 采用基于物理的线性参数模型，结合执行器损耗项与基线转矩校正，并引入成对交互项以建模多关节运动时的功率耦合；模型参数通过机器人实测数据辨识得到。

Result: 在897条轨迹上模型达到R²=0.933，RMSE为1.07W；在未见速度下的46条新轨迹上验证得R²=0.965，表现出良好泛化性；参数分析揭示了各关节不同的功耗特性。

Conclusion: 该模型能准确预测人形机器人手臂的电功耗并具有良好的解释性和外推能力，适用于复杂运动下的能量管理。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of electrical power consumption is essential for energy-aware motion planning, battery management, and thermal monitoring in battery-powered humanoid robots. This letter presents a physics-based, linear-in-parameters model for the electrical power consumption of the seven-degree-of-freedom left arm of the Unitree~G1 humanoid robot. The proposed formulation combines actuator loss terms with a baseline-torque correction that captures changes in gravity-compensation load and enables accurate prediction of negative net power trajectories. Pairwise interaction terms are introduced to model power coupling during simultaneous multi-joint motion. Model parameters are identified from experimental data collected on a physical Unitree~G1 using onboard power measurements as the regression target. Across 897 trajectories covering single-joint and coordinated arm motions at multiple speed levels, the identified model achieves $R^2 = 0.933$ with an RMSE of 1.07 (W). Validation on 46 trajectories executed at previously unseen speeds yields $R^2 = 0.965$, demonstrating strong generalisation beyond the identification dataset. Analysis of the identified parameters reveals distinct power-consumption characteristics across the arm, with viscous friction dominating most joints (shoulder pitch and all three wrist joints), copper losses dominating shoulder yaw and the elbow, and shoulder roll uniquely dominated by Coulomb friction.

</details>


### [178] [Energy-Efficient Arm Reaching for a Humanoid Robot via Deep Reinforcement Learning with Identified Power Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15918)
*Nestor N. Deniz,Simon Parsons,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的能量感知强化学习框架，用于Unitree G1人形机器人7自由度左臂的节能控制，结合物理动力学仿真与实验验证，实现了在能量约束下的高效臂部运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在田间操作任务中面临严重的能量限制，影响其每节电池充电后可执行的到达动作次数，因此需要开发节能的控制策略以延长作业时间。

Method: 采用基于Pinocchio刚体动力学的仿真环境，结合经过实验识别的电功率模型，使用Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）算法在增量关节位置动作空间上进行训练，并设计包含四点末端执行器星座距离和力矩范数能量代理的混合奖励函数。

Result: 在运动学仿真中，经过5×10⁶步训练后，对1000个随机目标达到69.9%的成功率，成功案例平均能耗为98.16焦耳；在真实Unitree G1机器人上的三个独立10目标批次测试中，平均能耗为71.5±48.3 J，末端位置误差为2.64±1.04 cm，姿态误差为6.92±1.33°，均在训练容差范围内。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了人形机器人手臂在能量感知强化学习驱动下的首次节能到达控制，为未来复杂环境中长期自主操作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots performing in-field manipulation tasks, such as robotic apple harvesting, face severe energy constraints that directly limit the number of reaching motions that can be executed per battery charge. This paper presents an end-to-end, energy-aware reinforcement learning framework for the 7-degree-of-freedom left arm of the Unitree~G1 humanoid robot, combining a physics-based, experimentally identified electrical power model with a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) policy trained in a Pinocchio-based rigid-body dynamics simulator. The RL policy operates on an incremental joint-position action space and is trained with a Hybrid Constellation Reward that combines a four-point end-effector constellation distance with a torque-norm energy proxy; after % $5\times10^6$ training it reaches a $69.9\%$ success rate over $1\,000$ random targets in kinematic simulation, at a mean energy of \SI{98.16}{\joule} on successful episodes. Finally, on the physical Unitree~G1, the policy is validated over three independent 10-target batches, achieving a mean energy of $71.5 \pm 48.3$\,J, an end-effector position error of $2.64 \pm 1.04$\,cm, and an orientation error of $6.92 \pm 1.33^\circ$ -- within the \SI{4}{\centi\metre}/$8.6^\circ$ training tolerance. These results constitute a first step toward energy-aware reinforcement-learning-based arm reaching for humanoid robots.

</details>


### [179] [ControlMap: Controllable High-Definition Map Generation for Traffic Scenario Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15930)
*Marwan Farag,Steffen Wäldele,Yu Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜在扩散模型和ControlNet的空间条件控制的高精地图生成方法，首次将空间引导信号引入扩散模型，实现了对道路拓扑结构的精确控制，并支持城市级风格迁移与可调节的条件强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶仿真场景中高精地图生成成本高、多样性不足，且缺乏对特定道路拓扑的精细控制能力。

Method: 采用数据驱动的方法，结合潜在扩散模型与ControlNet进行空间条件控制；引入分类器自由指导调节条件强度，并通过城市标签实现风格迁移；同时提出了两个新指标评估控制信号遵循度和真实地图相似性。

Result: 实验表明该模型能生成符合输入道路拓扑、细节逼真的高精地图，并在城市特定特征保持方面表现良好；所提新指标有效衡量了生成质量。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了高精地图生成的可控性与多样性，为自动驾驶仿真提供了更高效、灵活的地图合成方案。

Abstract: Simulation is central to validating autonomous driving systems, yet current pipelines are limited by insufficient scenario diversity due to costly High Definition (HD) map creation. Scaling HD maps requires expensive data collection and manual processing. Moreover, existing generative models lack the fine-grained control necessary to target specific road topologies during generation.
  This paper presents a data-driven pipeline for controllable HD map generation using latent diffusion and ControlNet for spatial conditioning. To our knowledge, we are the first to inject spatial guidance signals into a diffusion model for HD map synthesis. Furthermore, our model supports adjustable conditioning strength through classifier-free guidance and city-level style transfer via city label conditioning. To complement existing metrics, we introduce two novel metrics to evaluate adherence to the control signal and similarity to ground-truth maps. Experiments demonstrate that our model generates realistic HD maps that faithfully follow input road topologies while accurately preserving city-specific details.

</details>


### [180] [Friction Characterization of a Cable-Driven Differential Actuation System for Lower-Limb Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15997)
*Alberto Maria Nobili,Fabio Salsedo,Alessandro Filippeschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于下肢外骨骼髋-膝关节屈伸的新型差动驱动架构，通过电机与关节间的线性差动映射实现双电机协同扭矩分配，并结合基于模型的摩擦估计策略，实现了无需扭矩传感器的精确关节扭矩估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低下肢外骨骼系统的重量和复杂性，避免使用每个关节独立驱动和关节级扭矩传感器的传统方案。

Method: 设计了一种髋-膝关节差动驱动结构，采用线性差动映射实现双电机协同输出，并开发基于模型的摩擦力估计方法以补偿传动损耗。

Result: 在物理样机上验证了该方案的有效性，实现了无扭矩传感器下的精确关节扭矩估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的差动驱动与模型基摩擦补偿方法可行且有效，为轻量化、低复杂度的下肢外骨骼设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Lower-limb exoskeletons require actuation systems that can provide accurate joint torque control while preserving low mass and encumbrance. Conventional architectures often rely on independently actuated joints and joint-level torque sensors, increasing system complexity and weight. This paper presents a novel differential actuation architecture for hip-knee flexion/extension, enabling cooperative torque sharing between two motors via a linear differential mapping between motor and joint. To compensate for transmission losses, a model-based friction estimation strategy is developed and experimentally implemented, allowing accurate joint torque estimation without the need for torque sensors. The proposed solution is validated on a physical prototype, demonstrating the feasibility of sensorless torque estimation in a differentially actuated hip-knee module of a lower-limb exoskeleton.

</details>


### [181] [$λ$-Reachability: Geometric-Horizon Safety Bellman Equations for Humanoid Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16022)
*Rui Chen,Shangtao Li,Yifan Sun,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了λ-Reachability，一种用于高维机器人系统的可扩展哈密顿-雅可比安全分析方法，通过随机多步估计和几何分布的回溯水平提升安全性学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于固定单步Bellman更新的安全性分析方法难以有效处理高维系统中的长时域安全目标，需要更灵活且可扩展的方法。

Method: 引入λ-Reachability方法，采用具有几何分布回溯水平和随机吸收终端的随机多步估计器来估计安全值，并通过参数δ控制终端值的使用概率，实现局部一致性更新与长时域安全目标之间的插值。

Result: 理论证明该方法在δ<1时能形成收缩映射，允许时序差分学习；当λ→1时可恢复无折扣的可达性目标。实验表明其在模拟和真实人形机器人上均显著优于单步基线方法，在安全集边界分类和安全裕度估计方面表现更优。

Conclusion: λ-Reachability为高维机器人系统提供了一种高效、可扩展的安全性分析框架，兼具理论保证和实际性能优势。

Abstract: We introduce $λ$-Reachability, a scalable approach to Hamilton--Jacobi safety analysis for high-dimensional robotic systems. Unlike prior discounted formulations that rely on fixed one-step Bellman updates, $λ$-Reachability employs a stochastic multi-step estimator of the safety value, using a geometrically distributed rollout horizon together with a randomly absorbed terminal. Conceptually analogous to TD($λ$), $λ$-Reachability interpolates between local self-consistency updates and long-horizon max-over-trajectory safety targets via an interpretable horizon-control parameter. Unlike TD($λ$), where the terminal value is always incorporated in learning targets, the terminal safety value in $λ$-Reachability is only used at a probability controlled by parameter $δ$. We formally show that for $δ<1$, the update induces a contraction mapping that allows temporal-difference learning; as $λ\to 1$, the estimator recovers the undiscounted reachability objective. We apply $λ$-Reachability to high-dimensional safety learning problems with both simulated and real humanoid robots under balance and collision avoidance constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that $λ$-Reachability significantly improves both safe-set boundary classification and safety margin estimation compared to single-step temporal-difference baselines.

</details>


### [182] [Leveraging Deep Learning for Object and Position Recognition of Load Carriers for Autonomous Logistics Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16042)
*Christoph Legat,Tobias Miller,Marco Riess*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度学习的RGBD框架，用于自主物流车辆的载具检测与位姿估计，通过卷积神经网络识别特征点并结合几何信息计算位姿，实验验证了其在工业环境中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现自动化物流中移动机器人对载具的自主检测与精确位姿估计，以支持无人化搬运任务。

Method: 设计一个卷积神经网络，直接在RGBD图像上识别载具上的预定义标志点，并结合先验几何知识推算载具的位姿。

Result: 实验表明该方法在软件和硬件平台上均具有足够高的精度，能够满足工业环境中可靠检测的需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法适用于自主厂内物流应用，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: This work explores the use of artificial intelligence in mobile robotics to achieve autonomous detection and pose estimation of load carriers for automated pickup. A deep neural network is designed to recognize predefined landmarks on the carrier from RGBD data; these landmarks are then used to compute the carrier's pose. The network operates directly on RGBD images to estimate landmark positions, which form the basis for determining the carrier's location. The approach is validated in extensive experiments and comprises both software and hardware implementations.
  A deep learning-based framework is presented to detect load carriers and estimate their pose for use with autonomous logistics vehicles. Our method uses a convolutional neural network to identify characteristic reference points on the carrier from RGBD input and computes its pose by combining these inferred landmarks with prior geometric knowledge. Experiments show that the resulting accuracy is sufficient for reliable load carrier detection in industrial environments, confirming the suitability of the method for autonomous intralogistics applications.

</details>


### [183] [A Smart-Scheduled Hybrid (SSH) EKF-FGO State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16057)
*Eric Levi,Soosan Beheshti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在混合状态估计系统中优化调度作为独立设计变量的作用，通过SSH EKF-FGO框架实验表明，合理的优化调度可在降低计算成本的同时保留大部分全局优化的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人和控制中的状态估计需要平衡估计精度与计算成本，而现有方法通常隐式处理优化调度问题，本文旨在明确将其作为关键设计变量进行研究。

Method: 采用Smart Scheduled Hybrid (SSH) EKF-FGO框架，结合EKF状态传播与周期性批量优化，并固定求解器结构和计算量，以隔离并分析优化调度的影响。

Result: 仿真结果显示调度策略显著影响优化前的漂移、瞬态误差行为和运行时间，特定调度下可保留大部分全局优化性能，同时大幅降低计算开销。

Conclusion: 优化调度是影响中间估计精度与计算成本权衡的关键因素，在混合状态估计系统中应被显式考虑，具有重要设计价值。

Abstract: Reliable state estimation in robotics and control re quires balancing estimation accuracy against computational cost. While filtering-based methods such as the Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) provide efficient real-time updates, and optimisation based formulations using factor graphs improve global consistency, the role of optimisation scheduling is often treated implicitly rather than examined as an explicit design variable. This paper presents an experimental study that explicitly isolates optimisation scheduling using a Smart Scheduled Hybrid (SSH) EKF-FGO framework as a controlled testbed. By combining EKF-based state propagation with periodically invoked batch optimisation and holding solver structure and effort fixed, the main contribution of this work is the experimental characterisation of optimisation scheduling as an independent design variable governing the trade-off between intermediate estimation accuracy and computational cost. Simulation results in a planar SLAM environment show that scheduling strongly influences pre optimisation drift, transient error behaviour, and runtime. In particular, the results identify operating regimes in which most of the benefit of global optimisation can be retained at a fraction of the computational cost, highlighting optimisation scheduling as an under-explored yet critical consideration in hybrid state estimation systems.

</details>


### [184] [A Deployment Case Study in Robotic Apparel Automation: Digital Twin Integration, Interoperability, and Workforce Enablement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16078)
*Gokul Narayanan,Abhiroop Ajith,Jonathan Zornow,Carlos Calle,Auralis Herrero Lugo,Jose Luis Susa Rincon,Chengtao Wen,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向实际部署的机器人缝纫系统，用于牛仔布制造，通过数字线程、数字孪生、互操作性层和运行时验证等技术实现系统级集成，支持在工厂中规模化应用柔性自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服装制造中的自动化因织物易变形、难以由机器人操控而面临挑战，亟需系统性解决方案以推动实际部署和广泛应用。

Method: 开发了一个包含数字线程模块（将DXF图纸转化为机器人轨迹）和数字孪生工作单元（用于部署前验证可达性、布局、节拍等）的系统，并在部署中集成了协作机器人、传统缝纫设备、夹具与控制器，辅以运行时监控与操作员培训工具。

Result: 在两个阶段的牛仔短裤生产线部署中，成功实现了二维口袋缝制和三维成衣塑形缝合，验证了系统在减少编程负担、降低调试风险、提升鲁棒性和支持人机协作方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 数字孪生预验证、数字线程驱动任务生成、系统互操作性、运行时验证及操作员培训是推动机器人服装自动化规模化落地的关键要素。

Abstract: Despite steady advances in flexible automation in sectors such as electronics and automotive manufacturing, apparel automation remains challenging because fabrics are deformable and difficult to manipulate with robots. This paper presents a deployment-oriented case study of a robotic sewing system for denim manufacturing, emphasizing the system-level integration required for practical adoption. At the engineering level, a digital thread module parses DXF production drawings into process parameters and executable robot trajectories, reducing manual programming effort and enabling rapid re-targeting across sewing operations. In parallel, a digital twin of the workcell is used during pre-deployment to validate reach and clearance, refine layout and sequencing, evaluate operator access, and assess cycle-time compatibility with upstream and downstream tasks, thereby reducing commissioning risk. At deployment, the system integrates a collaborative robot with conventional sewing equipment, welding, suction fixtures, and machine-level controllers through an interoperability layer. Runtime monitoring and verification, including seam monitoring, collision checking, and trajectory-level validation, improve robustness under environmental variability, while operator-facing training and guidance tools support setup, troubleshooting, and technology adoption. Two staged factory deployments on denim shorts, covering 2D pocket operations and 3D garment-shaping seams, show that digital-twin-based validation, digital-thread-driven task generation, interoperability, runtime verification, and operator training are important for scaling robotic apparel automation.

</details>


### [185] [Scaling Short-Term Memory of Visuomotor Policies for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16178)
*Rutav Shah,Rajat Kumar Jenamani,Xiaohan Zhang,Lingfeng Sun,Roberto Martín-Martín,Yuke Zhu,Deva Ramanan,Karl Schmeckpeper*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一种基于transformer的具身视觉运动策略架构，通过门控注意力和分层结构有效利用短期记忆，支持长达两分钟的记忆跨度，并在新提出的ReMemBench基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数模仿学习训练的视觉运动策略仅依赖当前感知输入，缺乏对短期记忆的有效利用，难以应对需要记忆过去信息的机器人任务。

Method: 提出PRISM架构，包含两个关键组件：(i) 门控注意力机制，用于过滤历史信息、抑制无关细节；(ii) 分层结构，先将局部信息压缩为紧凑token，再整合以捕捉长期时序依赖。

Result: 在ReMemBench、RoboCasa和LIBERO等多个基准上，PRISM比最强基线平均提升5%–12%，相比无记忆版本和微调的VLA模型（如GR00T-N1-3B、OpenVLA）绝对提升达11%–15%，且无需大规模预训练。

Conclusion: PRISM与ReMemBench为可扩展的短期记忆增强型视觉运动策略提供了有效架构和系统评估基础，推动长视野机器人任务的发展。

Abstract: Many robotic tasks require short-term memory, whether it's retrieving an object that's no longer visible or turning off an appliance after a set period. Yet, most visuomotor policies trained via imitation learning rely only on immediate sensory input without using past experiences to guide decisions. We present PRISM, a transformer-based architecture for visuomotor policies to effectively use short-term memory via two key components: (i) gated attention, which filters retrieved information to suppress irrelevant details, improving performance by reducing the spurious correlations between the history and current action prediction, (ii) a hierarchical architecture that first compresses local information into compact tokens and then integrates them to capture temporally extended dependencies, improving its compute and memory footprint. Together, these mechanisms enable us to scale short-term memory in visuomotor policies for up to two minutes. To systematically evaluate memory in visuomotor control, we introduce ReMemBench -- a benchmark of eight diverse household manipulation tasks spanning four categories of short-term memory -- designed to foster general memory mechanisms rather than siloed, task-specific solutions. PRISM consistently outperforms prior works, including recurrent architectures, transformers, and their variants -- achieving an absolute improvement of 5%--12% over the strongest baseline. On the RoboCasa and LIBERO benchmarks, it achieves absolute improvements of 11%--15% over its no-memory variant and fine-tuned Vision-Language-Action baselines such as GR00T-N1-3B and OpenVLA, despite not leveraging any large-scale pretraining. Together, PRISM and ReMemBench establish a foundation for developing and evaluating short-term memory-augmented visuomotor policies that scale to long-horizon tasks. Additional materials are available at https://shahrutav.github.io/short-term-memory

</details>


### [186] [ATHENA: Accelerated Multi-Task Heterogeneous Influence Functions for Robot Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16208)
*Tao Xu,Jiaxin Wang,Runhao Zhang,Jiayi Guan,Xianchao Zeng,Weixi Song,Xinyu Zhou,Zhetao Chen,Guang Chen,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATHENA是一种针对十亿参数规模多任务视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的数据整理框架，通过改进影响函数计算，显著提升效率并在仿真和真实机器人任务中以更少数据达到或超过全数据微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人模仿学习中，影响函数可用于量化每个示范对任务结果的影响，但将其扩展到十亿参数的多任务VLA模型面临计算和多任务瓶颈。

Method: 提出ATHENA框架，利用线性层梯度的Kronecker结构降低投影成本，并采用低秩随机截断近似来逼近稠密Hessian矩阵的逆，从而加速影响计算；同时设计全局与局部交互影响以平衡50个联合训练任务的数据整理。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0和真实机器人部署上的实验表明，ATHENA在仅使用50%模拟数据和66.7%真实数据的情况下，性能达到或超过全数据联合微调，影响计算速度提升约313.4倍。

Conclusion: ATHENA有效解决了大规模多任务VLA模型中影响函数计算的效率与可扩展性问题，为高效数据整理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In robot imitation learning, influence functions provide a principled approach to quantify each demonstration's effect on robot task outcomes, yet scaling them to billion-parameter Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is limited by computational and multitask bottlenecks. To this end, we propose ATHENA, an influence function framework tailored for multitask VLA data curation at a billion-parameter scale. Concretely, it leverages the Kronecker structure of linear-layer gradients to reduce projection cost, and approximates dense Hessian inversion with a rank-r Random Truncated Approximation, achieving about a 313.4x speedup in influence computation. Furthermore, ATHENA formulates global and local interactive influence to balance data curation across 50 jointly trained tasks. Extensive evaluations on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-robot deployment, covering 9.34 and 6.90 hours of demonstrations, respectively, show that ATHENA matches or exceeds full-data joint fine-tuning using only 50% of demonstrations in simulation and 66.7% of data across six real-robot tasks. Overall, ATHENA demonstrates its effectiveness for data curation in billion-parameter multitask VLA fine-tuning.

</details>


### [187] [PolyMerge: Compressing 3D Gaussian Splats with Polytope Coverings for Provably Safe Resource-Constrained Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16232)
*Jihoon Hong,Chih-Yuan Chiu,Sara Fridovich-Keil,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PolyMerge的方法，将高保真的3D高斯点阵模型转换为轻量级的凸多面体表示，用于在计算资源受限的设备上实现实时、安全的避障路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于辐射场重建的障碍物检测方法虽然精度高，但计算和内存开销大，难以应用于机载实时路径规划。因此需要一种更轻量且保证安全性的表示方法。

Method: 提出PolyMerge方法，将大规模的3D高斯点阵（3DGS）场景模型合并为一组凸多面体，其并集可证明地过近似原模型中的所有障碍物；并通过调节多面体数量平衡保守性与计算成本，结合控制屏障函数（CBFs）实现安全路径规划。

Result: 在仿真和Crazyflie无人机硬件实验中验证了PolyMerge的有效性，能够在严格机载计算限制下实时计算并跟踪安全轨迹，相比基线方法在速度上表现更优，同时保证安全性。

Conclusion: PolyMerge实现了从高保真场景表示到轻量安全障碍物建模的高效转换，适用于资源受限平台的实时安全导航。

Abstract: Obstacle avoidance is essential for safe navigation and motion planning. Recent radiance field reconstruction methods enable object detection and modeling with high fidelity, but remain too memory- and compute-intensive for on-board perception-based path planning.
