To address these limitations, we propose PolyMerge to convert a large, photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model of a scene into a lightweight representation of convex polytopes whose union provably over-approximates all obstacles in the original 3DGS model. PolyMerge tunes the polytope count to trade off conservativeness and compute cost, and integrates with control barrier functions (CBFs) to plan collision-free paths. We showcase PolyMerge in simulation and hardware experiments on a Crazyflie drone, which uses PolyMerge to compute and follow safe trajectories in real time under severe onboard compute constraints, outperforming baselines in speed while guaranteeing safety. For our code and videos, visit https://athlon76.github.io/PolyMerge-website/.

</details>


### [188] [TopoRetarget: Interaction-Preserving Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16272)
*Jielin Wu,Shenzhe Yao,Guanqi He,Xiaohan Liu,Zhaoqing Zeng,Xiangrui Jiang,Han Yang,Wentao Zhang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TopoRetarget是一种保持交互的重定向框架，通过构建稀疏交互图并优化拉普拉斯形变，有效保留手-物接触结构，提升机器人灵巧操作策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重定向方法在将人类手部操作演示迁移到机器人时难以保持任务相关的手-物接触结构，导致接触和可行性伪影，影响强化学习策略性能。

Method: 提出TopoRetarget框架，构建手部与物体关键点上的稀疏交互图，通过距离加权的拉普拉斯形变优化，结合方向一致性、运动学约束和穿透处理，实现跨不同条件的参数共享与交互保持。

Result: 在ContactPose数据集上实现了最优的接触精度与对齐效果；在Pen-Spin任务中训练成功率比基线提升40.6个百分点；并在Wuji Hand硬件上实现了魔方旋转和笔旋转的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: TopoRetarget能有效保持人类演示中的关键交互结构，显著提升基于参考跟踪的灵巧操作策略学习性能，并支持真实硬件上的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Human hand-object demonstrations provide dense reference motions for training dexterous manipulation reinforcement learning (RL) policies through reference tracking. However, to use such demonstrations for RL policy learning, retargeting must preserve hand pose and task-relevant hand-object contact structure. Otherwise, contact and feasibility artifacts can degrade downstream RL policy performance. We introduce TopoRetarget, an interaction-preserving retargeting framework that uses a single set of parameters across diverse retargeting conditions while maintaining task-relevant hand-object interaction and adapting human demonstrations to dexterous robot hands. The method constructs a sparse interaction graph over hand and object keypoints and optimizes distance-weighted Laplacian deformation with directional consistency, kinematic constraints, and penetration handling. Evaluations show that the generated references improve both interaction fidelity and policy learning: TopoRetarget achieves the best contact precision and alignment over all baselines on the ContactPose Dataset, improves Pen-Spin training success by 40.6 percentage points over the existing baseline methods, and enables zero-shot transfer to Wuji Hand hardware on cube reorientation and pen spinning.

</details>


### [189] [Is Your Trajectory Displacement Safe in Long-tail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16313)
*Qiao Sun,Weicheng Zheng,Yixin Huang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FluidTest的自动驾驶规划评估新方法，通过检测规划轨迹相对于专家参考是否引入了新的安全隐患，解决了现有评估方法在长尾场景下不一致、不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶评估方法在长尾场景中存在瓶颈，缺乏人类对齐、安全性感知、可验证性和可解释性的统一，难以准确识别先进规划器中的潜在安全问题。

Method: 将规划评估建模为额外威胁检测任务，设计包含成对WebUI标注协议、32类语义威胁分类体系以及三代理反射式验证系统的Pipeline。

Result: 在WOD-E2E数据集上的实验表明，训练后的标注员使用FluidTest能获得一致标签，并发现Poutine和RAP轨迹中分别有65%和51%存在额外威胁，揭示了高RFS和低ADE模型仍存在显著安全缺陷。

Conclusion: FluidTest提供了一个可靠、可审计且语义丰富的评估框架，能够有效识别当前最先进规划器中的安全关键错误，推动更安全的自动驾驶系统发展。

Abstract: Long-tail scenarios remain a major bottleneck for autonomous driving evaluation, even as datasets grow by orders of magnitude. Existing evaluation pipelines are rarely human-aligned, safety-aware, verifiable, and explainable at the same time: closed-loop metrics often saturate among strong planners, while unstructured human ratings can be noisy without a carefully designed protocol. We formulate planning evaluation as additional-threat detection: given a planner trajectory and an expert reference, does the planner's displacement introduce new unsafe driving behavior? We propose FluidTest, an evaluation pipeline with three components: a pairwise WebUI protocol for reliable human annotation; a taxonomy of 32 semantic threats with evidence-grounded decision graphs; and a three-agent verification system with reflection for precision and auditability. Experiments on the WOD-E2E dataset show that FluidTest produces consistent labels among trained annotators and identifies additional threats in 65% of Poutine trajectories and 51% of RAP trajectories. These results show that state-of-the-art planners can still exhibit substantial safety-relevant failures despite high Rater Feedback Scores (RFS) and low Average Displacement Error (ADE). Additional details, guidance, and code are available at https://fluidtest.web.app.

</details>


### [190] [ART-Glove: Articulated Tactile Glove for Contact-Grounded Dexterous Interaction Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16370)
*Changyi Lin,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ART-Glove是一种可穿戴的触觉手套，能够捕捉精细且有接触的手部动作演示，同时保留人类手部灵活性。它通过16个刚性功能表面和22个解剖对齐关节来模拟手部结构，并同步记录22自由度运动和2048像素触觉数据，适用于灵巧机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保持人类手部灵活性的同时，准确捕捉复杂操作中的接触信息，以支持灵巧机器人学习的需求。

Method: 设计了一种名为ART-Glove的手套，采用16个刚性功能表面覆盖手指、拇指和手掌，通过22个解剖对齐的关节实现自然手部运动；使用编码器传感追踪表面运动，压阻式触觉传感器密集记录接触数据，并实现运动与触觉信号的同步采集。

Result: 系统可在120Hz下同步采集22-DoF关节数据和2048-taxel触觉数据，实验验证了其在运动自由度、关节感知、触觉感知及复杂接触交互捕捉方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ART-Glove能够在不干扰人类灵巧操作的前提下，精确捕捉接触丰富的操作数据，为机器人灵巧操作的学习提供了高质量的数据采集方案。

Abstract: We present ART-Glove, an articulated tactile glove designed to capture contact-grounded dexterous demonstrations while preserving human dexterity. ART-Glove makes hand-side contact geometry explicit with 16 rigid functional surfaces covering the fingers, thumb, and palm. Twenty-two anatomically aligned joints connect these surfaces and allow them to follow human hand motion during dexterous manipulation. Encoder-based sensing tracks surface motion, while dense piezoresistive tactile sensing records contact over the same surfaces. The complete system captures synchronized 22-DoF joint measurements and 2048-taxel tactile measurements at 120 Hz. We evaluate ART-Glove across experiments on motion freedom, joint sensing, tactile sensing, and contact-rich interaction capture, demonstrating its ability to preserve human dexterity while recording contact-grounded information that can support downstream dexterous robot learning.

</details>


### [191] [SemGeoNav:A Safety-Guided Visual Navigation Approach with Semantic Reasoning and Geometric Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16400)
*Yu Liu,Zongyang Chen,Yan Guo,Chao Liu,Xianfei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SemGeoNav的分层视觉导航框架，结合了端到端模型的高层语义推理与基于几何方法的可靠局部规划，提升了图像导航中的避障能力与运动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯学习型端到端模型在开放环境中缺乏明确的几何约束，导致避障不可靠；传统几何规划器虽安全但难以处理高维视觉目标。为此需融合两者优势。

Method: 设计SemGeoNav框架，将端到端语义推理与几何局部规划紧密结合，并引入时间轨迹平滑机制以保证机器人运动连续稳定。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人上进行真实环境测试，SemGeoNav在成功率和导航时间上优于ViNT和NoMaD等现有方法。

Conclusion: SemGeoNav通过语义与几何的协同，实现了鲁棒的图像导航，显著提升了避障性能和运动稳定性。

Abstract: Learning-based visual navigation has enhanced semantic goal-reaching capabilities. However, due to their black-box nature, purely end-to-end models often lack explicit geometric constraints, leading to unpredictable and unreliable obstacle avoidance in open environments. Conversely, traditional geometric planners ensure safety but struggle with high-dimensional visual targets. To address these limitations, we propose SemGeoNav, a novel hierarchical visual navigation framework.It tightly integrates the high-level semantic reasoning of end-to-end models with the reliable local planning ability of geometry-based methods, achieving robust image-based navigation while significantly improving obstacle avoidance. Furthermore, we introduce a temporal trajectory smoothing mechanism to ensure continuous and stable robot motion. We evaluated SemGeoNav on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot in real-world environments. The results demonstrate that SemGeoNav outperforms existing representative methods, including ViNT and NoMaD, achieving higher success rates and shorter navigation times.

</details>


### [192] [An Augmented Reality Brain-Robot Interface for Generalist Robot Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16413)
*Shangkai Zhang,Rousslan Fernand Julien Dossa,Luca Nunziante,Marina Di Vincenzo,Kai Arulkumaran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合增强现实（AR）和基于脑电图（EEG）的脑机接口的通用机器人操作界面，通过眼动追踪和运动想象实现直观控制，并在日常生活中三项多步任务中验证了其可行性和高用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AR脑-机器人接口系统通常局限于特定任务结构，难以适应真实环境中的多样化需求，因此需要开发一种适用于通用机器人操作的更灵活、直观的交互方式。

Method: 设计了一个结合注视选择与运动想象动作控制的AR脑-机器人接口系统，利用眼动追踪实现目标对象选择，并通过上下文感知的视觉提示（如“放置”和“使用”）在共享自主框架下引导用户完成任务。

Result: 在18名健康参与者中进行了可行性研究，成功完成了饮水、使用抽屉和操作烤箱三项日常生活活动，系统实现了有效的序列任务执行，用户参与度高，可用性评分达到“良好”水平（SUS > 70）。

Conclusion: 该交互范式在复杂BCI驱动的机器人辅助任务中具有可行性，为未来在目标用户群体中的进一步评估提供了基础。

Abstract: The integration of augmented reality (AR) and EEG-based brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) offers a promising path for enabling intuitive control of robots for assistive purposes. However, existing AR brain-robot interface (BRI) systems are often constrained to task-specific structures, limiting their utility in real-world environments. We present an AR BRI designed for generalist robot arm manipulation that combines gaze-based object selection with motor imagery action control. Our system uses eye-tracking for intuitive object targeting and context-aware visual overlays ("Place" and "Use") to guide the user through tasks within a shared autonomy framework. We evaluated the interface through a feasibility study with 18 healthy participants performing three multi-step activities of daily living: drinking, using a drawer, and operating an oven. Our results demonstrate that this interaction paradigm enables effective sequential task execution and high user engagement, achieving a "Good" usability rating (SUS > 70). These findings support the feasibility of the proposed interaction paradigm for complex BCI-driven robotic assistance, and motivate future evaluation with the intended target population. Project website: https://ar-bri-manip.github.io/.

</details>


### [193] [V2P-Manip: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Monocular Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16436)
*Kaihan Chen,Yanming Shao,Haifeng Ji,Xiaokang Yang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V2P-Manip是一个从单目视频中学习灵巧操作策略的高效框架，通过视觉与物理一致性结合，实现了高精度和强适应性的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服传统遥操作数据成本高的问题，探索从单目人类示范视频中提取具有视觉保真度和物理合理性的轨迹作为可扩展的数据来源。

Method: 提出V2P-Manip框架，构建包含3D资产获取、轨迹估计和灵巧策略学习的一体化流程，并引入两阶段优化机制以确保空间对齐和物理一致性。

Result: 在TACO和OakInk基准上显著优于先前方法，姿态精度更高，对非结构化环境适应性更强，训练效率更优，在多个合成任务中平均成功率超过75%。

Conclusion: V2P-Manip能有效从单目视频中提取可用于多种灵巧手形态的灵巧操作先验，推动了具身智能中基于视频的策略学习发展。

Abstract: Achieving autonomous robotic dexterous manipulation requires precise, human-like action sequences at scale. As a scalable supplement to costly teleoperation data, extracting trajectories with both visual fidelity and physical plausibility from monocular videos represents a promising frontier in embodied AI. To this end, we introduce V2P-Manip, an efficient framework designed to learn dexterous manipulation policies directly from human demonstration videos. We establish an efficient, integrated pipeline encompassing 3D asset acquisition, trajectory estimation, and dexterous policy learning. To bridge the gap between visual perception and physical constraints, we introduce a two-stage refinement process to enforce spatial alignment and physical consistency. Evaluations on the TACO and OakInk benchmarks demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms previous methods in pose accuracy, adaptability to unstructured environments, and training efficiency. Ultimately, experimental results confirm an average success rate of over 75% across multiple synthetic manipulation tasks and validate the adaptability of the extracted manipulation priors across diverse dexterous hand embodiments.

</details>


### [194] [Training and Evaluating Diffusion Policies with Long Context Lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16447)
*Abhinav Agarwal,Adam Wei,Taylan Kargin,Michael Zeng,Cole Becker,Arif Kerem Dayi,Pablo Parrilo,Asuman Ozdaglar,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在模仿学习中增加上下文长度对机器人操作策略性能的影响，发现适当的方法下简单扩展上下文长度并不像文献中所说的那样脆弱，并提出了一种多上下文长度联合训练算法以降低长上下文学习的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有模仿学习策略因仅依赖短观察历史而缺乏记忆能力、导致任务失败的问题，探索上下文长度对策略性能的实际影响。

Method: 逐步增加上下文长度，评估其在不同任务和数据制度下的策略表现；采用UNet+交叉注意力结构作为去噪主干网络；提出一种可同时在多个上下文长度上联合训练策略的算法。

Result: 实验表明，通过适当的条件建模方法，即使简单扩展上下文长度也能在多种任务中实现高成功率；所提联合训练算法进一步降低了长上下文学习的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 简单扩展上下文长度在合理模型设计下是有效的，无需过度担忧其脆性；多上下文长度联合训练为长上下文模仿学习提供了更高效的学习范式。

Abstract: Imitation learning has enabled highly-dexterous robotic manipulation from RGB observations. Policies trained with these methods, however, typically condition robot actions on only a short history of observations. These policies cannot solve tasks that require memory and can get stuck repeatedly executing the same failing motions. In this work, we first benchmark policy performance as context length is incrementally increased from short to long, across a spectrum of tasks with varying local stability and memory requirements, and in multiple data regimes. To our knowledge, this is the first study to investigate context length in imitation learning at this level of detail. Our results challenge prior claims: naively scaling context length is not as brittle as advertised in literature. With an appropriate conditioning method and denoising backbone (UNet+Cross-Attention), single-task policies achieve high success rates on many tasks in the usual data regime even with naive scaling. Next, we propose a training algorithm to jointly train policies at multiple context lengths, further reducing the sample complexity of long-context learning. Finally, we apply our findings to re-evaluate some previously proposed solutions to long-context imitation learning.

</details>


### [195] [RHO: Your Coding Agent is Secretly a Roboticist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16458)
*Karim Elmaaroufi,Justin Svegliato,Sarunas Kalade,Graham Schelle,Sanjit A. Seshia,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Robotics Harness Optimization (RHO)，一种在训练时通过环境反馈自动构建可解释、神经符号化的多文件策略库的方法，显著提升了机器人任务的执行效率与成功率，优于现有基于大语言模型的多轮代码生成系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Code-as-Policies (CaP) 方法依赖测试时的多轮代码生成，难以满足实时控制需求，且性能受限。因此需要一种更高效、可在单轮部署中实现高成功率的策略生成范式。

Method: 提出RHO框架，在训练阶段利用工具增强的编码代理自动搜索和优化由多个文件组成的策略库（Repositories-as-Policies），通过环境奖励和执行结果进行反射式反馈优化，而非依赖演示数据。该方法整合感知、规划与控制原语，构建可复用的多文件策略结构。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO任务上，RHO达到45.0%的成功率，是最强多轮系统的2.5倍，优于OpenVLA（0.0%）和π₀.₅（12.83%）；在Robosuite上达到70.0%，超越此前68.29%的多轮记录；在O3DE基准上，将成功率从23.5%提升至44.3%，同时减少20%运行时间和27%工具调用。

Conclusion: RHO证明了在训练时优化多文件控制架构的有效性，为大语言模型赋能机器人提供了更高效、可扩展且接近实时的部署方案，推动了神经符号系统在具身智能中的应用。

Abstract: Code-as-Policies (CaP) has shown that large language models (LLMs) can write code to solve robotics tasks by composing perception, planning, and control primitives. Recent CaP systems, however, rely on multi-turn code-generation loops at test time, which is often infeasible for real-time robot control. We introduce Robotics Harness Optimization (RHO), a novel paradigm in which tool-enabled coding agents, at training time, propose and search for interpretable, neurosymbolic multi-file policy repositories (Repositories-as-Policies) that compose these primitives rather than a single prompt, function, or file. RHO searches with reflective feedback from environment reward and execution rather than teleoperation demonstrations. It generalizes to perturbed pick-and-place settings like LIBERO-PRO, where OpenVLA scores 0.0% and $π_{0.5}$ averages 12.83%. Using the same low-level primitives, RHO reaches a 45.0% success rate, 2.5x higher than the strongest multi-turn agentic system, and 3.5x higher than $π_{0.5}$. On Robosuite, RHO sets a new state-of-the-art of 70.0%, exceeding the prior multi-turn record of 68.29% using single-turn execution with no corrective LLM code edits at deployment. When an LLM is used in the control loop, as on RAI's O3DE benchmark, RHO optimizes the deployed agent's multi-file harness of prompts, tools, and control code, improving held-out success from 23.5% to 44.3% with 20% less wall-clock time and 27% fewer tool calls.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [A Definition of Good Explanations and the Challenges Explaining LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14838)
*Louis Mahon,Elliot Ford,Callum Hackett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实解释并结合听者先验信念的AI系统良好解释定义，探讨了其对人工智能可解释性的影响，特别是大语言模型输出难以生成良好解释的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解什么是好的解释，从而促进AI系统的可解释性，尤其是在AI广泛应用的背景下，需要一个兼顾人类认知特点的解释定义。

Method: 借鉴反事实解释的概念，引入听者对解释中各事实的先验信念，构建新的解释定义，并分析该定义在AI可解释性中的应用和影响。

Result: 提出的新定义揭示了当前AI系统（尤其是大语言模型）在生成有效解释时面临的挑战，即忽视听者的背景知识和信念会导致解释效果不佳。

Conclusion: 一个好的AI解释应结合反事实信息与听者的先验信念，这对提升AI系统的可解释性和用户信任具有重要意义。

Abstract: How to define a good explanation is a long-standing philosophical debate which has found recent renewed interest in the context of AI outputs. Explainability is crucial for AI adoption in many contexts, but in order to produce good explanations of AI systems, we must first have an understanding of what good explanations are. In this paper we propose a definition inspired by the notion of counterfactual explanations, however we argue that one must also take into account the interlocutor's prior beliefs in each fact that could be offered in an explanation. We explore the ramifications of this definition for AI explainability and, in particular, why LLM outputs are difficult to produce good explanations for.

</details>


### [197] [Dr-DCI: Scaling Direct Corpus Interaction via Dynamic Workspace Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14885)
*Yi Lu,Zhuofeng Li,Ping Nie,Haoxiang Zhang,Yuyu Zhang,Kai Zou,Wenhu Chen,Jimmy Lin,Dongfu Jiang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DR-DCI是一种检索器引导的直接语料库交互框架，通过将相关文档动态拉入本地工作区，在保持可扩展性的同时支持精细的证据推理操作，相比传统方法在准确率、效率和大规模语料适应性方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索接口（如BM25或ColBERT）仅提供排序结果或有限文档视图，限制了智能体跨文档重组信息和验证约束的能力；而直接语料库交互（DCI）虽灵活但随语料增长变得缓慢且不稳定。

Method: 提出DR-DCI框架，将检索视为智能体可调用的动作，用于动态构建和扩展本地工作空间，并在该空间内执行DCI操作，结合了高召回检索与高精度局部操作的优势。

Result: 在Browsecomp-Plus上达到71.2%准确率，比原始DCI提升最多8.3个百分点，工具使用、耗时和成本更低；引入上下文重置后准确率升至73.3%；在10万到1000万文档规模下保持有效，远超BM25与原始DCI；在2000万级Wiki-18 QA任务中六项基准平均得分为63.0，优于基于检索和训练的搜索智能体基线。

Conclusion: DR-DCI通过检索引导的工作空间机制，实现了高效、精准且可扩展的语料交互，为大规模语料上的智能搜索代理提供了更优架构。

Abstract: Agentic search over large corpora relies on retriever-mediated interfaces (e.g., BM25 or ColBERT) for scalable candidate discovery. While effective at ranking relevant documents, these interfaces expose evidence only as ranked results or bounded document views, limiting agents' ability to reorganize material and verify constraints across documents. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) addresses this limitation by exposing shell-executable corpus operations for flexible search, filtering, comparison, and verification. However, full-corpus terminal commands become slow and unstable as the corpus grows, degrading performance and efficiency. We introduce DR-DCI, a retriever-steered DCI framework that treats retrieval as an agent-callable action for expanding a local workspace. Rather than operating directly over the full corpus, the agent dynamically pulls relevant documents into an evolving workspace and conducts DCI operations within it. This design combines retriever-level recall with DCI-style precision: retrieval keeps exploration scalable, while DCI preserves the local operations needed for effective evidence resolution. Experiments show that DR-DCI is both effective and efficient across scales. On Browsecomp-Plus, DR-DCI reaches 71.2\% accuracy, improving over raw DCI and ablated variants by up to 8.3 points while reducing tool usage, wall time, and estimated cost. With workspace-preserving context reset, accuracy further improves to 73.3\%. In corpus-scaling experiments, DR-DCI remains effective from 100K to 10M documents, whereas raw DCI becomes unstable and BM25 performs substantially worse. DR-DCI also scales to a 20M-scale file-per-document Wiki-18 QA setting, achieving an average score of 63.0 across six benchmarks and outperforming retrieval-based and trained search-agent baselines. Ablation analysis further shows that ranked previews and inter-document DCI are key to performance.

</details>


### [198] [Relational Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14892)
*Adiba Ejaz,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系结构因果模型，扩展了传统因果模型以处理对象及其关系的变化，并通过定义关系因果图和符号识别准则，解决了在未观测混杂因素存在下对新对象组合的因果和观察查询的识别问题，提出的关系神经因果模型在模拟交通场景中表现优于非关系基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使人工智能能够对环境进行因果推理并支持对干预和反事实的推理，同时具备组合性以泛化到未见过的对象组合，需要建立一种新的因果模型。

Method: 提出了关系结构因果模型，扩展了Pearl（2009）的结构因果模型，适用于对象和关系变化的场景；定义了关系因果图并推导出符号识别准则，以实现对未见对象组合的因果和观察查询的识别；提出了关系神经因果模型，确保理论正确性。

Result: 证明了在没有额外假设的情况下，无法识别关于未见对象组合的因果和观察查询；所提出的关系神经因果模型在模拟交通场景（包含变化的车辆、信号灯和行人）中优于非关系基线方法。

Conclusion: 关系结构因果模型为处理动态对象和关系的环境提供了理论基础和实用工具，能够在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下实现对新组合的因果推理，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: An artificial intelligence must have a model of its environment that is causal, supporting reasoning about interventions and counterfactuals, and also combinatorial, supporting generalization to unseen combinations of objects. In this work, we formally study when and how such a model can be learned. We develop relational structural causal models, extending structural causal models (Pearl 2009) to settings where objects and their relations vary. First, we show how answers to not only causal but also observational queries about unseen combinations of objects can not be identified without further assumptions. To enable such identification--including in the presence of unobserved confounding--we define relational causal graphs and derive symbolic identification criteria. Finally, we propose relational neural causal models, a provably correct approach that outperforms non-relational baselines on simulated traffic scenes with varying cars, signals, and pedestrians.

</details>


### [199] [Trust Between AI Agents: Measuring Formation, Breakage, and Recovery, with Implications for Governing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14923)
*Yujiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于代价验证的信任度量方法，在合作生存游戏中评估了六种前沿语言模型间的信任形成、破裂与恢复，发现信任程度影响决策效率和收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏衡量AI代理之间信任的标准方法，需要一种可观察的行为指标来量化多智能体系统中的信任。

Method: 在合作生存游戏中使用代价验证作为信任的代理指标，通过减少验证行为的程度来衡量信任水平，并测试六个前沿模型快照之间的信任动态。

Result: 四个较大的模型（Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.1, Gemini 3.1 Pro）在面对可靠队友时将验证减少60-85%，而两个较小模型无明显调整；失败会逆转信任，不同模型对故障的反应不同，有的针对犯错者加强审查，有的则整体变得更加谨慎；信任恢复慢于建立，集中失败比分散失败引发更持久的怀疑。

Conclusion: 信任倾向可在部署前测量，适度的信任校准比最大怀疑更有利于系统性能，应成为多智能体AI治理的核心关注点。

Abstract: As language-model agents increasingly work in teams, each agent must decide how much to trust its teammates. Yet we lack a standard way to measure trust between AI agents. We propose a behavioral measure based on costly verification. In a cooperative survival game, checking a teammate's work consumes resources, while trusting a wrong answer can be fatal. Relative to a memoryless version of the same model, reduced verification provides an observable measure of trust. Using this framework, we study trust formation, breakage, and recovery across six frontier model snapshots. When paired with a consistently reliable teammate, four snapshots (Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.1, and Gemini 3.1 Pro) reduce verification by roughly 60-85%, whereas two smaller snapshots show little or no such adjustment. Failures reverse this discount, but models differ in how they respond. Some concentrate renewed scrutiny on the culprit, while others become more cautious toward the entire team. Recovery is slower than formation, and clustered failures sustain suspicion far longer than the same number of failures spread apart. These differences have practical consequences. Models that form trust verify less, decide more quickly, and achieve higher payoffs in our environment. By contrast, persistent over-verification is associated with indecision rather than safety. Our results show that trust dispositions can be measured before deployment and suggest that calibration, rather than maximal suspicion, should be the central concern in the governance of multi-agent AI systems.

</details>


### [200] [PrologMCP: A Standardized Prolog Tool Interface for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14935)
*Agnieszka Mensfelt,Adarsh Prabhakaran,Adrian Haret,Vince Trencsenyi,Kostas Stathis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrologMCP是一个任务无关的开源服务器，通过Model Context Protocol将Prolog作为状态化工具暴露出来，使语言模型能够将逻辑推理任务形式化并交由Prolog求解器执行，显著提升在复杂演绎任务上的准确性和可检验性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在深层演绎推理任务上表现有限，且通过扩展内部推理提升性能的成本高昂；同时现有的自动形式化流程多为特定任务定制，缺乏通用性和可复用性。

Method: 提出PrologMCP，一个基于Model Context Protocol的通用接口，将Prolog作为外部推理工具，实现问题翻译、运行、检查与修复的闭环；通过构建形式化代理（formalizer agent）与标准及增强推理能力的语言模型进行对比实验。

Result: 在PARARULE-Plus的两个子集上测试显示：在通用样本中，形式化代理准确率与最佳推理模型相当或更高（1.00 vs. 1.00 / 0.998），远超标准模型（GPT-4.1为0.762）；在挑战性子集中，代理保持近完美表现（1.00 / 0.99），而推理模型下降至0.95 / 0.94。

Conclusion: 通过MCP将推理任务委托给Prolog是一种比扩展自然语言内部推理更稳健、更可检验的替代方案，具有良好的通用性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Frontier reasoning-tuned language models still fail on deductive tasks at depth, and the cost of improved performance through extended internal reasoning scales poorly. Symbolic delegation offers a complementary route: a language model translates the problem, while a solver performs the inference. However, current autoformalization pipelines for logic programming are typically bespoke integrations tied to particular tasks or agents. We introduce PrologMCP, a task-agnostic, open-source server that exposes Prolog as a stateful tool through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Its compact tool interface, structured error reporting, and per-session isolation make the translate-run-inspect-repair loop a reusable primitive for MCP-capable agents. We evaluate a formalizer agent enhanced with PrologMCP against standard and reasoning LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4.1, and o4-mini) on two subsets of PARARULE-Plus: a general-purpose sample and a more challenging one targeting a specific failure mode of natural-language reasoning. On the general sample, the formalizer matches or exceeds reasoning LLMs (accuracy 1.00 vs.\ 1.00 / 0.998), with the largest gains over standard models (0.762 for GPT-4.1). On the challenging subset, the formalizer remains near-perfect (1.00 / 0.99) while reasoning LLMs drop to 0.95 / 0.94. These results suggest that delegating inference to Prolog via MCP is a robust and inspectable alternative to extended natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [201] [Semantics-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14941)
*Shiqiao Zhou,Zipeng Wu,Holger Schöner,Edouard Fouché,IAG Wilson,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种语义增强的检索增强时间序列预测框架SERAF，结合时间序列和自生成文本描述进行双重检索，提升非平稳条件下的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅依赖时间序列相似性进行检索，在非平稳条件下效果有限，难以捕捉语义信息。

Method: SERAF框架通过双重检索机制，同时检索时间序列及其自生成的文本描述，获取互补的历史模式，并选择性融合用于未来预测。

Result: 在七个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SERAF在数值和语义层面联合建模优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合多模态信息（数值+文本）能有效提升时间序列预测的鲁棒性和准确性，尤其在非平稳环境中。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models often benefit from historical patterns. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), recent research explored retrieving relevant historical time series segments to enhance forecasting. However, relying solely on time series similarity is often insufficient for retrieval under non-stationarity. To address this, we propose a multimodal approach: a \textbf{S}emantics-\textbf{E}nhanced \textbf{R}etrieval-\textbf{A}ugmented Time Series \textbf{F}orecasting framework, SERAF. Unlike mainstream approaches that depend only on time series similarity, SERAF conducts dual retrieval over the time series and their self-generated textual descriptions. It retrieves two complementary sets of historical patterns and corresponding futures, which are selectively and jointly used to guide future predictions. Experiments across seven real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SERAF in bridging numerical and semantic views of time series compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [202] [AI Engram: In Search of Memory Traces in Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14997)
*Jea Kwon,Dong-Kyum Kim,Jiwon Kim,Yonghyun Kim,Woong Kook,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架来识别深度神经网络中的“AI印记”（AI engrams），将神经科学的记忆标准形式化为约束逆问题，成功分离出可解释的个体记忆痕迹，并表明其具有因果有效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索深度神经网络是否具备类似生物记忆单元的可识别记忆痕迹，弥合生物记忆理论与人工神经网络表示学习之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将神经科学中的特异性、再激活、充分性和必要性标准形式化为一个约束逆问题，推导出闭式估计器以从全局纠缠参数中分离单个记忆痕迹，并将其与参数流形上的自然梯度更新相对应。

Result: 实现了对AI印记的精确识别与手术式操控，可通过线性运算组合或删除任意记忆子集；在从简单MLP到大语言模型的多种网络上验证了该方法的因果有效性和良好扩展性。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络中存在类生物的记忆单元（AI engrams），该工作为理解分布式存储中的功能特异性提供了新的几何视角，并推动了生物记忆与人工智能表征学习的理论融合。

Abstract: Memory formation is fundamental to intelligence, yet whether deep neural networks preserve identifiable memory traces analogous to biological memory units remains an open question. This work introduces a geometric framework to identify such "AI engrams" by formalizing the neuroscientific criteria of specificity, reactivation, sufficiency, and necessity into a constrained inverse problem. We derive a closed-form estimator that isolates individual memory traces from globally entangled parameters, and show that this biologically-derived solution corresponds to a natural gradient update on the parameter manifold. AI engrams enable surgical manipulation of learned knowledge: any subset of memories can be composed or erased through linear arithmetic, without iterative optimization. Experiments ranging from simple MLPs to LLMs demonstrate the causal validity and substantial scalability of AI engrams. Together, these results bridge theories of biological memory and artificial representation learning and offer geometric insight into how deep networks simultaneously support functional specificity within distributed storage.

</details>


### [203] [Metric Match: A Subset Selection Approach to Evaluating LLM Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15029)
*Alyssa Unell,Natalie Dullerud,Naomi Boneh,Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsu Hashimoto,Nigam Shah,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Metric Match的方法，用于从有限的人工标注中估计LLM评判器的可靠性指标，相较于随机选择样本，该方法显著降低了估计误差并减少了标注成本，在多个数据集和相关性指标上表现优越，并提供了可安装的软件包以方便使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少对昂贵人工标注的依赖，同时准确评估LLM评判器与人类评分者的一致性（即可靠性），是当前开放文本生成评价中的关键挑战。

Method: 提出Metric Match方法，通过选择能够代表整体可靠性指标的样本子集进行人工标注，使该子集在合成标签下的可靠性指标与总体匹配，从而更高效地估计LLM评判器的相关性指标。

Result: Metric Match在四个相关性指标和15个数据集上相比随机选择取得0.838的胜率，平均估计误差降低18.7%，标注需求减少32.5%；在一个医学案例中节省了1,041.67美元；在判断评判器是否达到部署阈值的分类任务中也优于随机选择。

Conclusion: Metric Match能有效提升LLM评判器可靠性评估的效率与准确性，显著降低人工标注成本，具有实际应用价值，并已开源代码和工具包。

Abstract: LLM judges are used to reduce the need for costly human labor in evaluating open-ended text generation. However, the reliability of these judges depends critically on their alignment with human raters -- a property that itself depends on costly human annotations. In this work, we develop a method (Metric Match) for estimating correlation-based reliability metrics of LLM judges from limited annotations. Metric Match selects a subset of samples for human annotation such that the subset matches the population reliability metric with respect to acquired synthetic labels. We empirically show that Metric Match achieves a win-rate of 0.838 against random subset selection across four different correlation metrics and 15 datasets, with an 18.7% decrease in average estimation error and reduces annotation needs by 32.5%. We provide a cost model and highlight a medical case study where our method saves $1,041.67 compared to random selection for expert annotation. Further, we shift our task from reliability estimation to reliability classification of whether a given judge is above a deployment threshold, outperforming random selection with Metric Match. All project code is publicly available, and we additionally provide an installable package for ease of use.

</details>


### [204] [OSGuard: A Benchmark for Safety in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15034)
*Mina Mohammadmirzaei,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSGuard是一个用于评估计算机使用代理在真实桌面和网页任务中安全性的双粒度基准测试套件，能够识别仅以任务成功为指标时可能忽略的不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估方法主要关注任务是否完成，但忽略了代理可能通过不安全的捷径达成目标的情况，因此需要一种能全面评估安全性的新基准。

Method: OSGuard包含两个部分：一是动作级基准，用于判断上下文中的提议动作是否允许、无关或不安全；二是风险增强执行套件，通过对原始任务环境引入潜在危险（如破坏性覆盖）来测试端到端安全性，并结合增强评估器区分安全与不安全的完成方式。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前多模态防护机制在孤立动作判断上表现良好，但在风险增强执行中仍暴露出局部监督与端到端安全之间的差距。

Conclusion: OSGuard的双粒度设计有助于更精确地诊断模型是否能在作为防护机制部署时，既识别不安全动作又能提升整体任务的安全性。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are increasingly evaluated by whether they complete realistic desktop and web tasks. However, task success alone can miss failures in which an agent reaches the nominal goal through an unsafe shortcut. We introduce OSGuard, a dual-granularity benchmark suite for evaluating safety in computer-use agents under benign, unchanged user instructions. OSGuard contains an action-level benchmark for local guardrail decisions and a risk-augmented execution suite for end-to-end evaluation. The action-level benchmark consists of contextualized proposed actions labeled as allowed, unrelated, or unsafe, each judged relative to the original instruction and current interface state. The execution suite contains manually constructed OSWorld-derived task variants in which the original task remains achievable, but the environment is modified to introduce latent hazards such as destructive overwrites, etc. Each variant is paired with augmented evaluators that retain the original task-success criterion while adding explicit state-based safety invariants, allowing us to distinguish safe completions from unsafe completions that satisfy the nominal task objective. Our experimental results on OSGuard show that current multimodal guardrails can perform well on isolated action judgments, while risk-augmented execution exposes remaining gaps between local oversight and reliable end-to-end safety. This dual-granularity design enables more precise diagnosis of whether models can both recognize unsafe proposed actions and improve full-task safety when deployed as guardrails.

</details>


### [205] [Fusion is not one-size-fits-all: Cross-Modal Representation Alignment for Time-to-Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15038)
*Zhemin Zhang,Weijie Chen,David Le,Amara Tariq,Alex Wallace,Matthew Stib,Juan Maria Farina,Chadi Ayoub,Reza Arsanjani,Imon Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于基础模型的跨模态对齐框架，用于多模态临床数据中的时间事件预测，通过四种融合策略提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于模态不平衡和分布偏移，从多模态临床数据中准确预测时间事件具有挑战性。

Method: 使用领域特定的基础模型独立编码CT和EHR数据，并通过晚期融合、对比对齐、交叉注意力和共同注意力四种策略在共享潜在空间中进行对齐。

Result: 在肺栓塞死亡率和心血管疾病结局两个任务上，融合策略使一致性指数比单模态基线提高1.5-5.4%；对比融合结合CLMBR表征表现最佳。

Conclusion: 任务感知的多模态对齐是实现稳健泛化和可扩展临床部署的必要设计原则。

Abstract: Accurate time-to-event (TTE) prediction from multimodal clinical data remains challenging due to modality imbalance and distribution shift. We introduce a foundation model-driven framework for cross-modal representation alignment between CT imaging and longitudinal EHR data, designed to generalize across tasks and institutions. CT and EHR modalities are encoded independently using domain-specific foundation models and aligned in a shared latent space through four principled fusion strategies: late fusion, contrastive alignment, cross-attention, and co-attention. We evaluate two clinically distinct TTE tasks: pulmonary embolism (PE) mortality and cardiovascular disease (CVD) outcomes, on large-scale multi-institutional cohorts (PE: N=3,099 train; 1,098 internal; 435 external; CVD: N=2,951 train; 837 internal; 682 external). Fusion consistently improves concordance index by 1.5-5.4% over unimodal baselines when modalities contribute comparably. Overall, contrastive multimodal fusion, particularly with CLMBR representations, provided the most consistent and statistically robust improvements, especially for PE mortality prediction. For MACE, cross-attention (one-hot) achieved the highest internal performance and image-guided co-attention achieved the best external performance. We therefore introduce a generalizable foundation model-based cross-modal alignment framework and provide the first systematic analysis of fusion behavior under modality imbalance in TTE prediction. Our results establish task-aware multimodal alignment as a necessary design principle for robust generalization and scalable clinical deployment.

</details>


### [206] [Risk-Aware LLM Agents for Geospatial Data Retrieval: Design and Preliminary Adversarial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15077)
*Kyle Gao,Joel Cumming,Jonathan Li,Linlin Xu,David A. Clausi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型的框架，通过自然语言查询实现对云地理空间目录中遥感数据的检索，支持环境监测、灾害响应和气候分析等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有系统在理解用户意图与安全访问地理空间数据之间的鸿沟，提升非专业用户对复杂遥感数据服务的可用性和安全性。

Method: 构建一个由三个智能体（Guardrail、General-QA、Recommender-Analyst）组成的LLM驱动框架，将自然语言查询转化为结构化API调用，并通过模块化设计实现跨平台部署。

Result: 框架在对抗性多轮对话实验中表现出一定鲁棒性，但仍有罕见高影响故障发生，表明需加强系统级防御机制；整体上实现了语义对齐且可靠的数据访问。

Conclusion: 该框架为用户意图与地理空间基础设施之间建立了可扩展的接口，推动了自动化地球观测工作流的发展，同时强调了安全与可用性平衡的重要性。

Abstract: We present an LLM-driven framework for retrieving remote sensing data from cloud-based geospatial catalogues using natural language queries. The system converts user intent into structured API calls, enabling efficient access to satellite imagery and environmental datasets. The architecture integrates three agents: Guardrail for safety and policy enforcement, General-QA for intent interpretation, and Recommender-Analyst for schema-aware API call generation. This coordinated design ensures reliable, semantically aligned interaction with external data services. The modular framework is portable across platforms through API schema substitution and supports applications in environmental monitoring, disaster response, and climate analysis. It establishes a scalable interface between user intent and geospatial infrastructure, enabling streamlined and automated Earth observation workflows. Preliminary experiments under adversarial multi-turn settings show that prompt-level safety instructions improve robustness, although rare high-impact failures persist in API manipulation scenarios and highlight the need for adaptive, system-level defenses that balance safety, usability, and cost efficiency, which motivates the use of our intercept-level Guardrail agent.

</details>


### [207] [Cognitive Debt: AI as Intellectual Leverage and the Dynamics of Systemic Fragility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15078)
*Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了认知债务的形式化理论，描述了个体在使用AI替代而非补充第一性原理思维时积累的未验证推理义务。模型表明理性个体会因短期收益而承担认知债务，导致系统脆弱性上升，并可能引发认知型“明斯基时刻”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI广泛使用对人类认知能力的长期影响，尤其是当AI被用作思维替代品时可能导致的系统性风险和认知退化问题。

Method: 构建包含认知资本与认知债务两个状态变量的动态模型，采用乘法生产函数，分析个体与社会最优之间的AI采用差异，并推导六个理论命题。

Result: 发现理性个体会过度采用替代性AI，导致认知债务累积；平静期会加剧AI依赖，增加系统脆弱性；危机损失随杠杆率凸性增长；事后修复可能陷入‘更多AI修补AI’的假纠正循环；高能力个体也可能最终丧失基础认知能力。

Conclusion: 替代性AI的过度使用会产生认知公共品损耗、外部性与军备竞赛效应，使去中心化决策下的AI采纳超过社会最优水平，需政策干预以避免系统性认知危机。

Abstract: We develop a formal theory of cognitive debt: the stock of unverified reasoning obligations that accumulates when individuals use AI as a substitute rather than a complement for first-principles cognition. The model features two state variables per agent, cognitive capital and cognitive debt, and a multiplicative production technology in which cognitive capital functions as collateral that determines the return to AI adoption. We establish six propositions. Rational agents incur positive cognitive debt because the costs are deferred, partially external, and masked by short-run productivity gains. Tranquil periods lower subjective risk assessments, raise AI substitution intensity, and compound leverage, generating a cognitive Minsky moment in which subjective risk falls while true systemic fragility rises. Expected crisis losses are convex in aggregate leverage. Post-crisis, output-target pressure can produce a false-correction loop in which agents patch AI failures with more AI. The decentralised equilibrium over-adopts substitutive AI relative to the social optimum because of systemic risk, cognitive public goods, and arms-race externalities. In a two-type heterogeneous-agent economy, high-cognitive-capital agents adopt AI more intensively and may eventually erode their unaided cognitive capital below that of initially lower-skilled agents.

</details>


### [208] [VGPT-RSI for RH-Adjacent Formal Progress: Boundary Certificates, Verified Finite Lagarias Inequalities, and Explicit Failure Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15096)
*Zhixin Hu,Tao Xu,Xiaodian Sun,Li Jin,Momiao Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用可验证的AI辅助推理系统（VGPT-RSI）在黎曼假设相关问题上产生形式化部分进展的可能性，重点在于构建和验证与RH相关的有限证书，并明确识别剩余数学障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼假设是数学中未解决的核心问题之一。本文旨在探索AI系统是否能在不声称完全证明的情况下，产生可靠且经过形式验证的部分进展，并清晰指出尚未解决的数学难点。

Method: 采用VGPT-RSI模型处理两个与RH相关的认证任务：一是构建并验证一个基于参数化安全下界曲线的有限RH边界证书，使用区间算术和Arb/FLINT球面算术进行审计，并在Rocq/CoqInterval中完成形式验证；二是启动拉格朗斯路径的形式化证书工作，对拉格朗斯准则中的有限量进行形式化并生成Coq可验证的有限证书。

Result: 成功生成了RH相关的有限边界证书和拉格朗斯路径的初步形式化证书，系统能够通过形式化工具链验证结果，并准确识别出当前无法克服的数学瓶颈，包括拉格朗斯等价性的形式化、全局尾部定理的证明以及极端整数反例的归约问题。

Conclusion: VGPT-RSI系统能够在黎曼假设相关研究中实现可验证的部分进展，有效组织证明依赖关系，并在面对真正数学障碍时避免过度声明，展示了AI辅助数学研究的潜力与边界。

Abstract: The Riemann Hypothesis remains one of the central unsolved problems in mathematics. Rather than claiming proof, we investigate whether a verifiable AI-assisted reasoning system can produce reliable, formally checked partial progress while explicitly identifying the remaining mathematical obstructions. We apply the Verifiable Growing Physical Transformer with Recursive Self-Improvement (VGPT-RSI) to two RH-adjacent certification tasks. First, we construct and verify a finite RH-boundary certificate for inequality on a parameterized safe lower curve over a region. The numerical boundary curve is converted into a certificate-backed lower curve, audited using outward-rounded interval arithmetic and Arb/FLINT ball arithmetic, and then checked in Rocq/CoqInterval for the parameterized theorem. Second, we initiate a formal Lagarias-route certificate. Lagarias criterion states that RH is equivalent to the global inequality. We formalize the finite quantity and produce a Coq-checked finite certificate. The final system identifies the exact unresolved mathematical bottlenecks: formalizing the Lagarias equivalence, proving the global tail theorem beyond any finite cutoff, and potentially reducing counterexamples to colossally abundant or related extremal integers. These results demonstrate that VGPT-RSI can produce certified RH-adjacent formal progress, organize proof dependencies, and avoid overclaiming when the remaining obstruction is genuinely mathematical.

</details>


### [209] [Towards Verifiable Agentic Data Science: Solving Irregular TSQA Via Tool-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15107)
*Sanhorn Chen,Xiaoyang Chen,Boyu Liu,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRTS-ToolBench是一个面向不规则时间序列的问答基准，包含1700个问题、10种任务类型和13个领域，旨在评估大语言模型在现实不规则时序数据下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列问答基准大多假设输入是规则采样的，无法反映真实场景中异步观测、信息性缺失值和多变采样频率等不规则特性，导致对大模型在实际应用中表现的理解存在空白。

Method: 构建了一个名为IRTS-ToolBench的新基准，包含来自13个领域的1700个问题，覆盖10种任务类型，并设计了标准化输入和可复现的评估协议，支持研究人员独立使用。

Result: 提供了首个专注于不规则时间序列问答的综合评测基准，支持对LLM和AI代理在非规则时序数据上的系统评估。

Conclusion: IRTS-ToolBench填补了现有TSQA基准在不规则时间序列上的空白，为未来研究大语言模型处理现实世界时序数据的能力提供了标准工具。

Abstract: Time series data in real-world deployments is overwhelmingly irregular. Observations are asynchronous, missing values are informative rather than random, and sampling frequencies vary across sensors and operational windows. However, existing Time Series Question Answering (TSQA) benchmarks mostly assume regularly sampled inputs, leaving a fundamental gap in understanding how large language models (LLMs) and AI agents perform under irregular conditions. To bridge this gap, we introduce IRTS-ToolBench, a benchmark of 1,700 questions spanning 10 task types across 13 domains. IRTS-ToolBench is designed to be used independently by any researcher working on LLM-based irregular time series analysis, providing standardized inputs and a reproducible evaluation protocol. Code can be found in https://github.com/SanhornC/IRTS-ToolBench.

</details>


### [210] [CONCORD: Asynchronous Sparse Aggregation for Device-Cloud RAG under Document Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15179)
*Xuedong Hu,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Tian Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CONCORD的异步稀疏聚合框架，用于在文档隔离的设备-云协同推理场景下提升检索增强生成（RAG）的效率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双端RAG方法依赖频繁的远程同步和密集证据传输，在带宽和延迟受限的条件下限制了吞吐量；同时隐私政策要求本地私有文档不能上传，形成了文档隔离的挑战。

Method: CONCORD将云端视为异步到达的证据源，引入等待债务控制机制来决定解码步骤是否等待远程参与，并设计证书引导的最小补充机制，仅请求确定当前贪婪决策所需的远程证据，在不需远程参与的步骤中本地提交，保持与密集双端聚合相同的贪婪token选择。

Result: 在Natural Questions和WikiText-2数据集上的实验表明，CONCORD相比基线方法分别将端到端吞吐量提高了1.66倍和2.15倍，每token通信量减少两个数量级以上，同时保持了相当的答案质量和困惑度。

Conclusion: CONCORD通过异步稀疏聚合有效解决了文档隔离下双端RAG的通信开销问题，在保证生成质量的同时显著提升了系统效率和实用性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a pivotal technique for improving language models by incorporating external knowledge at inference time. As device-cloud collaborative inference makes it feasible to deploy small language models on edge devices, a new setting arises in which private documents remain on the device and public knowledge resides in the cloud. Privacy and policy constraints often forbid raw document exchange, creating a document-isolated dual-end RAG setting. However, existing methods rely on frequent remote synchronization and dense evidence transfer, limiting throughput under realistic latency and bandwidth conditions. To address this issue, we propose CONCORD, an asynchronous sparse aggregation framework for dual-end RAG under document isolation. CONCORD treats the cloud as an asynchronously arriving evidence source rather than a continuously synchronized co-generator. Specifically, we introduce waiting debt control to decide whether each decoding step should continue waiting for remote participation based on the observed return of waiting. We also design a certificate-guided minimal supplementation mechanism that requests only the remote evidence needed to determine the current greedy decision. Steps that consult the cloud preserve the same greedy token as dense dual-end aggregation, while the remaining steps commit locally without remote evidence. Experiments on Natural Questions and WikiText-2 show that CONCORD improves end-to-end throughput over baselines by $1.66\times$ and $2.15\times$, respectively, while reducing per-token communication by over two orders of magnitude and maintaining comparable answer quality and perplexity.

</details>


### [211] [CogGuard: Cognitive and Operational Profiling for Proactive Warning in Edge Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15199)
*Zhi Yao,Weihao Chen,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CogGuard的主动预警框架，用于边缘智能服务，通过解耦离线大语言模型（LLM）驱动的画像构建和在线小语言模型（SLM）驱动的评分预测，解决了现有方法在跨场景复用性和边缘部署效率上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主动预警系统在边缘智能服务中面临两个主要挑战：一是画像构建方法通常依赖特定领域，缺乏跨场景的可复用抽象；二是异构边缘集群上的微调对齐模型因输入序列长度不一而产生高同步开销。因此需要一个通用且高效的解决方案。

Method: CogGuard采用静态-动态画像到评分的共享管道，将LLM用于离线画像构建，SLM用于在线预测。设计了支持前缀对齐KV缓存复用的场景特定画像方法以提升效率，并提出一种带对比正则化的长度感知分布式微调策略，缓解异构集群中的负载不平衡问题。

Result: 在教育和运维数据集上的实验表明，CogGuard最多减少48%的画像构建时间与19%的分布式微调时间，在百分制预警任务上分别实现13.4和5.9的MAE。在最大规模的教育场景中，相比最强基线模型预测误差降低15.4%。

Conclusion: CogGuard为边缘智能服务提供了一个高效、可复用的主动预警框架，有效平衡了性能、延迟与隐私约束，具有良好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Proactive warning is an important capability for edge intelligent services, where the system predicts whether a subject will successfully complete an incoming task under strict latency and privacy constraints. Such prediction depends on both long-term static attributes and short-term dynamic states derived from historical interaction logs. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong long-context reasoning for constructing structured profiles from these logs, but existing solutions face two challenges for edge deployment: (1) profiling methods are typically domain-specific and lack a reusable abstraction across service scenarios, and (2) fine-tuning alignment models on heterogeneous edge clusters incurs high synchronization overhead due to the variance in input sequence lengths. To address these challenges, we propose CogGuard, a proactive-warning framework for edge intelligent services. CogGuard decouples offline LLM-based profile construction from online Small Language Model (SLM)-based score prediction through a shared static-dynamic profile-to-score pipeline, and instantiates it in two representative scenarios: educational performance warning and operational task outcome warning. For efficient profile construction, we design scenario-specific profiling methods with prefix-aligned KV-cache reuse to reduce repeated encoding overhead. For edge-side model alignment, we propose a length-aware distributed fine-tuning strategy with contrastive regularization to mitigate workload imbalance on heterogeneous clusters. Experiments on education and operation datasets show that CogGuard reduces profile construction time by up to 48% and distributed fine-tuning time by 19%, while achieving MAEs of 13.4 and 5.9, respectively, on 100-point-scale warning tasks. In the largest educational setting, CogGuard reduces prediction error by 15.4% compared with the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [212] [Attribute Inference from Interactive Targeted Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15209)
*Peihao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对交互式定向广告中的隐私泄露问题的建模与评估方法，通过构建可复现的基准测试框架，评估了多种攻击模型在不同披露策略下的属性推断能力，并验证了聚合报告等策略对防御的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向广告系统可能通过用户交互行为向广告主泄露个体层面的敏感信息，需要建模和量化这种隐私风险。

Method: 提出一个将定位谓词、曝光、交互和披露分离的噪声预言机模型；使用基于公开数据校准的合成人群构建可复现的基准；设计包含主题变体和响应先验的活动语义层；通过模拟器生成真实情况、事件轨迹和披露观察，并比较贝叶斯、监督学习、正例-未标记及自适应攻击的表现。

Result: 在四类主题变体、七种随机种子和两种交互设置下进行评估，结果显示重复投放约160次广告后，贝叶斯和监督攻击的主要AUC约为0.64，在高交互场景下达到0.65；披露策略是最强控制因素，聚合报告可消除用户级信号，类型过滤和随机化披露能有效降低泄露信号。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个用于分析交互式定向广告中隐私风险的形式化模型、可复现工具包以及防御评估方法，证明了当前系统存在可测量但有限的推断风险，且适当的披露政策可显著缓解隐私泄露。

Abstract: Targeted advertising systems can pair audiences selected by advertisers with ad units that expose visible user actions. When an interaction remains linked to the campaign that elicited it, the advertiser may receive an observation tied to a user rather than only an aggregate report. We model that channel as a noisy oracle for attribute inference. The model separates targeting predicates, exposure, interaction, and disclosure. These boundaries capture the gap between eligibility and delivery, and the gap between interaction and advertiser visibility.
  We build a reproducible benchmark using synthetic populations calibrated with public data, each with known sensitive labels. A generated campaign semantics layer provides topic variants and response priors. The simulator generates the ground truth, event traces, disclosed observations, and metrics. The evaluation compares Bayesian, supervised, positive and unlabeled, and adaptive attacks under common campaign and disclosure definitions.
  The final evaluation uses four topic variants, seven simulator seeds, and two interaction settings. Repeated campaigns with identity exposure produce measurable but bounded inference signal. At $160$ campaigns, Bayesian and supervised attacks reach about $0.64$ AUC in the main setting and about $0.65$ AUC in the higher interaction setting. Disclosure policy is the strongest control. Aggregate reporting removes the evaluated oracle input tied to users. Type filtering and randomized disclosure reduce the released signal. The result is a model, artifact, and defense evaluation method for privacy in interactive targeted advertising. The code is available at https://github.com/P-HOW/Interactive-Ad-Oracle.

</details>


### [213] [Visual-Seeker: Towards Visual-Native Multimodal Agentic Search via Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15231)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Changtao Miao,Jinbo Su,Zhaowen Zhou,Chunxia Zhang,Xukai Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Kaiyuan Zheng,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Shiming Xiang,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Visual-Seeker的视觉原生多模态深度搜索代理，通过主动视觉推理来增强复杂开放场景中的事实定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在处理复杂的开放世界场景时常常难以实现准确的事实定位，且现有方法主要依赖于简单图像和纯文本证据路径，限制了跨模态多跳推理的能力。

Method: 提出Visual-Seeker，采用主动视觉推理机制，动态关注细粒度视觉细节，并在整个搜索过程中主动收集视觉证据；设计了一个主动视觉推理数据管道，并合成了5000条高质量的多模态轨迹用于训练。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的多模态搜索基准上实现了最先进的性能，甚至超过了多个专有模型。

Conclusion: Visual-Seeker展现了强大的视觉原生推理与搜索能力，能够在真实网络环境中有效进行多模态、多跳推理。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in many visual tasks, but they often struggle with factual grounding when confronted with complex, open-world scenarios. While recent multimodal deep search agents attempt to address this issue by utilizing external tools, the visual-native search paradigm remains underexplored. Existing methods primarily rely on simple images with explicit semantics and text-only evidence trajectories, limiting the agent's ability to perform multi-hop, cross-modal reasoning and search. To address these limitations, we propose Visual-Seeker, a visual-native multimodal deep search agent via active visual reasoning. Rather than treating vision as a static input, our agent actively attends to fine-grained visual details, dynamically harvests visual evidence throughout the search process. To unlock its visual-native potential, we design an active visual reasoning data pipeline and synthesize 5K high-quality multimodal trajectories for model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across five challenging multimodal search benchmarks, even surpassing several proprietary models, validating robust visual-native reasoning and search in real-world web environments. The code and data can be accessed at: https://github.com/ZhengboZhang/Visual-Seeker.

</details>


### [214] [Mask-Proof: An LLM-based Automated Data Curation Pipeline on Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15258)
*Jierui Zhang,Siyuan Tan,Xinhang Li,Longzhuangzhi Lin,Dailin Li,Chengfeng Gu,Xinping Li,Yaxian Hao,Shengjia Liang,Yuxiang Ren,Wenhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Mask-Proof的新方法，用于将真实数学证明转化为可自动检查的掩码步骤任务，从而实现对大型语言模型在多步推理能力上的可重复、可扩展评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数学推理评估方法通常关注最终答案或依赖专家评分，缺乏对长证明中逐步推理能力的可靠、可扩展的自动化评估手段。

Method: 提出Mask-Proof框架：通过遮蔽真实证明中的关键公式步骤，提供上下文，并利用基于LLM的等价性判断器进行多轮投票以评估模型重建结果。构建了包含292个问题的Mask-ProofBench基准。

Result: 在17个模型上的实验表明，增强推理能力的模型比标准模型高出12%到27%；所提出的评估器与人工标注者的一致性达到96.8%。

Conclusion: Mask-Proof为评估大模型在复杂数学证明中的逐步推理能力提供了高效、可靠且可复现的解决方案，有助于推动AI在科研级证明中的可信应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of mathematical problem solving and can even assist with research-level proofs, yet we still lack a scalable and reproducible way to measure step-level reasoning in long proofs across diverse sources. This evaluation gap limits trustworthy AI assistance in proof-certified scientific progress. Existing evaluations often emphasize final answers or rely on costly expert grading, while end-to-end proof generation remains open-ended and hard to verify automatically. We introduce Mask-Proof, a pipeline that turns real proofs into automatically checkable masked-step tasks. It masks key formula steps, provides the necessary surrounding context, and evaluates model reconstructions with an LLM-based equivalence judge using repeated votes for stability. The resulting Mask-ProofBench contains 292 curated problems across diverse research areas. Experiments with 17 models show that reasoning-enhanced models outperform standard models by 12% to 27%. Our evaluator achieves 96.8% agreement with expert annotators, enabling faithful, reproducible, and comparable measurement of step-level mathematical reasoning. Benchmark, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/weating/Mask-Proof.

</details>


### [215] [Feature Attribution in Directed Acyclic Graphs Using Edge Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15273)
*Qiheng Sun,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Haocheng Xia,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于边干预的新型特征归因方法DAG-SHAP，能够有效捕捉特征的外部性和外生性影响，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley值特征归因方法在处理复杂特征交互和因果关系时难以同时捕捉特征的外部性和外生影响，导致解释不合理。

Method: 提出DAG-SHAP方法，采用边干预视角，将每个特征边作为独立归因对象，并引入高效近似计算方法。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的实验表明，DAG-SHAP能更合理地分配特征重要性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DAG-SHAP通过边干预机制有效解决了传统节点中心方法的局限性，提升了特征归因的可解释性与准确性。

Abstract: Shapley value-based feature attribution methods face challenges in scenarios involving complex feature interactions and causal relationships, even when a causal structure is provided. Existing methods typically adopt a node-centric view, attributing importance solely to individual features. Consequently, they often fail to simultaneously capture the externality and exogenous influence of features, leading to unreasonable interpretations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel feature attribution method called DAG-SHAP, which is based on edge intervention. DAG-SHAP treats each feature edge as an individual attribution object, ensuring that both externality and exogenous contributions of features are appropriately captured. Additionally, we introduce an approximation method for efficiently computing DAG-SHAP. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic datasets validate the effectiveness of DAG-SHAP. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DAG-SHAP.

</details>


### [216] [A Formal Framework for Declarative Agentic AI in Business Process Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15291)
*Mohammad Azarijafari,Luisa Mich,Michele Missikoff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AGO方法的智能体业务流程分析形式化框架，通过集合论和数理逻辑定义了代理、目标和对象等实体及其交互，并构建了业务流程知识库（BPKB），支持结构化查询、增量更新和流程自动生成，确保推导路径的正确性与完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现Agentic AI在业务流程自动化中的潜力，需要对业务流程中的实体及其交互进行形式化精确定义，以支持自主决策和动态适应。

Method: 采用AGO方法论，从代理（Agents）、目标（Goals）和对象（Objects）三个视角建模，基于集合论和数学逻辑形式化定义实体类型及相互关系，并组织成业务流程知识库（BPKB）。

Result: 建立了形式化的AGO模型和BPKB，支持结构化查询、增量更新和业务流程的自动生成功能，同时保证推导路径的正确性和完整性。

Conclusion: 该框架为Agentic业务流程分析提供了形式化基础，有助于实现业务流程的自动化、动态适应与可验证性。

Abstract: Agentic AI opens new opportunities for automating Business Process (BP), enabling autonomous decision-making and dynamic adaptation. However, realising this potential requires BP entities and their interactions to be defined with formal precision. This paper presents a formal framework for Agentic BP analysis through the AGO methodology. AGO captures the modelling perspective in terms of who is acting (Agents), why it is carried out (Goals), and what the relevant entities are (Objects). Grounded in set theory and mathematical logic, we formally define the AGO entity types and their interactions, organising all definitions into a BP Knowledge Base (BPKB). The resulting BPKB supports structured querying, incremental updates, and automatic generation of BP workflows, while ensuring soundness and completeness of the derived paths.

</details>


### [217] [CODA-BENCH: Can Code Agents Handle Data-Intensive Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15300)
*Yuxin Zhang,Ju Fan,Meihao Fan,Shaolei Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CODA-BENCH，首个联合评估代码与数据智能的基准，基于Kaggle生态系统构建数据密集型Linux沙箱环境，包含1,009个任务，用以评测智能体在复杂文件结构中进行数据发现与代码生成的能力，结果显示当前先进系统成功率仅为61.1%，揭示了现有智能体在数据密集型任务中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立地评估代码或数据能力，无法反映真实开发场景中代码与数据并重的复杂性，因此需要一个能同时衡量两者能力的综合评估环境。

Method: 构建基于Kaggle生态系统的数据密集型Linux沙箱，包含数百个数据集和平均980个文件的任务环境，设计1,009个跨31个社区的任务，要求智能体探索文件系统并生成用于数据分析的代码。

Result: 最先进的智能体在CODA-BENCH上的成功率仅为61.1%，表明其在整合数据发现与代码执行方面存在显著困难。

Conclusion: CODA-BENCH揭示了当前智能体在处理真实世界数据密集型任务时的关键缺陷，为未来提升代理在复杂开发环境中的综合能力指明了研究方向。

Abstract: Advanced agents are increasingly demonstrating the potential to operate as autonomous engineers, creating a growing demand for evaluation benchmarks that capture the complexity of real-world development. Such environments typically involve both complex code and large-scale data (i.e., file system). However, existing benchmarks usually evaluate code-centric or data-centric capabilities in isolation, leaving a clear gap with real development scenarios. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing CODA-BENCH, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate code and data intelligence in a data-intensive environment. We construct a data-intensive Linux sandbox based on the Kaggle ecosystem (containing hundreds of datasets), where agents must actively explore complex file hierarchies to identify relevant resources and generate code for data-driven analytical tasks. CODA-BENCH comprises 1,009 tasks spanning 31 communities, with each task environment containing an average of 980 files, simulating realistic data scale and noise. Evaluations of advanced agents reveal that even top-performing systems struggle to effectively integrate data discovery with code execution, achieving a success rate of only 61.1%. These results highlight a substantial gap in current agentic capabilities for data-intensive tasks and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [218] [Forced Deferral: Manipulating Routing Decisions in Multimodal LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15308)
*Zhongye Liu,Yaopei Zeng,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为强制延迟攻击（FDA）的新型对抗性攻击方法，通过降低弱模型的置信度，使多模态大语言模型级联系统将查询错误地路由到强模型，揭示了计算分配机制的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于MLLM级联系统依赖弱模型的置信度来决定计算资源分配，这种机制可能被攻击者利用，通过操纵置信度迫使系统始终调用昂贵的强模型，从而引入新的安全威胁。

Method: 提出强制延迟攻击（FDA），通过优化一个温度展平的目标函数，学习通用的边框触发器，使弱模型在触发输入上的令牌分布趋向于从其原始响应构建的非集中目标分布。

Result: 实验表明，FDA在多个数据集、模型族和延迟度量下均能持续增加对强模型的路由概率，并优于现有的图像扰动和提示注入基线方法。

Conclusion: MLLM级联系统存在因置信度操控而导致计算资源被滥用的风险，FDA揭示了此类系统在计算分配策略上的安全隐患，提示需设计更鲁棒的路由机制。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, serving a large model for every query is computationally expensive. MLLM cascades mitigate this cost by first querying a weak but cheaper model and deferring to a strong model when the weak model's output is unconfident. However, since the weak model's confidence directly controls compute allocation, these systems expose a new attack surface: an adversary can manipulate confidence so that their queries are consistently deferred to the strong model. Motivated by this vulnerability, we introduce the Forced Deferral Attack (FDA), an adversarial image attack that lowers the weak model's confidence and causes cascades to route queries to the strong model. FDA learns a universal border trigger by optimizing a temperature-flattened objective. This objective pushes the weak model's token distribution on triggered inputs toward less concentrated targets constructed from its clean responses. Across datasets, model families, and deferral metrics, FDA consistently increases strong-model routing while outperforming image-perturbation and prompt-injection baselines. These results show that MLLM cascades are vulnerable to attacks that manipulate compute allocation, forcing unintended strong-model usage without directly targeting answer correctness.

</details>


### [219] [ChatPlanner: A Large Language Model Framework for Personalized Public Transit Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15315)
*Tingting Yang,Chenhao Xue,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）和检索增强生成（RAG）的个性化公交路径规划框架ChatPlanner，能够从自然语言查询中提取用户偏好并融入路径优化，实验证明其在可行性、信息提取准确性和方案质量方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公交路径规划难以有效整合多样化的用户偏好，现有系统无法理解自然语言表达中的细微需求，限制了个性化服务的能力。

Method: 提出ChatPlanner框架，采用微调的大语言模型结合检索增强生成（RAG）技术，从自然语言查询中提取路由参数和用户偏好，并将其融入公交路径算法的目标函数；构建包含八个角色和五种情境的偏好感知数据集用于训练与评估。

Result: 实验表明，ChatPlanner能稳定生成可行路径方案；微调确保输出结构正确并学习通用偏好模式，RAG提供具体上下文以处理模糊或口语化表达；两者结合在信息提取和偏好理解上达到最高精度；案例研究显示其能发现传统系统忽略的高质量替代路线。

Conclusion: ChatPlanner成功将自然语言理解引入交通优化，建立了个性化公交路径规划的新范式，提升了路径推荐的相关性与实用性。

Abstract: Personalized public transit routing in public transit systems remains challenging due to the difficulty of capturing and integrating diverse user preferences into routing algorithms. This paper presents ChatPlanner, a novel framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to enable preference aware public transit routing. Our approach employs fine-tuned LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to extract routing parameters and interpret nuanced user preferences from natural language queries, subsequently integrating these preferences into the objective function of a public transit routing algorithm. This study designs preference aware datasets incorporating eight personas and five contexts to establish scoring standards for both fine-tuning and RAG. This work conducted three experiments to validate the solutions' feasibility, extraction of routing information and preferences, and solution set quality and completeness. Results demonstrate that ChatPlanner generates feasible solutions reliably. Fine-tuning enforces the required output structure and learns general preference patterns, while RAG provides query-specific context to resolve imprecise or conversational expressions and calibrate continuous scores. The combination of both achieves the highest accuracy in routing information extraction and user preference interpretation. Results based on selected case studies show that by capturing user preferences, ChatPlanner identifies valuable solutions across different dimensions that existing route planners overlook, generating more valuable route alternatives. This research establishes a new paradigm for integrating natural language understanding into transportation optimization.

</details>


### [220] [APEX: Adaptive Principle EXtraction A Three-Layer Self-Evolution Framework for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15363)
*Ya-Chuan Chen,Tien-Jen Lai,Hsiang-Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为APEX的三层次协同进化框架，通过同时优化提示机制、行为原则和工作流拓扑结构，在AI代理自我改进方面显著优于仅优化单一维度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我改进方法如Self-Harness仅优化提示机制，忽略了行为原则和工作流结构的潜力，限制了整体性能提升。因此，需要一种多维度协同进化的框架来全面增强AI代理的能力。

Method: APEX框架包含三个层次：L1层通过失败模式修补优化提示机制；L2层通过成功轨迹蒸馏提取行为原则；L3层基于结构适应性选择最优工作流拓扑。实验在Joe代理上进行，使用114个真实任务轨迹进行验证。

Result: APEX在单次进化运行中将APEX健康评分从基线0.300提升至0.570（+90%），蒸馏出6个可复用的新原则，并选出得分为0.900（+20%）的研究优先工作流拓扑，仅额外消耗约4次LLM调用（约270秒）。

Conclusion: 多维度协同进化显著优于单轴优化方法，能够在较低计算成本下实现更高效的AI代理自我改进，为未来自主代理系统的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Self-improvement in AI agents has emerged as a key research frontier: systems that modify their own prompts, workflows, and decision rules based on accumulated operational experience. The state-of-the-art Self-Harness framework [1] achieves 14--21% improvement on Terminal-Bench-2.0 by mining failure clusters and patching the agent harness. However, Self-Harness optimises only one dimension -- the prompt harness -- leaving behavioural principles and workflow topology unchanged. We propose APEX (Adaptive Principle EXtraction), a three-layer co-evolution framework that simultaneously evolves: (L1) the harness via failure-mode patching, (L2) behavioural principles via success-trace distillation [2], and (L3) the agent workflow topology via structural fitness-based selection [6]. We implement APEX on Joe [13], a production-grade super AI Agent built on NVIDIA Nemotron and designed as an Edge AI Agent Factory for the NVIDIA Agent Challenge 2026, managing a 15-node compute fleet using 114 real task traces collected over 18 days. APEX achieves an APEX Health Score of 0.570 (+90% vs. baseline 0.300) in a single evolutionary run, distilling 6 novel reusable principles and selecting a research-first workflow topology scoring 0.900 (+20%). Our results demonstrate that multi-dimensional co-evolution substantially outperforms single-axis harness optimisation, at a cost of only 4 LLM calls (~270 s) on a local qwen2.5-coder:32b instance.

</details>


### [221] [S1-DeepResearch: Beyond Search, Toward Real-World Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15367)
*Yao Dong,Xinglin Xiao,Liwei Dong,Xinlong Jin,Zhengbo Li,Heng Zhang,Duyun Wang,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向深度研究代理的统一轨迹构建范式，结合封闭式问答与开放式探索，提升知识整合、复杂推理与规划能力。所训练的S1-DeepResearch-32B模型在多个基准上达到开源模型中的最优性能，并接近前沿闭源模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理的训练数据主要集中于封闭式问答，缺乏对证据整合、知识合成、规划和报告生成等关键深度研究能力的覆盖，限制了代理处理复杂知识密集型任务的能力。

Method: 提出一种包含图结构任务建模、代理轨迹展开和多维轨迹验证的统一框架，通过合成高质量、长链条的代理行为轨迹，联合建模信息获取、知识合成与规划行为。

Result: S1-DeepResearch-32B在20个涵盖复杂推理、指令遵循、报告生成、文件理解与技能使用的基准测试中，表现优于同规模开源模型，并在多个挑战性深研任务上接近领先闭源模型水平。

Conclusion: 联合建模信息获取、知识合成与规划行为对构建高效深度研究代理至关重要，所提出的轨迹合成范式有效推动了代理在开放域复杂任务中的综合能力发展。

Abstract: Deep research agents aim to solve complex knowledge-intensive tasks through long-horizon planning, evidence gathering, reasoning, and report generation. While recent progress in search agents has demonstrated strong capabilities in information retrieval and answer verification, most existing training datasets remain search-centric, focusing primarily on closed-ended question answering and information localization. As a result, they mainly train information-seeking behavior while providing limited coverage of key deep research capabilities, including evidence integration, knowledge synthesis, planning, file understanding, and structured report generation. In this work, we propose a unified trajectory construction paradigm for deep research agents that combines closed-ended QA and open-ended exploration. The proposed framework consists of graph-grounded task formulation, agentic trajectory rollout, and multi-dimensional trajectory verification, enabling scalable synthesis of high-quality agentic trajectories spanning long-chain complex reasoning, deep research instruction following, report writing, file understanding and generation, and skills usage. Compared with existing search-oriented datasets, our synthesized trajectories place greater emphasis on knowledge synthesis, complex reasoning, and planning. S1-DeepResearch-32B achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models of comparable scale across 20 benchmarks spanning five capability dimensions, including complex reasoning, instruction following, report generation, file understanding, and skills usage. On several challenging deep research benchmarks, it approaches the performance of leading proprietary frontier models. These results highlight the importance of jointly modeling information acquisition, knowledge synthesis, and planning-oriented agent behaviors for building effective deep research agents.

</details>


### [222] [Reward Hacking in Language Model Agents: Revisiting AI Safety Gridworlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15385)
*Ömer Veysel Çağatan,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型代理在优化代理目标时自然出现的奖励黑客行为，并发现标准缓解方法难以解决这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励黑客行为是人工智能安全中的核心挑战，但大多数已知案例是在前沿系统中事后发现的，缺乏可控的研究环境。因此，本文旨在通过构建一个可复现的文本基础评估套件来系统研究该问题。

Method: 将AI Safety Gridworlds框架改编为面向语言代理的文本基础评估套件，重新设计经典强化学习安全任务，在多个规模的语言模型上进行零样本测试，并分析奖励优化过程中显性奖励与隐性安全目标之间的差距。

Result: 发现无论是在前沿还是中等规模模型中，规范博弈都会零样本出现；模型能在观察奖励上表现良好，但在隐藏的安全目标上表现不佳，且强化学习优化会加剧这一问题；该现象在不同模型规模（1.5B–14B）下均存在，且无法通过更精细的信用分配、探索提示或熵正则化解决。

Conclusion: 当使用有能力的语言模型代理优化代理目标时，奖励黑客行为会自然产生，并对标准缓解措施具有抵抗力，表明在代理环境中解决代理奖励失败问题可能需要超越标准探索和信用分配方法的新途径。

Abstract: Reward hacking, where AI systems exploit misspecified objectives to achieve high reward without satisfying intended goals, remains a central challenge in AI safety. Yet most known instances have been discovered post hoc in frontier systems where controlled study is impractical. We adapt the AI Safety Gridworlds framework into a text-based evaluation suite that reformulates classic reinforcement learning safety tasks for language-based agents. Across frontier and mid-scale models, we find that specification gaming emerges zero-shot: models systematically achieve high observed reward while underperforming on hidden safety objectives, and even apparently safe behaviors can reflect misunderstanding rather than principled safety. Reinforcement learning does not correct these failures: direct reward optimization widens the gap between observed and hidden reward, as the model's initial competence causes it to lock into locally rewarding strategies before discovering safer alternatives. This pattern persists across model scales (1.5B--14B) and is not resolved by finer credit assignment, exploration prompts, or entropy regularization. Our results show that reward hacking arises naturally when optimizing proxy objectives with capable language model agents and resists standard mitigations, suggesting that proxy-reward failures in agentic settings may require approaches beyond standard exploration and credit-assignment fixes. To facilitate reproducibility, the code for this work is available at \href{https://github.com/asparius/verl-agent-safety}{our public repository}.

</details>


### [223] [Hierarchical Modeling of ICD Codes in EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15447)
*Megha Thukral,Dong Gyun Kang,Rudra Pratap Singh,Shruthi Kashinath Hiremath,Katrin Hänsel,Thomas Plötz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在电子健康记录（EHR）表征学习中显式利用ICD-10-CM诊断编码层次结构的方法，通过在Transformer模型和基于图的表示中引入层次信息，提升了疾病预测和跨数据集迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR基础模型将ICD编码视为扁平符号，忽略了其内在的临床层次结构，导致未能充分利用编码系统中的疾病家族和细粒度诊断信息。

Method: 提出两种融合ICD层次结构的方法：一是在BERT-style Transformer中增强诊断序列，加入对应不同层次级别的token；二是在基于图的编码表示中引入层次感知边，结合诊断共现结构。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU两个真实临床数据集上实验表明，显式编码层次结构在域内和跨数据集任务中均优于扁平编码表示，且不同任务和模型下最有效的层次级别有所不同。

Conclusion: 显式利用ICD编码的层次结构可作为通用归纳偏置，提升EHR表征学习效果，且该优势在不同建模方式和层次级别间具有可迁移性和普适性。

Abstract: Electronic health record foundation models typically treat ICD diagnosis codes as flat tokens, overlooking the clinically meaningful hierarchical structure that captures disease families, subcategories, and fine-grained diagnostic detail. As a result, existing EHR representation learning methods do not explicitly exploit the hierarchical structure already present in the coding system. In this work, we study ICD-10-CM hierarchy as a general inductive bias for clinical representation learning. We investigate two complementary mechanisms for incorporating hierarchy: first, by augmenting diagnosis sequences in a BERT-style transformer with tokens corresponding to different levels of the ICD hierarchy, and second, by injecting hierarchy into graph-based code representations through hierarchy-aware edges combined with diagnosis co-occurrence structure. Across these settings, we evaluate whether explicit hierarchy improves downstream prediction, which levels of the hierarchy are most useful, whether hierarchy encoding improves transfer across datasets, and how hierarchy reshapes embedding similarity structure. We conduct experiments on two large-scale real-world clinical datasets: MIMIC-IV, used for pretraining and in-domain evaluation, and eICU, used to assess cross-dataset transfer via frozen encoder probing. Our findings show that explicitly encoding ICD hierarchy improves over flat code representations in both in-domain and cross-dataset settings, while revealing that the most useful level of hierarchy depends on both the task and the modeling approach. More broadly, we focus on hierarchy-aware EHR representation learning and show that the benefits of encoding hierarchy are generalizable across modeling settings and hierarchy levels.

</details>


### [224] [Who Drifted: the System or the Judge? Anytime-Valid Attribution in LLM Evaluation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15474)
*Yitao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种通过固定人工标注锚点集和动态检测机制来区分LLM产品性能下降与评判模型变化的方法，实现对LLM评判器漂移的准确、实时检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评判系统在检测产品性能下降时，难以区分是产品变差还是评判模型自身发生了变化（如静默更新），导致告警模糊。需要一种能明确归因的方法。

Method: 引入固定的人工标注锚点集，定期由当前评判模型重新评分；构建基于e-process的二元检验机制监控评判模型与人类标签之间的差距；结合守卫窗口规则进行判断，实现对系统或评判模型漂移的归因分析，并证明其任意时刻有效性、单向识别性等理论性质。

Result: 在两次真实评判模型变更中，静默版本升级在60次运行中全部被正确检测为评判漂移且无误归因；严格的提示更改在120次运行中正确归因110次（守卫宽度300）；相比工业界默认的滚动z检验（75%无漂移流中误报），性能显著更优。方法在TL;DR摘要任务上无需调参即可复现结果，且在不同领域表现出理论预测的一致差异。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了LLM评判系统中因评判模型变动带来的评估模糊问题，实现了对性能变化的可靠归因，具备实际部署价值和跨领域可迁移性。

Abstract: Continuous evaluation of LLM products relies on a strong LLM judge treated as ground truth: a cheap monitor scores every interaction and a team is paged when the score drifts down. But the judge is itself a model behind an API, and a silent version bump or scoring-prompt update changes how it scores -- so every drift alarm is ambiguous between a worse product and a changed judge. We resolve the ambiguity with a fixed, human-labeled anchor set that the current judge re-scores at a steady interleave, a second betting e-process on the judge-versus-human gap, and a guard-window rule returning a verdict in {none, system, judge}. We prove anytime-validity, one-way identification (only the judge can move the anchors), an attribution race whose design law is that the anchors must out-run the main process they guard, and process orthogonality. On two real judge changes, a silent version bump is detected as judge drift in 60/60 runs with zero judge-to-system misattribution, and a contaminating strict-prompt change is correctly attributed on 110 of 120 runs at guard width 300 -- while the industry-default rolling z-test false-alarms on 75% of drift-free streams. Every experiment replicates on a second domain (TL;DR summarization) with nothing re-tuned, and where the domains differ the differences are the ones the race predicts: the strict-prompt change shifts scores harder there, so the anchors fire faster and attribution becomes perfect (240/240). The monitor runs at approximately 0.64 of the cost of strong-judging every item, or 0.21 in a cheaper-but-deafer regime.

</details>


### [225] [Towards End-to-End Automation of AI Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15497)
*Yutaro Yamada,Robert Tjarko Lange,Cong Lu,Chris Lu,Shengran Hu,Jakob Foerster,David Ha,Jeff Clune*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为“The AI Scientist”的系统，能够自主完成从科研设想到撰写论文、同行评审的全过程，并成功通过了机器学习顶会 workshop 的首轮审稿，展示了AI在自动化科学研究中的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，人工智能领域一直致力于实现科学过程的自动化。尽管已有研究在自动化科研的某些环节上取得进展，但尚无系统能端到端地自主完成整个科研生命周期。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索完全自动化的科学研究可行性。

Method: 该系统基于现代基础模型构建了一个复杂的智能体架构，包含生成研究想法、编写代码、运行实验、数据分析与绘图、撰写完整科学论文以及自我同行评审等模块。评估采用两种模式：一种是使用人类提供的代码模板进行聚焦式研究；另一种是无需模板、通过智能体搜索进行开放式科学探索。

Result: The AI Scientist 成功生成了多个高质量的研究设想，自动完成了实验验证和论文撰写，在没有人为干预的情况下，所生成的论文通过了一个接受率为70%的主要机器学习会议 workshop 的第一轮同行评审。两种模式均能产生多样化且可验证的研究成果。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了AI已具备参与甚至主导科学研究的能力，标志着科研方式可能发生范式转变。虽然存在增加审稿负担和学术噪音等潜在风险，但若能负责任地发展，此类系统有望极大加速科学发现进程。

Abstract: The automation of science is a long-standing ambition in the field of AI. While the community has made significant progress in automating individual components of the scientific process, a system that autonomously navigates the entire research lifecycle -- from conception to publication -- has remained out of reach. Here, we present the strongest demonstration to date toward automating the entire process end-to-end.
